## Heritage Rules
{:#heritage-rules}

Every player character belongs to Haven Ember and serves in the Ember Corps. Heritage describes ancestry, inherited physiology, family techniques, or surviving schools of training; it does not grant citizenship or allegiance to an old Hidden Village.

Choose one Primary Heritage. Mixed ancestry is valid narratively, but only your Primary Heritage grants its starting features, affinities, exclusive abilities, and clan-jutsu access unless a rule explicitly says otherwise.

Heritage Cost is paid from the 6 Unique Points granted at character creation. It is not a Unique, does not count toward Creation Uniques, and is not paid again later.

## Heritage Cost Index
{:#heritage-cost-index}

* 0 UP: Sarutobi, Fūma, Yotsuki, Ouyoku, Karatachi Family, Funato, Iburi, Shiin, Clanless.

* 1 UP: Nara, Yamanaka, Kamizuru, Hokori, Hoshigaki, Silverhand, Samsara, Jinrō, Jashin Worshipper, Insect Glaive Corps, Vanguard Corps

* 2 UP: Aburame, Akimichi, Hyūga, Inuzuka, Senju, Uzumaki, Kazekage Clan / Jiton, Shakuton, Yōton, Hōzuki, Yuki, Futton, Jūgo's Bloodline, Ballistic Corps

* 3 UP: Uchiha, Bakuton, Kaguya, Karashi (Puppets).

# Clanless
{:#clanless}

**HERITAGE \- SELF-MADE**  
*No bloodline taught you how to survive. No inherited technique promised that you would be strong enough. Everything you know was learned from the people beside you and the places that tried to kill you.*

Haven Ember is full of hunters without famous names. Some come from families whose techniques were lost generations ago. Some are refugees, settlers, or children of people who never carried a hunters lineage at all. Others simply chose not to define themselves by whatever blood might run through them.

Clanless hunters do not compete with the great lineages by secretly being better at everything. They survive by learning broadly, changing approach when something fails, and building a toolkit instead of inheriting one answer to every problem. They have no secret bloodline waiting to awaken; they become useful in as many situations as they can.

## Passives
{:#passives}

### No Inheritance
{:#no-inheritance}

While those around you spent their early days inheriting power from their ancient bloodlines, all you inherited was hard work and debt. You start with 100% additional Ryo (100,000 Total), and instead of the Usual 25 Stat Points, you start with 30\. Finally, all abilities with the **Willpower** type cost 50% XP for you, at all stages.

Additionally, you gain an additional Talent point every 1500 XP earned (1500XP, 3000XP, 4500XP…)

### Self-Taught
{:#self-taught}

Clanless hunters are not locked into an inherited curriculum. During each Week's Rest, you may retrain one or more non-Heritage Jutsu or Abilities with a combined XP cost of up to 10\. Remove the chosen options, then immediately spend the same amount of XP on other non-Heritage Jutsu or Abilities you currently meet the requirements for.  
You cannot retrain Talents, Uniques, Attributes, Skills, Heritage options, or anything granted for free. Retraining never grants XP, and any unused portion of this 10 XP allowance is lost when that Week's Rest ends.

### Jack of All Trades
{:#jack-of-all-trades}

Clanless hunters are used to reaching for whichever tool works, rather than waiting for the one technique their family taught them.  
When you make an Attack, Defensive Roll, Skill Check, Status Resistance Roll, Stamina Exhaustion Roll, or Chakra Exhaustion Roll, you may use Adaptability to add \+2 to the roll after rolling but before the result is resolved.  
After using Adaptability, you cannot use it on the same kind of roll as your previous use for the rest of combat. Attack, Defense, Skill, Status Resistance, Stamina Exhaustion, and Chakra Exhaustion are separate kinds of roll. Outside combat, Adaptability may be used once per scene instead.

### Dig Deep
{:#dig-deep}

Once per Battle, when you or an ally within 10 yards would fail an Attack, Defensive Roll, Skill Check, Status Resistance Roll, Stamina Exhaustion Roll, or Chakra Exhaustion Roll, you may advance 1 Fatigue Level to add \+5 to the result. This can turn a failure into a success.

You cannot use Dig Deep if advancing Fatigue would Incapacitate you.

# Fūma
{:#fuma}

***CANON CLAN***

*Fūma descendants brought an old weapon tradition into Haven Ember’s armouries: giant shuriken, chained steel, false trajectories, and attacks designed to make distance feel temporary. In the Ember Corps, the lineage is valued less for raw numbers than for making cover, range, and angles stop behaving the way an enemy expects.*

## Passives
{:#passives-12}

### Fūma Skill
{:#fuma-skill}

The Limit for the Quick Hands ability is DEX/18 instead of DEX/20 for you.

### Fūma Power
{:#fuma-power}

Whenever you would throw a Shuriken of any kind or size, or any shuriken-like object, you may use your DEX, AGI, STR or CHA for it’s damage bonus. Additionally, you ignore up to one singular penalty to the Accuracy of your Shuriken or Shuriken-like attacks. This could be a wound, or it could be a single level of Fatigue. Treat this like “Strong-Willed” in the way it works, as whenever something else would hinder the Accuracy of your Shuriken, you may swap to ignoring that, and take the new penalty that you would ignore. In relation to conditions, this could ignore all ranks of that Condition.

### Fūma Mastery
{:#fuma-mastery}

Your thrown weapons have no need for ranges. You can hit anything you can physically see, regardless of range. If used in combat, or to attack, the attack gets a \-1 to it’s ACC for every 3 yards outside the weapon’s normal range the target is.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-11}

### Fūma Arsenal
{:#fuma-arsenal}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 8  
**Scale:** 6  
**Limit:** 4

**Effects**

Increase the maximum Range of thrown weapons by 5 yards per rank.

Increase the number of weapons you can throw as part of a Multi-Throw by 1 per rank.

Once per attack, reduce penalties from Cover against a thrown weapon by 1 per rank.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-11}

**D-Rank**

### Kage Shuriken no Jutsu - Shadow Shuriken Technique
{:#kage-shuriken-no-jutsu-shadow-shuriken-technique}

**Type:** D Rank \- Jutsu  
**Stamina:** 12  
**Damage:** As Weapon  
**Range:** Weapon Range  
**Speed:** As Weapon \+4  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Weapon

**Effects**

Throw two weapons of the same type but resolve them as one attack.

After seeing the target's first Defense result, you can force them to reroll that Defense against the second trajectory. If you do, the attack deals 75% Final Damage and cannot inflict a Wound above Major.

**C-Rank**

### Fūma Shuriken: Engetsu - Returning Crescent
{:#fuma-shuriken-engetsu-returning-crescent}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank C  
**Stamina:** 14  
**Damage:** As Weapon  
**Accuracy:** \-1  
**Range:** Weapon Range \+10  
**Speed:** As Weapon \+4  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Weapon

**Effects**

Choose one bend point within half the Attack's maximum Range. Draw the projectile's path from you to that point and then to the target; both segments must be physically clear.

The target calculates ordinary Cover from the bend point rather than from you. This can attack around a corner or shield that blocks one line, but never through Full Cover, sealed barriers or solid terrain.

After the Attack resolves, the weapon returns along the same path if that route remains clear. Catching it is Speed 0\.

# Funato
{:#funato}

***CANON ANIME CLAN \- RARE***

*Funato descendants brought an old maritime inheritance into Haven Ember: gill-like markings, comfort in open water, and chakra that answers the sea more readily than a conjured puddle. The Ember Corps uses that gift for patrol, pursuit, recovery, and battles where the horizon matters as much as the ground.*

## Passives
{:#passives-27}

### Sea Legs
{:#sea-legs}

Funato are comfortable on moving decks, in surf, and under water long before they learn to weaponise the sea.

Gain \+2 Athletics and \+2 Survival on checks primarily involving swimming, diving, currents, navigation, boats, or remaining stable on a moving vessel. Mundane water, waves, rain, and the motion of a vessel do not impose an Awareness penalty on you by themselves.

This does not remove supernatural terrain or environmental effects.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-26}

### Sea Dragon Blood
{:#sea-dragon-blood}

Type: Ability

Cost: 20

Scale: 10

Limit: XP/500

Effects

You can breathe underwater and move through natural bodies of water at \+10% movement per rank.

Suiton techniques using at least 20 gallons of natural water cost 1 less Chakra per rank, minimum 1\.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-24}

**C-Rank**

### Suiton: Kairyū no Michi - Sea Dragon Wake
{:#suiton-kairyu-no-michi-sea-dragon-wake}

Type: C Rank \- Jutsu

Chakra: 24

Range: 30

Area: Line 10, 30

Speed: 16

Seal Speed: 16

Special: Knockback 6

Tags: Attack, AoE, Suiton

Effects

Requires at least 20 gallons of water in the affected line. On a full hit, choose whether each target is pushed toward the far end or pulled toward the near end.

# Iburi
{:#iburi}

***CANON ANIME CLAN \- RARE***

*The Iburi can loosen their bodies into chakra-bound smoke, an extraordinary gift for infiltration and escape with an equally extraordinary failure state. Haven Ember training is built around that contradiction: use the freedom of a body without solid edges, while never forgetting what a hard wind can do to cohesion.*

## Passives
{:#passives-30}

No additional Heritage passives.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-29}

### Smoke Body
{:#smoke-body}

Type: Ability

Cost: 15

Upkeep: 4

Speed: 5

Limit: 1

Effects

While active, you can pass through non-chakra-reinforced openings at least an inch wide and gain \+4 Dodge against non-AoE physical attacks.

You may move through a creature’s occupied space if there is a physically open path for smoke, but may not end movement inside another creature.

While fully smoke-bodied you cannot normally use weapons, make Taijutsu attacks, use Handseals or Cast Jutsu. You can however use Grapple Jutsu. When you do, the opposing creature gains advantage on all associated Grapple Defence moves, you cannot \- however, deal damage with any grapple technique while smoke-bodied.

You gain the following Link while Smoke Body is active:

### Partial Reformation — \[Chakra 5, Speed +2, Link\]
{:#partial-reformation-chakra-5-speed-2-link}

Link this to a Melee Attack, Grapple action, item interaction, or Perform Handseals action. You reform the body parts required to perform that action. Until the linked action fully resolves, you are treated as solid and do not receive Smoke Body’s \+4 Dodge bonus. Afterward, you immediately return to smoke if Smoke Body is still active.

Ending Smoke Body is Speed 3\.

Fūton attacks against you while Smoke Body is active gain \+3 Accuracy. On a full hit, end Smoke Body and suffer Stun 6 in addition to the attack’s normal effects.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-27}

**D-Rank**

### Kemuri Kakure: Vanishing Haze
{:#kemuri-kakure-vanishing-haze}

Type: Ninjutsu, Rank D

Chakra: 12

Speed: 8

Tags: Utility, Self, Smoke

Requires: Smoke Body active

Effects

Until the end of your next Action, reduce penalties to Stealth caused by moving by 5 and treat your body as ordinary environmental smoke for the purpose of mundane attempts to identify which part of a larger smoke cloud is you. Attacking, forming obvious handseals through Partial Reformation, or leaving all smoke/cover ends this effect.

**C-Rank**

### Kemuri Utsusemi - Smoke Cicada Escape
{:#kemuri-utsusemi-smoke-cicada-escape}

Type: C Rank \- Jutsu

Chakra: 20

Speed: 4

Tags: Interrupt, Self, Movement

Effects

Requires Smoke Body active. Use as a Defensive Interrupt against a non-AoE attack.

Make a Dodge with \+2. On success, move up to NDB yards to a location air could physically reach and end Smoke Body.

On failure, resolve the attack normally and Smoke Body remains subject to its usual weaknesses.

# Karatachi Family
{:#karatachi-family}

***CANON FAMILY***

*The Karatachi name carries the memory of old Mist leadership, but no office follows the bloodline in Haven Ember. What remains is a family tradition of public service, discipline, and competence under pressure \- deliberately modest beside the stranger bloodlines around it, and useful for exactly that reason.*

## Passives
{:#passives-26}

### Stand Your Ground
{:#stand-your-ground}

After you finish an Action without voluntarily moving during that Action, you become Braced until the start of your next Action.

While Braced:

- Gain \+2 to Block and Parry.

- Gain \+3 to rolls made to avoid Aborting an Action because of damage.

- Reduce hostile Knockback, pulls, or other forced movement by 2 yards, minimum 0\.

- Gain \+2 on any roll specifically made to remain standing or resist being moved.

Braced ends immediately if you voluntarily move. Being moved by an enemy does not end Braced until that movement has fully resolved.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-25}

### Mist-Born Discipline
{:#mist-born-discipline}

Type: Ability

Base Cost: 8

Scale: 6

Limit: 3

Effects

Gain \+1 per rank to Stamina Exhaustion rolls and to Resistance rolls against fear-based Immobilization effects.

While Braced, also gain \+1 per rank to rolls made to resist Stun, Immobilization, Knockback, forced movement, or an effect that would make you Prone, when that effect allows a roll.

Once per combat, after rolling one of the above rolls or a roll to avoid Aborting an Action, you may add \+2 per rank before the result is resolved.

# Ouyoku
{:#ouyoku}

***RARE LINEAGE \- SETTING ORIGINAL***

*The Ouyoku descend from observatory families whose Junsugan was built for distance, darkness, and impossible sightlines. In the Ember Corps they are prized as reconnaissance specialists: not because the eye knows everything, but because it can turn a bad angle, a distant silhouette, or a night horizon into actionable information.*

## Passives
{:#passives-20}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-19}

### Junsugan
{:#junsugan}

**Type:** Dōjutsu Ability  
**Cost:** 20  
**Upkeep:** 3  
**Speed:** 1  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

While active, gain 240-foot-equivalent Darkvision and 120-foot-equivalent Chakra Sight for narrative-scale perception. In combat, extend ordinary visual Search Range to the battlefield limit unless terrain blocks sight. With this you can see around corners, through invisible barriers such as glass, and off of reflections. This gives you utter visual knowledge of the battlefield from all angles. It does not automatically reveal people or chakra, simply extending your visual sight to everywhere. You can see everything on the battlefield that is not behind an opaque barrier or wall of some kind. 

Gain \+4 Search against targets 20 or more yards away and ignore the first \-3 Visibility penalty from distance, darkness, smoke, or weather.

The Junsugan does not see through solid cover and does not identify techniques automatically.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-18}

**C-Rank**

### Kyōkai Ayumi - Boundary Step
{:#kyokai-ayumi-boundary-step}

**Type:** C Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 20  
**Range:** Self  
**Speed:** 8  
**Tags:** Utility, Self, Movement, Dōjutsu

**Effects**

Requires Junsugan active. Move up to to a location you can clearly see.

This movement cannot pass through barriers, creatures, or sealed space. If used from Stealth it reduces your Stealth TN by 5 but does not automatically break Stealth.

# Sarutobi
{:#sarutobi}

*The Sarutobi name survived because its inheritance was never one miraculous bloodline. Haven Ember remembers generations of disciplined fundamentals, fire release, weapon work, teaching, and stubborn loyalty \- exactly the sort of broad competence the Ember Corps can build a squad around.*

## Passives
{:#passives-11}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-10}

### A Thousand Familiar Lessons
{:#a-thousand-familiar-lessons}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 10  
**Scale:** 10  
**Limit:** XP/500 , Max 3

**Effects**

Rank 1: Choose Accuracy, Dodge, Parry, or Chakra Exhaustion. Gain \+1 to the chosen value, this increases by \+1 for each rank you gain in this ability.

Rank 2: Gain Elemental Affinity: Jutsu. This special Affinity applies to all Ninjutsu, Genjutsu and Taijutsu, following the normal rules for Affinity. Your ranks in this Affinity are equal to your XP/200, with a Maximum of 10\. This does not grant Aptitudes you don’t normally have access to, even if you have Affinity for Lightning, you may not learn Lightning Jutsu without the appropriate Aptitude. 

Rank 3: Using the **Mentor** downtime action, you can learn any Jutsu that you have witnessed, without the help of a teacher. This includes Jutsu that are limited to certain clans, and speciality jutsu that you would normally not apply for. For each rank above your normal limit this Jutsu is, increase it’s costs by 50% (including seal speed), and if that clan would require a special Clan feature that you don’t possess, such as a Sharingan, or a Kekkei Genkai that you don’t have, you also double any CE/STM penalties that Jutsu would give you.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-10}

**D-Rank**

### Tanjun Hien - Simple Flying Swallow
{:#tanjun-hien-simple-flying-swallow}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank D  
**Chakra:** \+8  
**Speed:** \+2  
**Tags:** Link, Melee, Weapon

**Effects**

Link Hien to a Melee Weapon Attack. For that Attack, increase the weapon's Reach by 1 yard and ignore 2 points of Damage Reduction per point you beat their defences by.

The Attack also counts as dealing \+10% damage for the purpose of determining Wounds per point you beat their defence.

A weapon must have a defined striking edge or point to use Hien.

**C-Rank**

### Haisekishō - Burning Ash
{:#haisekisho-burning-ash}

**Type:** C Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 24  
**Damage:** 18d8\>\!8  
**Range:** 20  
**Area:** 15  
**Speed:** 14  
**Seal Speed:** 16  
**Special:** Ignite 4

**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Environmental, Energy

**Effects**

The ash persists for 30 IC. Visibility inside it is \-2.

You can detonate the cloud with any damaging Katon that touches it. When detonated, creatures inside defend against this technique using the original Accuracy; afterwards the cloud ends.

# Shiin
{:#shiin}

***CANON ANIME CLAN \- OLD SOUND***

*Shiin memory techniques survive in Haven Ember as a small and closely watched hiden tradition. They do not grant broad mind control; they obstruct chosen routes through memory, making the lineage valuable to intelligence work and dangerous enough that Ember Corps instructors emphasise limits, consent, and mission authority.*

## Passives
{:#passives-31}

### Memory Reader
{:#memory-reader}

You can deliberately skim a recent sensory memory from another person.

### \[Speed 10, Chakra 10, Utility, Touch, Genjutsu\] Read Memory
{:#speed-10-chakra-10-utility-touch-genjutsu-read-memory}

Against a willing or unconscious living target, no attack roll is required. Against a resisting target, make a normal Genjutsu Attack at Touch range.

On a full hit, ask one specific question about something the target personally saw, heard, smelled, tasted, or physically felt within the last 24 hours. The answer is limited to what the target actually perceived; it does not reveal deductions, secret information they never knew, or memories already removed or supernaturally sealed.

A target can be unwillingly read by you only once per scene. A willing target can share any number of memories at the GM’s discretion.

### Sound-Hidden Tradition
{:#sound-hidden-tradition}

Shiin Clan Genjutsu may use a flute, ocarina, whistle, song, or other clearly audible performance as their casting medium instead of handseals.

Doing so does not reduce the technique’s Speed or other costs. A creature that cannot hear the performance cannot be affected by a Shiin technique cast this way. Silence or an effect that completely blocks sound prevents the casting.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-30}

No Heritage-specific purchasable abilities.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-28}

**D-Rank**

### Oto Genjutsu: Borrowed Voice
{:#oto-genjutsu-borrowed-voice}

Type: Genjutsu, Rank D

Chakra: 10

Range: 25

Speed: 8

Tags: Utility, Genjutsu, Sound

Effects

Create a voice or other sound you have personally heard, originating from any point in Range. The sound may speak words you choose, but it cannot convincingly reproduce information, accents, passwords, or verbal habits you have never heard from the source.

If you have successfully Read Memory containing the sound or voice, the reproduction is exact. A suspicious observer may contest your Genjutsu Accuracy with Awareness to identify it as false.

### Kioku Utsushi: Memory Projection
{:#kioku-utsushi-memory-projection}

Type: Genjutsu, Rank D

Chakra: 14

Range: 15

Area: 6

Speed: 10

Tags: Utility, Genjutsu

Effects

Project a short sensory scene from a memory you have personally experienced or successfully read. Choose up to 30 seconds of sight and sound. Creatures in the Area perceive the scene as an illusion layered over the real environment.

This technique does not control behaviour or conceal physical interaction with the real world. It is useful for showing witnesses exactly what someone remembers, reconstructing a crime scene, creating a distraction, or demonstrating a face, route, voice, or event without relying on description.

**C-Rank**

### Magen: Broken Refrain
{:#magen-broken-refrain}

Type: Genjutsu, Rank C

Chakra: 22

Accuracy: \-1

Range: 20

Area: Cone 8

Speed: 14

Special: Stun 2

Tags: Attack, Genjutsu, Sound, Control

Effects

A discordant phrase catches in the target’s perception and repeatedly interrupts the transition between thought and action.

On a full hit, apply Stun 2\. Until the end of the target’s next Action, the first Utility Action they declare has its final Speed increased by 3\.

If cast through Sound-Hidden Tradition, targets must be able to hear you when the technique resolves.

### Kioku Sōshitsu - Memory Blocking Technique
{:#kioku-soshitsu-memory-blocking-technique}

Type: C Rank \- Jutsu

Chakra: 24

Accuracy: \-1

Range: 15

Speed: 14

Seal Speed: 18

Tags: Attack, Genjutsu

Effects

On a full hit, choose one specific event, face, place, or piece of information the target personally experienced within the last month. For 1 hour they cannot consciously recall it.

# Yotsuki
{:#yotsuki}

***CANON CLAN***

*The Yotsuki bodyguard tradition has an obvious place in the Ember Corps. Old Cloud training survives as service, physical conditioning, and the instinct to put yourself where the impact is about to happen, turning personal durability into time and room for the rest of the squad.*

## Passives
{:#passives-13}

### Bodyguard’s Instinct
{:#bodyguard-s-instinct}

When you declare Take the Hit for an ally, you may move up to 2 yards toward that ally before resolving the action. This movement costs no AP or Speed, cannot pass through occupied spaces or solid barriers, and can only be used to enter or maintain a legal position for Take the Hit.

If Take the Hit succeeds, the protected ally gains \+2 on their next Defensive Roll before the end of their next Action. This bonus is lost if they use it against the same attack that you intercepted.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-12}

### Shoulder of the Cloud
{:#shoulder-of-the-cloud}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 8  
**Scale:** 6  
**Limit:** 4  
Gain \+1 per rank to Take the Hit rolls.

When you successfully Take the Hit for an ally, regain 2 AP per rank, up to your normal maximum.

After a successful Take the Hit, reduce the intercepted attack’s Final Damage against you by 5% per rank, counting after all other reductions and reductions. After that attack resolves, the protected ally may move up to 1 yard per rank without AP or Speed. 

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-12}

**D-Rank**

### Raiton: Ikazuchi Ayumi - Thunder Intercept
{:#raiton-ikazuchi-ayumi-thunder-intercept}

Type: Ninjutsu, Rank D

Chakra: 12

Speed: \+2

Tags: Link, Raiton, Movement, Interrupt, Self

Effects

Link this technique to Take the Hit or to a Defensive Interrupt made against an Attack targeting an adjacent willing ally.

Before that defense resolves, move up to AGI/10 yards toward a legal interception point. This movement must end within reach of the ally or on a space that allows the linked defense to function.

If linked to a successful Take the Hit, gain \+2 on your first valid Defensive Interrupt against the intercepted Attack.

Thunder Intercept cannot move through barriers or effects that would normally stop movement.

**C-Rank**

### Raiton: Raimei Tate - Thunder Guard
{:#raiton-raimei-tate-thunder-guard}

Type: C Rank \- Jutsu

Chakra: 22

Speed: \+3

Tags: Interrupt, Link, Raiton, Self

Effects

Link this to Take the Hit, a Block Interrupt, or use it on it’s own as a Speed 3 interrupt when you would take damage or effects.

Reduce the Final Damage you take by NDB × 4, counting after all other reductions, then the attacker gains Paralysis 2 for 20 IC if they were within 2 yards.

<div id="heritage-up-1" class="clan-cost-banner"><span class="clan-cost-value">1 UP</span><span class="clan-cost-caption">HERITAGES <span class="clan-cost-count">11 ENTRIES</span></span></div>

# Hokori
{:#hokori}

***RARE LINEAGE \- DUST RELEASE***

*Haven Ember’s archives use Hokori as a catch-all name for the vanishingly rare descendants capable of maintaining the three-way elemental equilibrium behind Dust Release. Fire provides compression, Wind gives the boundary direction, and Earth stabilizes the structure long enough for chakra to separate matter inside it. The old stories call that “erasure.” Modern Hokori instructors are less romantic: a failed boundary is usually harmless, but a successful one leaves very little behind.*

*Hokori are therefore trained less like artillery and more like surveyors. They study angles, materials, sightlines, and where an enemy can be forced to stand. In combat, a Hokori is at their best when allies create the opening and the Dust user turns that opening into a precisely measured kill-zone.*

## Passives
{:#passives-19}

### Particle Intuition
{:#particle-intuition}

Hokori training makes the structure of solid matter unusually intuitive. As a Speed 5 Utility Action while touching an unattended solid object, terrain feature, or created barrier, you may make a Chakra Control check in place of Research, Artisan, or Survival to determine its composition, approximate thickness, hollows, seams, and obvious structural weaknesses. The GM sets the TN based on complexity.

This is analysis, not supernatural omniscience. It does not reveal hidden creatures, seals, poison, ownership, history, or chakra properties you could not otherwise sense, and it grants no direct Accuracy or Damage bonus.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-18}

### Three-Nature Equilibrium
{:#three-nature-equilibrium}

Type: Ability  
Cost: 25  
Limit: 1  
Requires: Katon, Fuuton, and Doton Aptitudes

**Effects**

Gain Elemental Affinity: Jinton. Jinton is treated as its own element and does not include its component elements.

Whenever a Jinton Perform Handseals action is interrupted by damage, gain \+3 to the Chakra Control check to maintain it. Jinton techniques cannot have their Seal Speed reduced below half their listed Seal Speed by any source.

### Calibration
{:#calibration}

After combat begins, the first time a Katon, Fuuton, or Doton Jutsu with a Final Chakra Cost of at least 10 resolves and meaningfully affects another creature, object, or the battlefield, gain that nature’s Calibration. You may hold one Katon, one Fuuton, and one Doton Calibration at a time. They last until the end of battle or until spent.

When declaring a Jinton technique, you may spend any Calibration you hold:

Katon — Compression: You may reduce the technique’s Area by up to half. If you reduce it to half, reduce its inherent Accuracy penalty by 1, to a minimum penalty of 0\.

Fuuton — Projection: Increase the technique’s Range by 10 yards.

Doton — Stability: Gain \+5 on Chakra Control checks made to prevent that technique from collapsing.

Perfect Equilibrium: If you spend all three different Calibrations on the same Jinton technique, also reduce its Delay by 3, to a minimum Delay of 3\.

A Jutsu cannot generate Calibration from Jinton itself, and spending a Calibration removes only the Calibration spent. This means a Hokori can build toward one perfect boundary or repeatedly use smaller pieces of their setup.

## Jinton Techniques
{:#jinton-techniques}

**C-Rank**

### Jinton: Keii Kekkai - Coordinate Lattice
{:#jinton-keii-kekkai-coordinate-lattice}

Type: C Rank \- Jutsu  
Chakra: 18  
Damage: 20d4\!o\>3  
Range: 30  
Area: 12  
Speed: 8  
Seal Speed: 20  
Tags: Utility, AoE, Energy, Jinton  
Requires: Three-Nature Equilibrium

**Effects**

Create a stationary, translucent geometric lattice centered on a point within range. It remains for 30 IC, and you may have only one Coordinate Lattice active at a time.

When created, unattended nonliving objects and created barriers inside the Area take this technique’s damage. This damage cannot affect creatures or anything currently worn or carried. Against created barriers, it ignores Armor and doubles Final Damage.

The first time you declare a damaging Jinton technique whose center is inside your Coordinate Lattice, you may consume the lattice and choose one:

Focus — reduce that technique’s inherent Accuracy penalty by 1, to a minimum penalty of 0\.

Anchor — gain \+5 on checks made to prevent that technique from collapsing.

Guide — reduce that technique’s Delay by 2, to a minimum Delay of 3\.

The lattice is obvious to anyone who can see it. Enemies are free to leave it, which is exactly why Hokori crews value immobilization, forced movement, and coordinated positioning.

Delay: 8  
Seal Speed: 30  
Tags: Attack, AoE, Energy, Jinton

**Effects**

This technique ignores Armor but not DR. Against unattended objects and created barriers, Final Damage is doubled.

During the Delay, the forming boundary is visible. If you suffer a Major or worse Wound before it resolves, make Chakra Control TN 20; on failure the technique collapses and deals no damage.

**A-Rank**

### Jinton: Hakuri Kekkai - Detachment Prism
{:#jinton-hakuri-kekkai-detachment-prism}

Tags: Attack, AoE, Energy, Jinton

**Effects**

Ignore Armor and half DR. This attack cannot be made a surprise or sneak attack.

From declaration until resolution, you cannot take non-Defensive Actions without aborting the technique. Any Major or worse Wound forces Chakra Control TN 25 or the technique collapses.

If this attack wounds a limb, that limb is destroyed. If this attack inflicts a critical wound, the target dies, and their body evaporates into nothing. This technique destroys all objects, terrain, mists, or anything within it’s area.

# Hoshigaki
{:#hoshigaki}

***CANON CLAN \- EXPANDED GAMEPLAY***

*Hoshigaki descendants are unmistakably suited to the maritime work Haven Ember cannot avoid: pursuit, boarding, underwater recovery, and ugly fights where water keeps accumulating. Their shark-like physiology and predatory style are old Water Country inheritances; their loyalty, like every other heritage in this book, belongs to the Ember Corps.*

## Passives
{:#passives-25}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-24}

### Feeding Pressure
{:#feeding-pressure}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 8  
**Scale:** 6  
**Limit:** 4

**Effects**

When you begin an Action while within 10 yards of a creature suffering Bleed or a Major+ open Wound, gain \+1 Movement per rank for that Action. Additionally, you deal \+10% damage per rank to creatures suffering Bleed or an open Major+ Wound for determining Wounds.

After damaging such a target in melee, regain 1 AP per rank, up to your maximum.

### Shark Physiology
{:#shark-physiology}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 18  
**Scale:** 10  
**Limit:** XP/1000,2

**Effects**

You can breathe underwater and suffer no ordinary penalties for fighting while submerged. Suiton Ninjutsu cannot force you to Suffocate, gain Immob, cause Bleeding or any Wound above Minor.

Use AGI instead of STR for ordinary swimming calculations if higher.

Gain \+3 Awareness to detect bleeding creatures in the same connected body of water.

At the second Rank of this ability, your speed doubles while swimming, and you deal an additional \+25% damage with Suiton Jutsu.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-23}

**B-Rank**

### Suiton: Sameodori Kekkai - Shark-Dance Water Prison
{:#suiton-sameodori-kekkai-shark-dance-water-prison}

**Type:** B Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 34  
**Upkeep:** 8  
**Range:** 12  
**Area:** 10  
**Speed:** 18  
**Seal Speed:** 22  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Environmental, Suiton

**Effects**

On a full hit, create a mobile water sphere centered halfway between you and the target. You and hit targets are submerged while inside.

The sphere follows you up to 3 yards per IC. A creature may leave by reaching the edge using normal movement; forced movement also works.

While both you and a damaged target are inside, gain \+2 Accuracy against that target. Raiton techniques affecting the sphere treat all creatures inside as valid targets where appropriate.

# Insect Glaive Corps
{:#insect-glaive-corps}

**WEAPON TRADITION \- SETTING ORIGINAL**

*The Insect Glaive Corps began with Wastes hunters who discovered that some chakra-active insects could do more than track prey. Properly bred, fed, and bonded, they could taste the different ways chakra moved through a living body \- aggression in the limbs and jaws, motion through the legs and wings, stability through the torso and shell \- then carry that stolen rhythm back to a hunters.*

The first glaives were practical hunting tools: long polearms for keeping claws and teeth away, hollowed through the haft so a bonded insect could launch, return, and nest against the wielder's arm. What began as monster-hunting fieldcraft became one of Haven Ember's strangest martial schools. Modern Corps hunters fight as a pair. The hunters creates openings, the Kinsect steals a fragment of the enemy's fighting rhythm, and the glaive turns that information into movement that should not be possible for a weapon that large.

Kinsects are not kikaichu. Each is a single cultivated chakra-active companion raised alongside its hunter. Most are beetles, moths, mantises, dragonflies, or things that only resemble those species after generations in the Wastes.

## Passives
{:#passives-36}

### The Hunter's Pair
{:#the-hunter-s-pair}

You begin with a Bonded Kinsect and an Insect Glaive at no Ryo cost.

An Insect Glaive is a Large, Two-Handed Polearm with both the Slashing and Piercing Damage Types. Under the normal Weapon Crafting rules, the additional Damage Type means the weapon begins with 2 Artisan Points before any other customization. Its internal Kinsect channel, launching fittings, and resting brace have no Artisan Point cost and function only for a member of the Insect Glaive Corps bonded to the Kinsect using them.

The weapon otherwise follows all normal Equipment, Durability, Artisan Point, and wielding rules. If it is destroyed or permanently lost, a replacement must be obtained or crafted normally. A Kinsect is not destroyed merely because its glaive is lost.

Your Bonded Kinsect is part of your equipment for rules purposes. It is not a Summon, Clone, Puppet, Swarm, or separate character; it has no independent Initiative, cannot take actions by itself, and cannot be used to provide flanking, block spaces, or absorb attacks. Commands that send it into danger are resolved through the actions and techniques below.

All actions granted by this clan are counted as having the Chain X keyword, and Clan-Jutsu from this clan don’t break your Chain.

### Extract Hunter
{:#extract-hunter}

A trained Kinsect can briefly sample the chakra and physical rhythm of a resisting living creature. You can hold one Red, one White, and one Orange Extract at a time.

Willing creatures cannot provide Extracts. A hostile living creature, living Summon, or comparable chakra-active beast can. Humanoids can always provide all three colors. Against stranger anatomy, Red is normally taken from an offensive structure, White from a locomotive structure, and Orange from a core, shell, or defensive structure. If a creature genuinely lacks an equivalent, the GM may rule that color unavailable.

You gain the following actions while your Kinsect is housed on you:

● \[Speed 5, Chakra 5, Attack, Projectile\] Dispatch Kinsect \- Target one valid creature within 10 \+ CHA/5 yards and make a normal Accuracy attack. This deals no damage and cannot inflict a Wound. On a hit, choose Red, White, or Orange. Your Kinsect becomes Deployed and carries that Extract. You cannot Dispatch it again while it is Deployed. On a miss, it immediately returns to you without an Extract.

● \[Speed 2, Utility, Self\] Recall Kinsect \- Your Deployed Kinsect returns to you. If it is carrying an Extract, you gain that Extract. Recall requires the Kinsect to remain within your Dispatch Range; if you or the target move farther away, the Kinsect automatically returns at the end of the current Action and the carried Extract is lost.

Extracts last 30 IC. Whenever you gain a new color, refresh the duration of all Extracts you currently hold to 30 IC. Gaining a color you already possess only refreshes that color; it does not count as gaining a new color for this purpose.

Each color grants the following benefit:

● Red Extract \- Your Insect Glaive Attacks gain \+2 Accuracy.

● White Extract \- Your movement speed is increased by 20%. Vault has its Speed reduced by 1, minimum 2, and its maximum distance is increased by 2 yards.

● Orange Extract \- You gain \+2 to Parries made with your Insect Glaive and reduce hostile Knockback or other forced movement against you by 2 yards, minimum 0\.

When you hold Red, White, and Orange at the same time, they immediately become Triple Up. Triple Up lasts 30 IC, during which you receive all three Extract benefits and your Insect Glaive Attacks gain \+10% Wounding. Triple Up cannot be refreshed and you cannot gain new Extracts while it is active. When it ends, all three Extracts are lost.

### Vaulting Form
{:#vaulting-form}

The glaive is a weapon, walking stick, spring pole, and an extremely irresponsible answer to the question of whether hunters need terrain in order to jump.

You gain the following action while holding your Insect Glaive and able to move:

● \[Speed 4, Stamina 6, Utility, Movement\] Vault \- Move up to AGI/10 yards in any direction, with no more than AGI/20 yards of that movement being directly upward. This is not a Move Action. It may cross gaps and creatures but cannot pass through solid barriers. If you finish unsupported, you begin Falling normally after the Action resolves.

A technique with the Aerial tag may be used while Falling as the direct result of your own Vault or another Insect Glaive Corps technique. The Aerial tag has no rules on its own beyond identifying techniques for Corps abilities and effects.

At Chuunin or 2,500 total XP, whichever comes first, the Corps will reforge the glaive around the hunter who has actually survived long enough to understand it. Your Bonded Insect Glaive gains 5 bonus Artisan Points that may only be spent on Weapon Features, not Structure or Special Properties. These bonus points count toward tier prerequisites and the weapon's total Artisan Points for wielding restrictions, but do not require you personally to possess the Artisan ranks needed to craft them; the Corps armourers perform the work. You may redesign these 5 bonus points with one Downtime Slot. Replacing a permanently lost Bonded Insect Glaive removes these points until the replacement is reforged with one Downtime Slot.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-35}

### Cultivated Kinsect
{:#cultivated-kinsect}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 10  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

Choose one Kinsect strain. This represents how your companion has been bred and trained; the exact species and appearance are cosmetic. You may change strain with one Downtime Slot if you have access to Haven Ember's breeders or an equivalent facility.

Assist \- When you hit with an Insect Glaive Attack while your Kinsect is housed, the Kinsect joins the strike. Add one additional weapon damage die. While Triple Up is active, add two weapon damage dice instead.

Speed \- Dispatch Kinsect has Speed 4 instead of 5, Recall Kinsect has Speed 1 instead of 2, and your Dispatch Range is increased by 5 yards.

Powder \- When Dispatch Kinsect hits, place Powder 1 on that target, to a maximum of Powder 3 from you. When you hit that target with an Insect Glaive Attack, you may consume any amount of your Powder on it. Add 1d8 Energy damage per Powder consumed. This added damage cannot independently inflict a Wound and is part of the triggering Attack for defenses and damage reduction.

### Aerial Hunter
{:#aerial-hunter}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 10  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

While Falling as the direct result of your own Vault or an Aerial Insect Glaive Corps technique, ignore the normal Prone penalties from Falling for Insect Glaive Attacks and Defensive Interrupts.

After you hit with an Aerial Insect Glaive Attack, you may move up to AGI/20 yards in any direction without Speed or AP. If at least 2 yards of this movement are upward, reset your current Falling IC and distance. This movement cannot exceed your normal Vault's vertical limit.

### Mutual Instinct
{:#mutual-instinct}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 12  
**Limit:** 1  
**Requires:** Cultivated Kinsect

**Effects**

You and your Kinsect have stopped behaving like a hunters issuing commands to an animal and started behaving like one fighting style with an inconvenient number of wings.

Immediately after you hit the creature your Kinsect is currently Deployed against with an Insect Glaive Attack, you may resolve Recall Kinsect at Speed 0\. The Extract is gained after the triggering Attack fully resolves.

Additionally, if an Insect Glaive Attack modified by Kinsect Mark misses, your Kinsect immediately returns instead of remaining Deployed.

### Predator's Trinity
{:#predator-s-trinity}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 15  
**Limit:** 1  
**Requires:** 2,500 total XP, Mutual Instinct

**Effects**

Triple Up lasts 40 IC instead of 30\.

When Triple Up ends normally, choose Red, White, or Orange. You retain that single Extract for 20 IC. This retained Extract cannot immediately trigger another Triple Up until you successfully gain at least one different color after the previous Triple Up ended.

Link this technique to an Insect Glaive Attack while your Kinsect is housed. If the linked Attack hits a valid Extract target, immediately resolve Dispatch Kinsect against that same target without additional Speed, Chakra cost, or a second Defense. Choose the Extract normally. The Kinsect becomes Deployed carrying it.

Kinsect Mark cannot be linked to Dispatch Kinsect, Grand Harvest, or another technique that already sends the Kinsect away from you.

Before making the Attack, move up to half your normal Vault distance. This movement may be vertical and follows the normal restrictions of Vault, but does not require a separate Vault Action or its Stamina cost.

If you have White Extract, use your full Vault distance instead. If you finish the technique unsupported, you begin Falling normally.

Twist around the haft and use the impact as another launch point. If the Attack hits, move up to AGI/20 yards in any direction after damage resolves. If at least 2 yards of this movement are upward, reset your current Falling IC and distance.

If you have Red Extract, add two weapon damage dice. 

#### Hunter's Recall
{:#hunter-s-recall}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank C

**Speed:** 6

**Tags:** Utility, Movement, Aerial

**Effects**

Your Kinsect turns back toward you and you use its chakra-threaded flight path as a guide for an explosive reposition.

Move up to AGI/10 yards toward your Kinsect or the creature currently carrying it, then immediately Recall Kinsect. This movement may include up to AGI/20 yards vertically and does not require Ground. If the Kinsect is carrying an Extract, gain it after the movement finishes.

If you have White Extract before this technique begins, increase the movement by 2 yards. If you finish unsupported, you begin Falling normally.

### B-Rank
{:#b-rank-2}

#### Grand Harvest
{:#grand-harvest}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 24  
**Speed:** 10  
**Accuracy:** \+2  
**Range:** Dispatch Range  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Utility  
**Requires:** Kinsect housed, not in Triple Up

**Effects**

The Kinsect is flooded with chakra and sent through a target in a tight looping path, sampling more than one part of its fighting rhythm before returning.

On a hit, choose two different Extract colors the target can provide. The Kinsect immediately returns and you gain both Extracts. This Attack deals no damage and cannot inflict a Wound.

On a miss, the Kinsect immediately returns without an Extract. Grand Harvest cannot gain the same color twice and cannot be modified by Kinsect Mark.

#### Falling Comet
{:#falling-comet}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank B  
**Stamina:** 24  
**Damage:** As Weapon \+ 4d10  
**Accuracy:** \+1  
**Speed:** As Weapon \+4  
**Tags:** Attack, Melee, Weapon, Movement, Aerial  
**Requires:** You are at least 2 yards above the target

**Effects**

Drive the glaive downward and turn height into a single brutal thrust. Move to the nearest legal space within your weapon's Reach of the target as part of the Attack and end Falling after it resolves.

For every full 3 yards you descend as part of this technique, gain \+5% Wounding, to a maximum of \+15%.

If you have Orange Extract, reduce any Wounding penalty caused by a successful Block against this Attack by 10%, minimum 0\. This does not change the Block's normal damage reduction.

### A-Rank
{:#a-rank-2}

#### Threefold Ascension
{:#threefold-ascension}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank A  
**Stamina:** 30

**Speed:** As Weapon \+6  
**Tags:** Attack, Melee, Weapon, Movement, Aerial  
**Requires:** Triple Up

**Effects**

Consume Triple Up on declaration. The Kinsect launches first, the glaive follows, and hunter and companion spiral through the target before the wielder kicks away into open air.

Before the Attack, move up to your normal Vault distance in any direction. This movement may be vertical and ignores the usual requirement to begin Vault from a stable surface. The Attack gains \+15% Wounding from the three consumed Extracts.

If the Attack hits, after damage resolves choose one Extract color the target can provide. Your Kinsect becomes Deployed carrying that Extract without requiring Dispatch Kinsect or another Defense. You may then move up to AGI/20 yards away from the target. If you finish unsupported, you begin Falling normally.

Threefold Ascension consumes Triple Up even if the Attack misses. Predator's Trinity does not retain an Extract when Triple Up is consumed this way.

# Jashin Worshipper
{:#jashin-worshipper}

RARE CULT PATH

Jashinism is a faith, not a bloodline, and it sits uneasily inside Haven Ember. A playable worshipper is still bound by Ember Corps law and squad responsibility; the heritage represents ritual pain, profane vitality, and sympathetic blood curses without granting a licence to murder comrades, civilians, or anyone else the cult would rather call an offering.

## Passives
{:#passives-35}

### Prayer Before Violence
{:#prayer-before-violence}

You track Devotion, starting at 0 and with a maximum of 3\.

You gain the following Action:

### \[Speed 5, Utility, Self\] Invocation
{:#speed-5-utility-self-invocation}

Offer a short prayer to Jashin and choose one hostile creature you can perceive as your Offering. You may have only one Offering at a time. Choosing a new Offering removes the previous one.

When you either deal HP damage to your Offering with an Attack or voluntarily suffer HP damage as an explicit cost/effect of a Jashin Clan technique, gain 1 Devotion.

Devotion returns to 0 at the end of combat.

You may spend 1 Devotion in either of the following ways:

- Conviction — after making a Stamina Exhaustion or Resistance roll, add \+2 before the result is resolved.

- Fervour — when declaring a Jashin Clan technique, reduce its final Speed by 2, minimum 1\.

You cannot spend more than 1 Devotion on the same roll or Action.

### Sacramental Blood
{:#sacramental-blood}

The first time each combat that you inflict HP damage on your Offering with a Slashing or Piercing Melee Attack, you may collect the blood required for Jashin techniques as part of that Attack, provided you have a free hand, weapon, or container capable of retaining it.

This does not create extra blood samples and does not bypass any requirement that the sample be fresh or belong to the correct target.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-34}

### Profane Vitality
{:#profane-vitality}

Type: Ability

Cost: 15

Limit: 1

Effects

Gain \+3 Resistance against Bleed and Wound effects.

While you have at least 1 Devotion, reduce penalties caused by your own Wounds by up to 2\. This does not remove, heal, or reduce those Wounds.

Once per combat, after suffering a Major or worse Wound, you may spend 1 Devotion to gain 3 AP, up to your normal maximum.

## Jutsu

{:#consecrated-circle}

Type: Jashin Clan Jutsu, Rank C

Chakra: 16

Speed: 12

Area: 3

Tags: Utility, Ritual, Environmental

Effects

Draw and consecrate Jashin’s symbol beneath you. The circle lasts for the scene or until physically destroyed beyond recognition. You may have only one Consecrated Circle at a time.

While standing inside your Consecrated Circle:

- Gain \+2 Resistance against fear-based Immobilization effects.

- The Delay of your Jashin Ritual techniques is reduced by 5, minimum 0\.

- When you gain Devotion, gain 1 AP.

Leaving the circle suppresses these benefits but does not end the circle.

**B-Rank**

### Jujutsu: Shiji Hyōketsu - Blood Rite Link
{:#jujutsu-shiji-hyoketsu-blood-rite-link}

Chakra: 30

Accuracy: \-3

Range: 20

Speed: 18

Delay: 10

Seal Speed: 18

Tags: Attack, Ritual, Mark

Effects

Requires a fresh sample of the target's blood obtained during the current scene. During the Delay you must remain inside a ritual circle you draw as part of the technique.

On a full hit, for 20 IC whenever you voluntarily deal HP damage to yourself, the marked target takes equal damage after DR, to a maximum of 10% of its maximum HP per instance. Self-damage from this technique cannot be reduced or redirected.

Leaving the circle, losing the blood sample, or becoming unable to act ends the link.

# Jinrō
{:#jinro}

***RARE CURSED BLOODLINE***

*The Jinrō carry a predatory transformation that feels less like a technique than sharing a body with hunger. Haven Ember does not pretend that danger disappears because someone joins the Corps; instead, Jinrō hunters are trained around discipline, squad trust, and knowing exactly how much of the beast they can afford to invite forward.*

## Passives
{:#passives-34}

### Predator’s Jaw
{:#predator-s-jaw}

Your Basic Unarmed Attacks may be Bite attacks. A Bite may deal Slashing or Piercing damage instead of Blunt and counts as an Unarmed Attack for Taijutsu and other compatible effects.

When a Bite fully hits a creature you are Grappling, gain \+1 Control after the attack resolves.

{:#abilities-33}

### The Beast Hungers
{:#the-beast-hungers}

Type: Ability

Cost: 15

Limit: 1

Effects

Hunger begins at 0\.

Whenever any one of the following occurs, gain 1 Hunger, maximum 5:

- You deal HP damage to a hostile creature.

- You take HP damage from a hostile creature.

- You successfully gain Control in a Grapple against a hostile creature.

Only one Hunger can be gained from this feature in the same 10 IC, even if several triggers occur.

You can spend Hunger on Jinrō techniques. At Hunger 5, suffer \-2 Diplomacy and \-2 Genjutsu Defense against effects that directly provoke rage, predation, or appetite.

Hunger returns to 0 after combat.

Before the Attack, move up to half your normal Movement toward the target.

Rending Pounce cannot be used if you could not legally Grapple the target.

On a full hit, gain 2 Control after damage resolves. You may spend 1 Hunger to gain 3 Control instead.

Until the end of the target’s next Action, its Break Clinch and Escape Grapple actions have their final Speed increased by 2\.

Ending Beast Form is Speed 5\.

While Beast Form is active, Bite attacks gain \+1 Accuracy, and the first Hunger spent on each Rending Pounce or Lockjaw is refunded after the technique resolves.

# Kamizuru
{:#kamizuru}

***CANON ANIME CLAN***

*The Kamizuru brought an old bee-keeping combat tradition into Haven Ember: bred swarms, wax, honey, and explosives maintained as carefully as any weapon rack. Where an Aburame host carries the hive within, Kamizuru operators cultivate territory around the squad and make the battlefield itself feel occupied.*

## Passives
{:#passives-17}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-16}

### Living Hive
{:#living-hive}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 18  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

Your Hive has a Swarm Pool equal to (CHA \* 5)+(Survival Ranks \*5). Damage specifically dealt to your bees reduces this pool before any technique they are maintaining can continue.

The Hive shares your Initiative Count and never receives independent Actions or AP. Bee techniques use your Actions, Interrupts, and costs normally.

After a battle, the Swarm Pool returns to maximum after 1 hour if at least 1 point remained; if reduced to 0, restoring it requires one Downtime action or 5000 Ryo of breeding stock.

Reduce your Swarm Pool by 10 when declaring this technique. If you cannot, it fails before costs are paid.

If the attack fully hits, you can leave 5 bees behind as a visible hazard. The next creature to enter that 2-yard spot within 20 IC takes NDB × 3 Energy Damage; this follow-up cannot Wound.

**B-Rank**

### Mitsurō Bunshin - Beeswax Clone
{:#mitsuro-bunshin-beeswax-clone}

**Type:** B Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 28  
**Speed:** 14  
**Seal Speed:** 20  
**Tags:** Utility, Clone, Creation

**Effects**

Create one clone using the normal Bunshin targeting rules. It has HP equal to CHA and shares your action economy; when you choose an Action, either you or the clone may be its point of origin where physically possible.

When destroyed in melee, the attacker suffers Immobilization 3 for 20 IC from wax and bees unless they pass Resistance against your Chakra Control.

Only one Beeswax Clone may exist at a time.

# Nara
{:#nara}

*Nara shadow craft survives in Haven Ember as patient tactical doctrine: shape the ground, steal the safe route, and let the squad exploit the opening. Their strongest turns are rarely the loudest ones; they are the moment an opponent realises every sensible square has already been claimed.*

**Starting Features**

* Nara may start with Elemental Affinity for Clan/General Ninjutsu.

## Passives
{:#passives-6}

**IQ of…**

You may be a genius, but you sure do struggle to apply yourself. However, there are moments where your intelligence shines. You have a pool of IQ points equal to (XP/500). These IQ points can be used in the following ways. You regain IQ after a Week’s rest (1 OOC Day). When you activate an IQ ability, pay it’s IQ cost as well as any speed or Chakra costs involved.

* \[ 1 IQ Point \] : *Queen’s Plan* \- (Speed \+4, Interrupt, Link)  
  * *This ability may link to any attack you, or an ally makes while that ally is within (Max IQ\*10) yards. You focus your mind, and work towards the endgame. For the purposes of determining damage, the linked attack is counted as its base speed \+4, ignoring any AP or other speed reductions such as Uniques or abilities. If this would cause a wound, you choose the location of the wound, regardless of any other abilities.*  
* \[ 1 IQ Point \] : *Knight’s Plan* \- (Speed 4, CE 10, Clan Ninjutsu, Interrupt)  
  * *You place the battlefield exactly as you wish it, and begin the dance of the Knight. Choose a location within Max IQ \* 3 yards, and create a sphere on that space, measure from it’s centre to you as it’s radius. Each creature of your choice within that sphere may immediately move a number of yards equal to the Diameter of the sphere you measure with this ability as a Speed 0 interrupt.*  
* \[ 2 IQ Point \] : *Pawn’s Plan* \- (Speed 0, AP X)  
  * *You know that battles are not always won by Knights and Queens. Oftentimes, it is the simple pawn that steps forward to Checkmate. Choose one creature you can see, if it hasn’t targeted you with an effect, measure the amount of IC you have gone untargeted, unaffected, or otherwise ignored by that creature to now. The creature must either have been actively ignoring you, or intentionally distracted in some way by your allies to apply for this. You then complete your plan \- You may take an amount of IC of actions equal to (X\*(1+(Y/5)) where Y is the amount of IC that you have been planning, to a maximum of your Maq IQ \* 5\.*   
* \[ 3 IQ Point \] : *Flow of Battle* \- (Speed \-5, Action)  
  * *You saw that right. You lock in, and find your moment to plan. You go back 5 IC, but cannot move until your IC catches up to your next action.*   
* \[ 4 IQ Point \] : *Rook’s Plan* \- (Speed \+2, Interrupt, Link)  
  * *As an attack turns towards you, you spin to declare it true \- You saw this coming, and it is not you that is in danger. Reduce the attacker’s damage bonus by your equivalent damage bonus (If they are using a NDB attack, reduce it by your NDB, ect). If this would reduce the DB to 0, or lower, you negate the attack entirely. Otherwise, apply damage as normal. This technique can be used as a defensive action in and of itself, or be linked to a defensive interrupt.*  
* \[ 5 IQ Point \] : *Checkmate* \- (Speed 50, Delay 50, STM 50, CE 50, Action)  
  * *Once this action resolves, you figure it out. The storyteller must grant you the answer to any one question relating to the situation you are in truthfully, regardless of the length or complexity of that question. If you manage to get this off in combat, the combat ends in a victory that you describe, within reason.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-5}

### Multiple Shadow Bind
{:#multiple-shadow-bind}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 6  
**Scale:** 4  
**Limit:** CHA /30

**Effects**

You can Target multiple people with the Kagemane and Kagenui Techniques. Every person beyond the first adds \+5 to that Jutsu’s Chakra Cost, and for the case of Kagemane, \+2 to its Upkeep. You can Target up to 1.5 (rounded down) additional people per Rank.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-5}

**D-Rank**

### Kagemane no Jutsu - Shadow Imitation Technique
{:#kagemane-no-jutsu-shadow-imitation-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 16 \+ X\*2  
**Upkeep:** 5  
**Accuracy:** \-6  
**Range:** CHA/5 (Doubled in Dusk/Dawn)  
**Speed:** 16  
**Seal Speed:** 12  
**Tags:** Attack

**Effects**

X is the number of times you’ve previously hit with this jutsu in the present battle. For as long as you maintain this jutsu, they stop acting on their own IC and being tracked in initiative all together.

While caught, the following effects apply:

Anyone caught by this technique is treated as being in a Clinch using 20 \+ CHA/4 \+ CC/10 \- X\*2 to determine your own Grapple TN. Once every 15 IC or whenever they advance a fatigue level they may attempt a free Break Clinch check. For every time the target has attempted to break out of this technique they gain a \+2 bonus.

Any time you move (including forced movement), your target is moved in an equal amount mirrored to you (if you move west away from them they will move east the same amount, if you then move north they will also move north).

Any time you take an action they will immediately copy you performing the action with the exact same Speed, Fatigue Cost, Accuracy, etc (and pay the fatigue cost normally). This includes actions like Drop Weapon even if you aren’t holding a weapon). If you use any action that requires a free hand whilst the victim is holding something in their hands, they drop the item in their hands before mimicking the seals you are performing.

If they are forced to use a jutsu they haven’t learned its Fatigue cost increases by \+2, with an additional \+3 if they would be unable to learn the jutsu. If they are forced to perform a jutsu they cannot physically do (like a clan jutsu or drawing a weapon they don’t have) they mimic your actions but perform nothing.

Attacking the target directly causes them to attack back. Using any single target attack causes them to attack the same target with a \-3 accuracy. Any areas have their center mirrored similar to how movement works. If the target or center is outside of their normal range (such as different sized weapons or lower Improved Area) then they get a \-1 accuracy for every 2 Yards gap.

Anytime your Victim is attacked, you can partially relax this Technique long enough for them to defend themselves. Alternatively, you can force them into taking no Defense in which case they immediately attempt a Break Clinch against this jutsu, with an additional \+5 bonus to whatever other bonuses they had already accrued.

**C-Rank**

### Kage Nui no Jutsu - Shadow Sewing Technique
{:#kage-nui-no-jutsu-shadow-sewing-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 10 \+ X\*2  
**Upkeep:** X  
**Accuracy:** \+2  
**Damage:** (X\*3)d10  
**Range:** CHA/5  
**Speed:** 12  
**Seal Speed:** 14  
**Tags:** Attack, Piercing

**Effects**

X has a maximum of 6\. This technique ends if its Upkeep is broken or the target is over double the range of this jutsu away from you.

Using this Jutsu counts as a "Use" for Kagemane No Jutsu. When targeting anyone affected by this jutsu with Kagemane No Jutsu, that technique has its Accuracy penalty reduced by X. Should Kagemane No Jutsu successfully hit, this jutsu ends.

### Kagebuki Shibari no Jutsu - Shadow Neck-Binding Technique
{:#kagebuki-shibari-no-jutsu-shadow-neck-binding-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** \+5  
**Speed:** \+2  
**Requires:** Target trapped in Kagemane No Jutsu  
**Tags:** Link

**Effects**

This technique is added on to any Grapple Jutsu with the Trapping, Ground Fighting, or Submission tags replacing its Stamina cost with a Chakra Cost (with the \+5 increase). The technique is performed by your Shadow Imitation Technique against the trapped target.

**B-Rank**

### Kageyose no Jutsu - Shadow Endgame Technique
{:#kageyose-no-jutsu-shadow-endgame-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 25  
**Upkeep:** 6  
**Range:** CHA/3  
**Speed:** 16  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

Every 10 IC you can use your shadow tendrils to grab onto things (or people) or attack. You pay 1 AP for every 2 Speed of the action you wish your Shadows to make, and can pay more AP to reduce the effective Speed of their action further if you wish. You use your NDB in place of PDB for Damage and gain a \+4 Grapple TN:

* Retrieve Object (X AP) \- Your shadows pick up X unattended objects on the battlefield that are within range; if it’s ever relevant, you can pick up (and hold onto) up to CHA/8 objects this way at once

* Grab (5 AP) \- As per the E-Rank Grapple jutsu

* Bind (Variable AP) \- You can have your Shadows use any Grapple jutsu you know

* Attack (Variable AP) \- Using a weapon it is holding or can get access to (including Multithrows)

# Samsara
{:#samsara}

***RARE DŌJUTSU \- SETTING ORIGINAL***

*Samsara is a rare ocular lineage known to Haven Ember through scattered case files rather than a great clan. Its users describe the world through opposing instincts of creation and destruction. The eye is not the Rinnegan; its power is narrower, stranger, and personal enough that the Ember Corps classifies the bearer before it ever pretends to classify the bloodline.*

## Passives
{:#passives-33}

### Soul Anchor
{:#soul-anchor}

Samsara does not gain **Willpower.** And cannot access or use it in any way, shape or form. They can use the **Limit Break** ability, and the XP cost for Limit Break is reduced by 50%. However, Samsara still carry the Will of a Hunter within them, and their powers over life, death, creation and destruction grant them unique abilities unlike anything else. 

A Samsara can prepare for their next life. By spending 1 Downtime Slot, you may establish a Soul Anchor.

An Anchor is a prepared combination of seals, Chakra imprinting and whatever physical vessel or method your character uses to facilitate their return. You may only possess one active Soul Anchor at a time. Creating another destroys the previous Anchor. An Anchor is a physical thing somewhere in the world.

It may be hidden. It may be moved. It may be found. And it may be destroyed.

The exact appearance of an Anchor is chosen by the player and GM. One Samsara may use an elaborate sealing shrine, another a preserved biological vessel, while another may use technology recovered from the old world.

Regardless of appearance, all Soul Anchors function identically.

Each Soul Anchor created has a TN of 15+(Soul Anchors Active \* 5 ) , and when creating one you must succeed this roll with either Chakra Control or Stealth. On a fail, the Downtime is wasted, and you do not create a soul anchor. 

You may consume Soul Anchors to gain the normal benefits of Willpower destroying them forever, as well as **Turn the Wheel.** 

### Turn the Wheel
{:#turn-the-wheel}

If you die while you possess an intact Soul Anchor, your Ember pattern attempts to return through it. This is not a combat revival. You are still dead for the purposes of the current Scene, Mission, Hunt, or Event. Whatever killed you succeeded, your body remains where it fell, and anything carried by that body remains with it.

After the event has concluded, you reincarnate through your Soul Anchor.

When reincarnating:

- You retain your XP, Attributes, Skills, Abilities, Jutsu, Uniques and Samsara Heritage.  
- You retain your memories and personality.  
- You do not gain the Heritage, Kekkei Genkai, biological abilities, implants, or other special properties of whatever vessel is used.  
- Your new incarnation is mechanically treated as the same base character unless a GM explicitly approves otherwise.  
- Equipment left on your previous body does not magically return.

The Soul Anchor is consumed.

You return at Fatigue 2, recovering normally afterward.

You cannot return to the same Scene, Mission, Hunt, or Event in which you died.

Reincarnation cannot be deliberately used to respec a character, remove unwanted permanent mechanics, obtain another bloodline, duplicate equipment, or otherwise benefit from intentionally killing yourself.A Samsara without an Anchor is just as mortal as anyone else.

Additionally, effects which explicitly seal, capture, consume, destroy, or prevent the movement of the soul/Ember identity may prevent Turn the Wheel at GM discretion.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-32}

### Samsara Eye
{:#samsara-eye}

**Type:** Dōjutsu Ability  
**CE:** 5 \+ (1 per time you use **Turn the Wheel** permanently)  
**Upkeep:** 3 \+ (1 per 5 times you use **Turn the Wheel** permanently)  
**Speed:** 2  
**Cost:** 5  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

When activated choose Creation or Destruction. Switching modes is Speed 6 or can be done as part of the same action to change your past life echo.

- **Creation**: gain \+3 to Barrier, Creation, and crafting-related Jutsu Accuracy/rolls, including Fatigue rolls, Parry Rolls, rolls to maintain seals. This applies generally to all rolls relating to Barriers, creating things, or crafting things, regardless of context; created objects gain \+NDB×2 HP, not including Summons. This does apply to Bushin.


- **Destruction**: damaging Jutsu against objects, barriers, and summons deal \+20% Final Damage, but your healing received is reduced by 20% while active.


When activated, the Samsara Eye reveals the invisible continuity connecting Chakra, memory and identity. You gain a \+(XP/200) bonus to all checks relating to Identify an individual's Chakra signature. Examine possession or soul-related phenomena. Investigate reincarnation. Identify Soul Anchors. Study techniques which directly manipulate identity or Chakra imprints.

If you have previously personally encountered someone's true Chakra signature while your Samsara eye is active, mundane disguises and physical changes do not prevent you from recognising that it is the same individual, as long as your Dojutsu is active.

The appearance varies between Samsara. Concentric rings, fractured pupils, pale halos and overlapping irises have all been recorded. Despite historical comparisons, the Samsara Eye is not the Rinnegan.

**Past Life Echo**

When activating the Eye, choose one Echo.

Switching Echoes is a Speed 6 Action, or can be done as part of the same action you use to change between creation and destruction.

- **Warrior**  
  - Once per 20 IC, gain \+2 to one Physical/Taijutsu Accuracy. Accuracy roll, Parry roll or Dodge roll.  
- **Hunters**  
  - Once per 20 IC, gain \+2 to one Ninjutsu or Genjutsu Accuracy, Accuracy roll, Barrier roll, or roll made to establish or maintain a Seal  
- **Wanderer**  
  - Once per 20 IC, gain \+4 to one non-combat Skill check.

You cannot benefit from multiple Echoes on the same roll.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-30}

### Samsara: White Forge
{:#samsara-white-forge}

**Range:** 10

**Seal Speed:** 16  
**Tags:** Utility, Creation

**Effects**

Requires Creation mode. Create a mundane object up to a 2-yard cube or a wall with HP CHA×3 and DR NDB. It lasts 50 IC. This can create anything mundane. You can create weapons with zero artisan points, or up to 50 units of ammunition with this. You cannot create poisons, pills, or kits. You can make ninja tools. 

Created weapons use ordinary equipment profiles and gain no free upgrades.

**Seal Speed:** 22  
**Tags:** Attack, Energy

**Effects**

Requires Destruction mode. Against Barriers, Clones, Summons, or created objects, double base dice before damage bonuses.

Against living non-summoned targets, this attack bypasses any barriers, Clones or Summons \- Anything such as an Earth Wall, a Block with a Weapon, ect. Any defences that aren’t specifically your target’s body moving to block or dodge are entirely ignored.

# Silverhand
{:#silverhand}

***RARE TRADITION \- SETTING ORIGINAL***

*Silverhand is not a bloodline so much as a Haven Ember cultural inheritance: performers, rebels, hunters musicians, and public agitators who learned that chakra resonates differently when a whole crowd feels the same thing at once. In the Ember Corps, that becomes morale, disruption, signal, and occasionally a weapon loud enough to reshape a fight.*

## Passives
{:#passives-32}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-31}

### Rocker Soul
{:#rocker-soul}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 18  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

Choose Violence or Melody.

Violence: after you take damage, gain \+2 Accuracy on your next Melee or Sound-tagged Attack.

Melody: after an ally within CHA/2 spends Willpower, they regain 2 AP and you gain 2 AP.

A character may not hold both expressions unless a later Talent explicitly allows it.
