# Vanguard Corps
{:#vanguard-corps}

**WEAPON TRADITION \- SETTING ORIGINAL**

Heritage Cost: 1 UP

*The Vanguard Corps are Haven Ember's front-line monster hunters: weapon masters trained to stand close enough to a beast to read the twitch before the charge, the shoulder before the claw, and the half-second opening after several tons of muscle commits to the wrong direction.*

Where other Corps traditions are built around a bloodline, a companion, or a piece of forbidden technology, the Vanguard Corps are built around practice. Their armouries are filled with oversized blades, crushing hammers, shielded lances, transforming weapons and field repairs scarred by things that should have killed the person holding them.

A Vanguard is expected to know one truth above all others: the weapon is not the style. The hunt is. Every form below is a different answer to the same question — how do you survive inside a monster's reach long enough to put it down?

## 

## Passives
{:#passives-37}

### The Vanguard Arm
{:#the-vanguard-arm}

You begin with one custom Melee Weapon worth up to 3 Artisan Points at no Ryo cost. It is your Vanguard Arm. Build it using the normal Weapon Crafting rules in Equipment & Crafting; it uses normal PDB, Durability, WDP, wielding, hands, Damage Type and Artisan Point rules unless this Heritage explicitly states otherwise.

You may designate a different qualifying Melee Weapon as your Vanguard Arm during a Week's Rest by spending 1 Downtime Slot training, balancing and fitting it. You may only have one Vanguard Arm at a time.

Morph mechanisms, shell chambers, bucklers and paired grips granted by a Vanguard Discipline are Corps-specific fittings. They do not grant Ballistic or Firearm access, do not count as separate weapons unless a rule says so, and only function while you are using the Discipline that grants them.

All actions granted by this clan are counted as having the Chain X keyword, and Clan-Jutsu from this clan don’t break your Chain.

### 

### Corps Refit
{:#corps-refit}

At Trailhand or 2,500 total XP, whichever comes first, the Corps rebuilds your Vanguard Arm around the hunter you have become.

Your Vanguard Arm gains 3 bonus Artisan Points that may only be spent on Weapon Features, not Structure or Special Properties. These bonus points may take the weapon beyond the normal 5 Artisan Point design limit, but do not increase the Artisan Point total used to determine whether you personally can wield your own Vanguard Arm.

You may redesign these 3 bonus points during a Week's Rest by spending 1 Downtime Slot. If your Vanguard Arm is permanently lost or destroyed, the bonus points return after the replacement is rebuilt with 1 Downtime Slot.

### 

### Hunter's Opening
{:#hunter-s-opening}

Immediately after you successfully Dodge, Parry, or Block an Attack made by a creature within your Vanguard Arm's Reach, you gain an Opening against that creature until the end of your next Action.

Your next Melee Weapon Attack against that creature may consume the Opening to choose one:

● Reduce the Attack's Final Speed by 1\.

● Add 1 Damage Die to the Attack.

An Opening is lost if you attack a different creature first, become unable to perceive the creature, or no longer wield your Vanguard Arm.

### 

### Hunting Discipline
{:#hunting-discipline}

Choose one Vanguard Discipline from the list below. You only gain the rules of your current Discipline while wielding its Qualifying Weapon as your Vanguard Arm.

You may change Discipline during a Week's Rest by spending 1 Downtime Slot training with a qualifying weapon. You cannot change Discipline during a scene.

Unless a Discipline says otherwise, its Charges, Spirit, Notes, Shells, Phials and other stored resources are lost when the scene ends, when you stop wielding a qualifying Vanguard Arm, or when you change Discipline. A single Attack can generate a Discipline resource only once, regardless of Multi-Attack or effects that cause it to strike more than once.

Certain Disciplines grant Vanguard Guard. While you have Vanguard Guard, you may use \[Speed 6, Interrupt, Defense\] Vanguard Guard to reduce the Damage of the triggering Attack by 25%. This counts as a Block for effects that care about Blocking. If another rule already allows you to Block, you may use either version, but their Damage Reduction does not stack. You do not need the Weapon Block crafting Feature or any other Ability to use Vanguard Guard.

## 

## Vanguard Disciplines
{:#vanguard-disciplines}

### Great Sword — True Charge
{:#great-sword-true-charge}

**Qualifying Weapon:** Large or Extreme Two-Handed Sword.

Great Sword hunters accept that they will not swing often. The entire style is built around making the swing that matters.

● \[Speed 3, Stamina 4, Utility\] Charge — Gain Charge 1, to a maximum of Charge 3\. If you voluntarily move more than 2 yards, make an Attack without consuming Charge, change weapons, or stop wielding the Great Sword, lose all Charge.

● Charged Slash — When declaring a Great Sword Attack, you may consume any amount of Charge. Add \+2 Damage Dice per Charge consumed. If you consume Charge 2, the Attack also gains \+1 Base Speed. If you consume Charge 3, it instead gains \+2 Base Speed and \+10% Wounding.

● Shoulder Tackle — When an Attack hits you while you have at least Charge 1, you may spend 8 Stamina to reduce any hostile forced movement caused by that Attack by 3 yards and retain all Charge. If the Attack moves you 0 yards after this reduction, gain Charge 1 after it fully resolves, to a maximum of Charge 3\.

### 

### Long Sword — Spirit Edge
{:#long-sword-spirit-edge}

**Qualifying Weapon:** Medium or Large Two-Handed Sword.

Long Sword hunters build rhythm rather than force. Every clean cut feeds the next until the blade seems to move ahead of the hunter.

● Spirit Gauge — When you either hit with a Long Sword Attack or successfully Parry with the Long Sword, gain Spirit 1, to a maximum of Spirit 3\.

● Spirit Slash — When declaring a Long Sword Attack, you may spend Spirit 1 to add \+1 Damage Die.

● Spirit Roundslash — When declaring a Long Sword Attack at Spirit 3, you may consume all Spirit. The Attack gains \+3 Damage Dice, \+2 Stamina and \+2 Base Speed. If it hits, enter Red Spirit for 20 IC. While in Red Spirit, you may use Spirit Slash without spending Spirit. You cannot gain Spirit while Red Spirit is active.

### 

### Sword & Shield — Hunter's Guard
{:#sword-shield-hunter-s-guard}

**Qualifying Weapon:** Small or Medium One-Handed Sword with your other hand free.

The shield is not a wall. It is a fist, a wedge, a climbing hook and the half-second of safety needed to keep fighting.

● Hunter Buckler — While using this Discipline, you gain Vanguard Guard. The buckler is part of the Vanguard Arm and does not count as a second weapon.

● Free Hand — You may draw, stow, or use ordinary Small equipment without first sheathing your Vanguard Arm. This does not reduce the Speed or other cost of the item or Utility action.

● Shield Bash — When declaring a normal Vanguard Arm Attack, you may strike with the buckler instead. Change the Attack's Damage Type to Blunt, add \+1 Damage Die and \+2 Stamina. It remains a Weapon Attack and uses the sword's listed Speed, Accuracy and Delay.

### 

### Dual Blades — Demon Mode
{:#dual-blades-demon-mode}

**Qualifying Weapon:** A matched pair of Small or Medium One-Handed Swords, one in each hand.

Dual Blade hunters trade patience for pressure, turning stamina into a storm of cuts that only stops when the hunter does.

For this Discipline the matched pair is treated as one Vanguard Arm. Attacks use the statistics of one blade and do not gain the Two-Handed PDB bonus merely because both hands are occupied.

● \[Speed 3, Utility\] Demon Mode — Enter or leave Demon Mode. Demon Mode ends if you choose to leave it, stop wielding both Dual Blades, become Incapacitated, or the scene ends.

● While in Demon Mode, normal Dual Blade Attacks gain Final Speed \-1 and \+3 Stamina. Once per Attack when one hits, gain Demon 1, maximum Demon 3\.

● Demon Dance — When declaring a normal Dual Blade Attack at Demon 3, consume all Demon. The Attack gains \+3 Damage Dice and \+2 Base Speed. After it fully resolves, Demon Mode ends.

### 

### Hammer — Impact Charge
{:#hammer-impact-charge}

**Qualifying Weapon:** Large or Extreme Two-Handed Club.

Hammer hunters do not cut around a problem. They put enough force through it that the problem forgets what it was doing.

● \[Speed 3, Stamina 4, Utility, Movement\] Power Charge — Gain Impact Charge 1, maximum 3, and move up to 2 yards. Changing weapons or making a Hammer Attack without consuming Impact Charge removes all Impact Charge.

● Charged Impact — When declaring a Hammer Attack, you may consume any amount of Impact Charge. Add \+1 Damage Die per Impact Charge consumed.

● Staggering Blow — When a Hammer Attack that consumed Impact Charge hits, increase that target's next Attack's Base Speed by the amount of Impact Charge consumed. If you consumed Impact Charge 3, the Hammer Attack also gains \+5% Wounding. Creatures immune to interruption or physical staggering may ignore the Base Speed increase at the GM's discretion.

### 

### Hunting Horn — Echo Recital
{:#hunting-horn-echo-recital}

**Qualifying Weapon:** Large Two-Handed Club.

The Hunting Horn is a blunt instrument with excellent acoustics. A practiced Vanguard turns the cadence of impacts into signals that keep an entire hunting party moving together.

● Echo Notes — Once per Attack when you hit with the Hunting Horn, choose Power, Guard, or Pace and add that Note to your sequence. You cannot choose the same Note twice in a row. Hold a maximum of 3 Notes.

● \[Speed 5, Stamina 8, Utility\] Recital — Consume all 3 Notes and choose one melody represented among them. You and allies within 10 yards when the Recital resolves gain that melody's benefit. A creature can hold only one of your Recital benefits at a time.

● Power Melody — The creature's next damaging Attack gains \+1 Damage Die.

● Guard Melody — The creature's next Parry or Block is reinforced. A Parry gains \+1 Parry; a Block increases its Damage Reduction by 10 percentage points. The benefit is consumed after that defense resolves.

● Pace Melody — The creature's next Move Action or Utility action with the Movement tag gains Final Speed \-1.

### 

### Lance — Iron Counter
{:#lance-iron-counter}

**Qualifying Weapon: Large Two-Handed Polearm.**

Lance hunters make a monster fight through the point of the weapon before it ever reaches the person behind it.

● Set Guard — While using this Discipline, you gain Vanguard Guard. If you have not voluntarily moved since the end of your previous Action, Vanguard Guard used with the Lance reduces Damage by 35% instead of 25%. Voluntary movement ends this increased reduction until the end of your next Action.

● Counter Thrust — After you successfully Block an Attack with the Lance, your next Lance Attack against that attacker before the end of your next Action gains Final Speed \-2.

● \[Speed 4, Stamina 6, Utility, Movement\] Guard Advance — Move up to 3 yards. Until this action resolves, forced movement against you is reduced by 2 yards, minimum 0\. This movement cannot pass through creatures or solid barriers.

### 

### Gunlance — Sealed Shelling
{:#gunlance-sealed-shelling}

**Qualifying Weapon: Large Two-Handed Polearm.**

A Gunlance is not a firearm. Its 'shells' are close-range sealed blast chambers built into the head of a polearm and vented through the strike itself. They do not use Ballistic Attacks, Ballistic ammunition, BDB, Reload rules, or any Ballistic Corps feature. While using this Discipline, you gain Vanguard Guard.

● Shell Chamber — Begin each scene with Shell 3, maximum 3\.

● Shelling — Once per Attack when declaring a Gunlance Attack, you may consume Shell 1\. Add 1d8 Energy damage and \+2 Stamina to the Attack. This added damage is part of the same Attack and cannot independently inflict a Wound.

● \[Speed 5, Utility\] Reload Shells — Restore your Shell Chamber to Shell 3\. This is not a Ballistic Reload and cannot benefit from effects that modify Reload.

● Wyvern Burst — When a Gunlance Attack hits, you may consume all remaining Shells, minimum 2\. Add \+1 Damage Die per Shell consumed and \+5% Wounding. 

### 

### Switch Axe — Morph Gauge
{:#switch-axe-morph-gauge}

**Qualifying Weapon: Large Two-Handed Polearm.**

The Switch Axe is a Corps-built transformation frame. In Axe Mode it creates space and stores momentum; in Sword Mode that stored force is pushed through a shorter, more violent cutting edge.

A Switch Axe begins in Axe Mode. Its morph fittings have no Artisan Point cost and only function for a Vanguard using this Discipline.

● Axe Mode — Once per Attack when you hit, gain Switch 1, maximum Switch 3\. While in Axe Mode, increase the weapon's Reach by 1 yard, to a maximum of Reach 4\.

● \[Speed 2, Utility\] Morph — Change between Axe Mode and Sword Mode.

● Sword Mode — Sword Mode loses the extra Reach from Axe Mode. Each Sword Mode Attack consumes Switch 1 and gains \+1 Damage Die. If you have no Switch, immediately return to Axe Mode. If an Attack consumes your last Switch, return to Axe Mode after it resolves.

● Zero-Sum Finish — When declaring a Sword Mode Attack while at Switch 3, you may instead consume all 3 Switch. This replaces Sword Mode's normal Switch cost and \+1 Damage Die. The Attack gains \+3 Damage Dice, \+2 Base Speed and \+5% Wounding. After it resolves, return to Axe Mode.

### 

### Charge Blade — Phial Engine
{:#charge-blade-phial-engine}

**Qualifying Weapon: Medium or Large One-Handed Sword.**

The Charge Blade folds a guarding sword-frame into a two-handed axe head. The first form stores force; the second spends it.

A Charge Blade begins in Sword Mode. Its shield and morph fittings have no Artisan Point cost and only function for a Vanguard using this Discipline.

● Sword Mode — While in Sword Mode, the weapon is treated as One-Handed, cannot receive the Two-Handed PDB bonus, and you gain Vanguard Guard. Once per Attack when you hit, gain Phial 1, maximum Phial 5\.

● \[Speed 2, Utility\] Morph — Change between Sword Mode and Axe Mode. In Axe Mode, you lose Vanguard Guard from this Discipline and the weapon is treated as Two-Handed, gaining the normal Two-Handed PDB bonus if it was not already receiving it. This bonus cannot stack with another instance of Two-Handed.

● Phial Discharge — When declaring an Axe Mode Attack, you may spend up to 3 Phials. Add 1d8 Energy damage per Phial spent. This added damage is part of the same Attack and cannot independently inflict a Wound.

● Guard Point — Immediately after you successfully Block in Sword Mode, you may Morph into Axe Mode at Speed 0 after the Block fully resolves.

## 

## Abilities
{:#abilities-36}

### Partbreaker
{:#partbreaker}

Type: Ability  
Cost: 10  
Limit: 1  
**Effects**

When a Melee Weapon Attack with your Vanguard Arm damages Armor Durability, a physical Barrier, an object, or a destructible monster part, deal \+50% damage to that object's Durability. This bonus applies after the Attack's normal damage is determined.

### 

### Adaptive Arsenal
{:#adaptive-arsenal}

Type: Ability  
Cost: 10  
Limit: 1  
**Effects**

Choose a second Vanguard Discipline for which you own a qualifying weapon. At the beginning of a scene, choose either of your prepared Disciplines as your current Discipline without spending a Downtime Slot.

Once per scene, while wielding a qualifying weapon for your other prepared Discipline, you may change to it with a \[Speed 5, Utility\] action. All resources from the previous Discipline are lost. Corps-specific fittings for both prepared weapons remain functional, but only your designated Vanguard Arm gains Corps Refit bonus Artisan Points.

### 

### Veteran Hunter
{:#veteran-hunter}

Type: Ability  
Cost: 15  
Limit: 1  
Requires: 2,500 total XP, Partbreaker  
**Effects**

When you consume Hunter's Opening, you may take both benefits instead of choosing one: the Attack gains Final Speed \-1 and \+1 Damage Die.

This does not create a second Opening.

If the linked Attack consumed Hunter's Opening, increase this movement to 3 yards.

After Vanguard Execution resolves, all stored resources from your current Vanguard Discipline are lost. This includes Charge, Spirit, Demon, Impact Charge, Notes, Shells, Switch and Phials. Red Spirit also ends. Sword & Shield and Lance have no stored resource to lose.

# Yamanaka
{:#yamanaka}

*Yamanaka families fit naturally into the Ember Corps’ intelligence and command structure. Their techniques make information immediate, let squads coordinate without shouting, and cross the dangerous border between one mind and another; their best operators know that trust is as important as reach.*

## Passives
{:#passives-10}

*No additional Heritage passives.*

## Abilities
{:#abilities-9}

### Mind Network
{:#mind-network}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 15  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

You can establish telepathic communication with willing creatures you know within CHA/2 yards. Maintaining a network of more than CHA/20 creatures has Upkeep 2\. Once this network is established, you can maintain it across a distance of CHA/10 Miles.

A willing linked creature may send a short warning, image, direction, or sentence to every other linked creature as part of declaring an Action or Interrupt.

Mind Network does not read unwilling thoughts and does not reveal information a linked creature does not intentionally send.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-9}

**D-Rank**

### Shindenshin no Jutsu - Mind Transmission Relay
{:#shindenshin-no-jutsu-mind-transmission-relay}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 12  
**Upkeep:** 2+X  
**Speed:** 8  
**Seal Speed:** 8  
**Tags:** Utility, Clan

**Effects**

While maintained, the range of your Mind Network range increases by (50+(X\*50))% This does not let the network pass through effects that explicitly block chakra communication.

Before a willing creature linked to your Mind Network makes an Awareness or Genjutsu Defense roll, you can spend 2 AP to grant that roll \+2. You must be conscious and able to communicate through the network.

Mind Transmission Relay does not share senses, memories, or unwilling thoughts.

**C-Rank**

### Shintenshin no Jutsu - Mind Body Switch Technique
{:#shintenshin-no-jutsu-mind-body-switch-technique}

**Type:** C Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 24  
**Accuracy:** \-4  
**Range:** CHA/2  
**Speed:** 14  
**Delay:** 6  
**Seal Speed:** 14  
**Tags:** Attack, Genjutsu, Control

**Effects**

Resolve this as an Accuracy Roll against the target's Genjutsu Defense. On a full success your body becomes Incapacitated and your mind occupies the target for up to 30 IC.

While occupying them, you can cause ordinary movement, speech, Basic Actions, and non-clan physical actions. You cannot access their memories, bloodline, Jutsu, Uniques, or Willpower.

Each time the controlled body suffers a Major or worse Wound, the target may spend 1 Willpower to immediately end the technique. You can end it as a Speed 0 action.

**B-Rank**

### Shinranshin no Jutsu - Mind Disturbance Technique
{:#shinranshin-no-jutsu-mind-disturbance-technique}

**Type:** B Rank \- Jutsu  
**Chakra:** 34  
**Accuracy:** \-3  
**Range:** CHA/2  
**Speed:** 16  
**Seal Speed:** 22  
**Special:** Confusion

**Tags:** Attack, Genjutsu, Control

**Effects**

On a full hit, for 20 IC the target cannot willingly choose the same target for two consecutive hostile Actions and must spend 1 AP whenever they change targets.

At the start of each of their turns they may spend 1 Willpower to ignore this effect until their next turn. This technique never forces self-harm or an action the target did not already have access to.

<div id="heritage-up-2" class="clan-cost-banner"><span class="clan-cost-value">2 UP</span><span class="clan-cost-caption">HERITAGES <span class="clan-cost-count">14 ENTRIES</span></span></div>

# Aburame
{:#aburame}

*In Haven Ember, Aburame families preserve the old host-bond as a living inheritance rather than a village institution. Their kikaichū make them natural Ember Corps scouts and controllers: patient, information-hungry, and at their best when the enemy is forced to spend time solving the swarm.*

**Starting Features**

* Aburame start with Kikaichu no Jutsu at no XP cost, ignoring its normal requirements. They may start with Elemental Affinity for Clan/General Ninjutsu.

## Passives
{:#passives-2}

### Living Colony
{:#living-colony}

Kikaichu training is part of the Aburame lineage rather than a separate purchase. You can use your bugs to make skill checks through your Kikaichu at range. The normal rules for communicating with Kikaichu and how long they can survive apart from the Host apply, except their survival away from the Host is increased to a week. GMs may apply a penalty of up to \-10 on checks Kikaichu are ill suited for, or disallow checks they simply could not perform. You still perform any relevant actions and pay any associated cost. Awareness, Stealth, and Survival are common uses for spying, sneaking a bug into position, or tracking people. This does not meaningfully deplete the Swarm, and Kikaichu no Jutsu does not need to be active. A single Kikaichu can be trivially crushed by anyone who spots it. Once per 10 IC, when one of your Clan Jutsu successfully affects a creature inside the Swarm, restore CHA x 2 HP to the Swarm.

### Predatory Cycle
{:#predatory-cycle}

When you place a female insect on a hostile target, you can immediately choose one:

* Feeding: the target suffers \-1 on its next Chakra Exhaustion roll before the end of its next Action.

* Frenzy: the target suffers 1d4\*(NDB) Energy damage every 5 IC for the next CHA/2 IC. After that duration, the insect is removed.

* Proliferation: place one additional female insect after the normal Delay. This extra insect cannot trigger Predatory Cycle again.

* Recall: move the Swarm up to CHA/30 yards without AP or Speed.

A target can suffer at most \-3 from Feeding at once.

When a hostile creature carrying at least one female insect declares a Chakra-costing Action, you can pressure the infestation before costs are paid. The creature chooses one:

Purge — increase that Action's final Speed by 3\. After it resolves, remove one female insect from that creature.

Feed — resolve the Action normally. After it resolves, restore CHA × 2 HP to the Swarm and move the Swarm up to CHA/30 yards toward that creature without AP or Speed.

This cannot trigger from Upkeep, passive Chakra loss, or an Action that is cancelled before its costs are paid.

At 2,000 total XP, gain Crouching Mantis, Hidden Centipede at no XP cost if you do not already have it.

## Abilities
{:#abilities}

### Crouching Mantis, Hidden Centipede
{:#crouching-mantis-hidden-centipede}

**Cost:** 10  
**Limit:** 1

**Effects**

The Aburame’s Kikaichu Swarm is now considered a separate character for Stealth. Clan Ninjutsu no longer end the Aburame’s Stealth, but will end the Swarm’s. If the Aburame is in Stealth when they use Kikaichu no Jutsu, the Swarm is created with the same Stealth TN. If the Swarm triggers a Chakra roll, its Stealth ends. The Aburame may have Hide and Improve Hiding affect their Swarm instead of themselves.

Additionally, the Aburame gets access to the following actions:

* \[Speed \+10, Delay 10\] Secrete Bugs \- This action modifies Kikaichu no Jutsu while the Aburame is not in Stealth. The Swarm is created in Stealth 2\.

* \[Speed 0 Interrupt\] Wait \- You can declare a Kikaichu Swarm in Stealth is waiting. While waiting, the Kikaichu Swarm passively and discretely crawls on opponents, waiting to bite all at once. While the Swarm remains in stealth it adds 1 Female Insect every 25 IC (10 Minutes out of Combat). Out of combat, this increases Bugs to a maximum of 10\. Out of combat this prompts an Awareness roll from anyone who gets Bugs.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu}

**E-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Konchū no Bōdō - Hidden Technique: Insect Insurrection
{:#hijutsu-konchu-no-bodo-hidden-technique-insect-insurrection}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank E  
**Speed:** \+2  
**Delay:** 5  
**Tags:** Link

**Effects**

This add-on modifies any attack  increasing to Speed \+5 if the attack su has an Area. If the attack hits, the Aburame places a female insect on the opponent after the delay ends. They are hidden with a Stealth TN of 25 \+ your stealth bonus. This isn't affected by and doesn’t contribute to the penalties for attempting Stealth multiple times per combat. If the person with the Bugs spots them, they may remove them with a Speed 5 action increased by 1 per every extra female insect currently on them.

### Hijutsu: Mushiyose - Secret Technique: Bug Gathering
{:#hijutsu-mushiyose-secret-technique-bug-gathering}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank E  
**Chakra:** 10  
**Speed:** 10  
**Seal Speed:** 12  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

This grants the Aburame a \+5 bonus on relevant Survival, Awareness, and Research rolls, or \+20 when the roll directly relates to finding or understanding insects. The Aburame may be able to get information not normally available, but there is no guarantee a bug's perspective will be useful.

**D-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Kikaichu Mushikui - Hidden Technique: Destruction Bug Bite
{:#hijutsu-kikaichu-mushikui-hidden-technique-destruction-bug-bite}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 15  
**Area:** As Parasitic Destruction Insect Technique \- Kikaichu no Jutsu  
**Speed:** 8  
**Accuracy:** \+X/4  
**Tags:** Attack, Self

**Effects**

X is the number of female insects on the target. The target makes a Chakra roll against TN 8 \+ X × 2, to a maximum TN of 10 \+ your Chakra Control. On hit, the target takes irreducible HP damage equal to 10 \+ 3X, to a maximum of 30\. If the Chakra roll fails, increase that damage by 50%. Until the end of the target's next Action, its next Chakra Exhaustion roll suffers a penalty equal to X, maximum \-3. Any female insects already on the target increase their Stealth Level by 1, to a maximum of 3\.

**C-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Mushidama - Hidden Technique: Bug Sphere
{:#hijutsu-mushidama-hidden-technique-bug-sphere}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 24  
**Upkeep:** 4  
**Speed:** 16  
**Accuracy:** \+X/3  
**Tags:** Attack, Self, Piercing

**Effects**

X is the number of female insects on a target. Target one person inside your Swarm but calculate their Dodge penalties based on the Swarm’s area. If it hits, the Swarm surrounds the target (losing its Area) and cannot be used for anything besides Hijutsu: Kikaichu Mushikui \- Hidden Technique: Destruction Bug Bite targeting only the one surrounded. While in the Swarm, the opponent gains a \-2 Immobilization penalty. Every 10 IC spent in the Swarm applies another female insect to the target. If the opponent moves, the Swarm will automatically chase them at a rate of CHA/30 Yards per IC.

You can end this jutsu with a Return action either reverting the swarm back to normal or returning them to your body as usual.

### Hijutsu: Mushikabe - Insect Screen
{:#hijutsu-mushikabe-insect-screen}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 18  
**Speed:** 8  
**Tags:** Interrupt, Utility, Self  
**Requires:** Kikaichu no Jutsu active

**Effects**

Pull the Swarm across an incoming attack. Choose yourself or one ally inside the Swarm. The Swarm takes the attack's final damage before the protected character. If the attack is single-target and the Swarm still has HP remaining afterward, the protected character is considered not to have been hit. Area attacks still affect the protected character normally, but the Swarm takes only half of the damage it would normally suffer from that Area.

### Mushi Bunshin no Jutsu - Bug Clone Technique
{:#mushi-bunshin-no-jutsu-bug-clone-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 12 \+ 4\*X  
**Upkeep:** X  
**Speed:** 14 \+ X  
**Seal Speed:** 6 \+ 3\*X  
**Tags:** Utility, Clone

**Effects**

Create X Clones, to a maximum of 3\. The Clones occupy anywhere in the Swarm, with swirling bug Swarms shifting their position. You don't need to track the clone's exact position and they are immune to area of effect attacks. While this Jutsu is maintained, using the Hide Among Clones action restores all Mushi Bunshin for free and the Area of your swarm is reduced by 20% per clone. If the Swarm is destroyed, this Jutsu ends.

The Clones have the Aburame's Stats as though they were Fatigue 1\. If they advance a Fatigue level, they are destroyed. Clones are created with copies of the items the user had, except for consumables, chakra-infused items, electronic items, and items with complex moving parts.

While one or more Clones are active, the Aburame gain access to the following actions:

* \[Variable AP\] Attack: The Clone performs a Basic Unarmed Attack, Basic Weapon Attack, a Dual Weapon Attack, or a Multi-Throw. The attack can use any Weapons the Clone has and the attack is modified by Insect Insurrection. This costs half of the total Speed in AP, instead of Insect Insurrection's regular AP cost. Use the Aburame's CHA in place of their STR and Ninjutsu DB in place of their Physical DB. This attack is considered to be Held Back. The Clones can only target someone else who is inside of the Swarm.

* \[1 AP\] Defend \- The clone may Dodge or Parry (using any weapon it has or no weapon) any attack aimed at it. This will not defend the Swarm, but it may prevent the clone from becoming dispersed.

* \[Speed 10\] Masquerade \- With the Aburame’s direct help and guidance, the Swarm condenses down fully into a perfectly you-shaped area or returns to its normal shape. While you-shaped, it gains a \+5 bonus on any rolls to convince other people that it is you. This action can only be used if there is only one clone.

### Parasitic Destruction Insect Technique - Kikaichu no Jutsu
{:#parasitic-destruction-insect-technique-kikaichu-no-jutsu}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Range:** CHA/10  
**Area:** Up to CHA/10  
**Speed:** 12  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

You create a cloud of Kikaichu which cannot be dispersed by abilities that specifically end environmental effects. The Swarm has CHA\*10 HP. It can only take damage from Area effects and only takes half damage if the Area doesn’t encompass the entire Swarm. It’s immune to all conditions except for ignite and knockback. The Swarm's HP persists between uses, and heals at a rate of CHA\*3 every hour out of combat. At 0 HP this Jutsu ends and may not be used again until the Swarm recovers some HP.

Kikaichu may be communicated without to a range of CHA/5 yards through minor fluctuations in the Host's Chakra. Kikaichu can operate beyond that range, and are surprisingly intelligent; but they may not receive new orders and will die after 24 Hours away from their Host. Kikaichu always know the distance and direction to their Host and any female insect within Chakra Control miles.

Aburame gain access to the following actions to utilize their Kikaichu:

* \[Speed 4 or \+4\] Return \- If the Swarm is within range, it temporarily re-enters the Aburame's body ending any actions that modify the Swarm or Ignite status. This can be used as a link allowing the Swarm to benefit from an interrupt. After the Defense is resolved, whether it succeeds or fails, the Swarm returns to the Aburame's body.

* \[AP X or Speed X\] Move \- The Swarm Moves at a speed of CHA/30 for the next X IC. The swarm can slip through tiny cracks and fly.

**B-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Mushitatsumaki - Devouring Cyclone
{:#hijutsu-mushitatsumaki-devouring-cyclone}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 30  
**Speed:** 18  
**Area:** As Kikaichu no Jutsu  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Self, Piercing

**Effects**

Everyone hostile inside the Swarm is attacked. On a hit, deal irreducible HP damage equal to 8 \+ twice the number of female insects on that target, maximum 28, and place one female insect. A target with 3 or more female insects also suffers \-2 to its next Chakra Exhaustion roll. After resolving this technique, the Swarm loses HP equal to CHA; if this would reduce it to 0, leave it at 1 instead.

### Mushi Kame no Jutsu - Bug Jar Technique
{:#mushi-kame-no-jutsu-bug-jar-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Speed:** Variable  
**Requires:** Kikaichu no Jutsu active  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

This is a variable Speed defensive action declared like a Block. You can declare another variable speed defensive action alongside Bug Jar such as Block, Guard, Full Defense, Defensive Formation, et cetera. While active, the Aburame and any of their allies inside the Swarm may choose to let the swarm defend them when attacked. If they do, they may not apply another defense, but damage is subtracted from the Swarm’s HP before the protected person. Attacks that have their damage completely prevented are not considered to have hit their target. Bug Jar will stop non-damaging attacks that need to hit, but it will not stop Genjutsu.

# Akimichi
{:#akimichi}

*Akimichi descendants in Haven Ember preserve calorie control as both family discipline and fieldcraft. In the Ember Corps they are dependable shock troops and protectors, turning stored energy into sudden mass, momentum, and the kind of commitment that can change a whole engagement.*

## Passives
{:#passives-3}

### Akimichi Energy Bars
{:#akimichi-energy-bars}

**Type:** Item  
**Cost:** 20k Ryo each

**Effects**

Eating a bar is a Speed 4 action that raises your effective Calorie Conversion rank by 1 for 50 IC, increasing your current CP maximum by 5\. Eating multiple bars stacks up to a maximum of 3\. If a bar is still active when another one is consumed, its duration is extended until the last bar consumed ends. Each bar also restores 10 \+ RES/10 CP, up to your current CP maximum.

### Calorie Engine
{:#calorie-engine}

You gain 5 ranks of Calorie Conversion at no XP cost. Calorie Conversion uses Calorie Points (CP) as described under the Ability. You recover RES/5 CP after a Full Rest with proper food.

You can overeat up to double your normal CP maximum. For every 10 CP above the normal maximum, suffer \-1 Dodge and \-1 Athletics until enough CP has been spent to remove the excess.

### Mass Is Momentum
{:#mass-is-momentum}

While Baika no Jutsu is active, every full 5 points of X also grants \+1 yard of melee reach and \+10% final damage to damaging melee Taijutsu. When you successfully Take the Hit, convert Baika's Dodge penalty into an equal bonus on that Take the Hit roll. After moving at least 5 yards in one direction, reduce the final Speed of your next Akimichi melee attack by 2\.

While Baika no Jutsu is active, when you hit with a melee Attack after moving at least 5 yards in a straight line, the target chooses before damage is resolved:

Brace — it remains in place, but the Attack counts as 25% higher for Wounding.

Yield — it is moved directly away from you by X/5 yards, minimum 3, after the Attack resolves. This is forced movement and does not gain the Brace Wounding increase.

If Yield would move the target into a solid obstacle, the movement stops normally and causes no additional collision damage.

### Three-Coloured Pills
{:#three-coloured-pills}

An Akimichi may be issued one Green, one Yellow, and one Red pill per mission. Consuming one is Speed 5\. Each later colour may only be taken after the earlier one.

* Green: restore half your normal CP maximum and gain \+5 STR and RES for 50 IC. When it ends, gain Fatigue \+1.

* Yellow: restore your normal CP maximum and gain \+5 DEX and AGI for 40 IC. When it ends, gain Fatigue \+1 and Poison 2\.

* Red: restore CP to your true maximum, gain \+10 STR, RES, DEX, and AGI for 25 IC, and immediately enter Butterfly Mode. When it ends, gain Fatigue \+2 and Poison 4\. You cannot reduce or ignore these costs.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-2}

### Calorie Conversion
{:#calorie-conversion}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 1  
**Scale:** 1  
**Limit:** RES/15

**Effects**

Each rank grants 5 Calorie Points (CP). Spend CP one-for-one to reduce the Chakra cost of an Akimichi Clan Jutsu, to a minimum of 0\. If you reduce the Chakra cost to 0, no Chakra Exhaustion roll is required for that activation. CP spent this way remains spent until recovered. At 0 CP, you can pay 4 HP for each missing CP used to fuel an Akimichi Clan Jutsu.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-2}

**D-Rank**

### Baika no Jutsu - Multi-Size Technique
{:#baika-no-jutsu-multi-size-technique}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 15 \+ X/2  
**Upkeep:** 3  
**Speed:** 10  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

X has a maximum of AGI/2. You subtract X points from your AGI, and add X/2 to your STR and RES. This increase does not increase your Current Vitality and HP but it does increase your maximums in each. The following effects apply during Baika no Jutsu:

* All your unarmed attacks gain Area X/10, or have their existing Area increased by that amount.

* You gain a damage reduction of X/2.

* You have a \-X/2 penalty to Stealth rolls.

* You reduce the severity of any Stuns inflicted upon you by X/10, and any effects which would move you involuntarily will move you X/3 fewer yards.

* Your Taijutsu attacks have a reach of \+1 yards.

### Nikudan Sensha - Human Bullet Tank
{:#nikudan-sensha-human-bullet-tank}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 20  
**Upkeep:** 2  
**Speed:** 6  
**Requires:** Baika no Jutsu or Cho Baika no Jutsu active  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

When you activate this jutsu you are considered to be rolling in place. While this Jutsu is active, the following characteristics which apply to you:

* You are considered to be always Blocking

* You can use the Dodge action.

* You cannot use the Parry action.

* You cannot use any jutsu that requires handseals.

* You have a \-5 penalty to Awareness.

* You cannot grapple and your break Clinch, Break Grapple, and Escape Grapple actions all get a \+10 bonus.

* You ignore Immobilization penalties of less than 10\. If you are ever affected by an immobilization penalty of 10 or more, this technique ends automatically.

* You cannot use any Taijutsu except for the following:

### Rolling Crush
{:#rolling-crush}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank D  
**Stamina:** 15  
**Damage:** (STR/15)d8  
**Speed:** 15  
**Requires:** Nikudan Sensha active  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Blunt

**Effects**

You roll forward in a line until you smash into a target or an obstacle, crushing it with your massive weight enhanced by the rotational force of your charge. You can Move With Attack up to the full speed of this attack (instead of half as normal). If you move at least 5 yards in the same direction while attacking, your Damage Bonus for this attack is doubled. Parries have a \-3 Penalty against this attack, or a \-5 penalty if you moved at least 5 yards towards your target before reaching it.

**C-Rank**

### Bubun Baika no Jutsu - Partial Multi-Size Technique
{:#bubun-baika-no-jutsu-partial-multi-size-technique}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 5 \+ X/4  
**Speed:** \+2  
**Tags:** Link

**Effects**

This is added on to any Taijutsu Attack. During that attack your STR is increased by X, to a maximum of double your normal STR. You cannot use this while Baika no Jutsu or Cho Baika no Jutsu are active.

### Nikudan Hari Sensha - Spiked Human Bullet Tank
{:#nikudan-hari-sensha-spiked-human-bullet-tank}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 30  
Upkeep 4

**Speed:** 6  
**Requires:** Baika no Jutsu or Cho Baika no Jutsu active  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

Using Nikudan Hari Sensha requires at least 30 specially prepared kunai, which may be used repeatedly for this technique and are unable to be used for any other purpose. This functions the same as Nikudan Sensha, with the following exceptions:

* Anyone hitting you with an unarmed attack, suffers Piercing damage equal to your current DR.

* If you Block a Weapon, you block 50% of the damage (instead of the normal 25%).

* You cannot use any Taijutsu except for the following:

**B-Rank**

### Cho Baika no Jutsu - Super Multi-Size Technique
{:#cho-baika-no-jutsu-super-multi-size-technique}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 20 \+ X/2  
**Upkeep:** 10  
**Speed:** 15  
**Tags:** Utility

**Effects**

X is your AGI/2. If used when already in Baika no Jutsu, that jutsu is ended and Cho Baika's Speed is reduced by 10 and its Chakra Cost is reduced by 10 \+ the X of that Jutsu.

When active, the following effects are applied:

* Your AGI is reduced by 50% and your STR and RES are increased by 25%. This increase does not increase your Current Vitality and HP but it does increase your maximums in each.

* All your unarmed attacks gain Area X/5, or have their existing Area increased by that amount.

* You gain a damage reduction of X.

* You cannot Hide.

* You reduce the severity of any Stuns inflicted upon you by X/5, and any effects which would move you involuntarily will move you X/2 fewer yards.

* Your Taijutsu attacks have a reach of \+5 yards.

* Your physical damage bonus is calculated as STR/15.

* You can grapple someone using only a single hand. This does allow you to, potentially, hold one person in each hand.

* You are immune to any environmental effect with an area of X/5 or less.

### Chodan Bakugeki - Butterfly Bullet Bombing
{:#chodan-bakugeki-butterfly-bullet-bombing}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 40  
**Accuracy:** X/5  
**Damage:** (STR/5+RES/5+X)d20  
**Speed:** 25  
**Area:** X\*2  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Blunt

**Effects**

You can spend X Calories on this technique, losing them for the remainder of the day. You must spend at least 15 Calories to use this technique and can go to a minimum of \-5 Calories for this purpose.

You add your physical and ninjutsu damage bonuses together to determine its damage bonus. The primary target takes full damage; anyone else caught in the area takes half of that damage. This jutsu cannot be used while maintaining Baika no Jutsu or Cho Baika no Jutsu.

### Chō Chakra Mode - Butterfly Mode
{:#cho-chakra-mode-butterfly-mode}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 25  
**Upkeep:** 8  
**Speed:** 10  
**Tags:** Utility, Self

**Effects**

Chakra vents from your back like wings. Your movement increases by 50%, your unarmed reach increases by 2 yards, and your Physical Damage Bonus is increased by 2\. When you land a damaging Taijutsu, you may spend up to 10 CP; each 2 CP spent this way increases its final damage by 5%. If activated through the Red Pill, its Chakra cost is 0\.

# Ballistic Corps
{:#ballistic-corps}

**WEAPON TRADITION \- SETTING ORIGINAL**

**Heritage Cost:** 2 UP

*The Ballistic Corps are Haven Ember's gunners, ammunition specialists and hunter-engineers: the people trusted with weapons that turn sealed powder, chakra coils and monster-bone machinery into portable* thunder.

The tradition began in the Ashen Anvil's Ballistics Annex, where recovered firearms and industrial launchers were rebuilt for Hunter combat. Their weapons are not mass-produced firearms. Every chamber is fitted, every recoil system tuned, and every load chosen for the thing the hunter expects to meet outside the walls.

A Ballistic Corps hunter does not simply carry a weapon. They maintain a platform: changing magazines, rebuilding barrels, replacing seal plates and turning trophies from dead monsters into the next answer fired downrange.

## Passives
{:#passives-38}

### Ballistics Certification
{:#ballistics-certification}

Only characters with the Ballistic Corps Heritage may wield a weapon with the Firearm or Ballistic tag unless a rule explicitly states otherwise.

This is a hard Heritage restriction. Characters without this Heritage cannot make Ballistic Attacks, Reload or Brace a firearm, benefit from Ballistic Attachments, use specialist Ballistic ammunition, or use Ballistic Modifications. They may carry, sell, transport or study the equipment, but possessing it never grants access to its combat rules. General Equipment access never overrides this restriction unless a rule explicitly names the Ballistic Corps restriction.

A Ballistic Weapon is a ranged Weapon. It uses the normal Attack and ranged-defense rules, but does not add Physical Damage Bonus. Instead, Ballistic Attacks add Ballistic Damage Bonus (BDB) equal to DEX / 24, rounded as normal.

When making an attack of any type with a Ballistic Weapon, instead of using a base Accuracy result, you roll a 1d20, and add your relevant Accuracy modifier to it, not including your Base 10\. If you use your DEX for your BDB, use your Taijutsu Accuracy, if you use your CHA for your BDB, use your Ninjutsu Accuracy.

Ballistic Weapons cannot Parry ranged attacks. They may Parry Melee Weapon or Melee Unarmed attacks using their listed Parry modifier.

Forge Rating: A catalogue firearm's Forge Rating counts as its Artisan Point total for wield restrictions and any effect that checks Artisan Points. A Hunter Arm's Forge Rating is equal to the number of Modification Points currently installed on it; you may always wield your own Hunter Arm regardless of Forge Rating.

Durability: Firing a Ballistic Weapon does not generate WDP by itself. It still uses normal Durability, Damage Level and WDP rules when struck, damaged or stressed by another effect.

Ballistic Attacks are still subject to the normal ranged-defense rules. A bullet does not become unblockable or unparryable merely because it is a bullet.

Ballistic Arts are exclusive Ballistic Corps Weapon Jutsu. For requirements, XP costs and effects that care about Jutsu type, they count as Bukijutsu. Unless a technique says otherwise, a Ballistic Art requires a Ballistic Weapon and cannot be used with an ordinary weapon.

### The Hunter Arm
{:#the-hunter-arm}

You begin with one custom Hunter Arm at no Ryo cost and three Standard Loads. Choose one Frame and spend 4 Modification Points on Ballistic Modifications. Your Hunter Arm is always considered a Ballistic Weapon and a Firearm.

Modification Points are not Artisan Points. They do not count toward normal Weapon Crafting prerequisites or tier requirements. The modifications below may only be installed on your Hunter Arm unless another rule explicitly allows otherwise.

You may redesign any number of your Hunter Arm's Modifications during a Week's Rest by spending 1 Downtime Slot at the Ballistics Annex or an equivalent workshop.

You may also change its Frame during a Week's Rest by spending 1 Downtime Slot and 10,000 Ryo in replacement parts. A Frame change may include a full redesign of its Modifications as part of the same action.

If your Hunter Arm is permanently lost or destroyed, a replacement chassis costs 20,000 Ryo. Its previous Modifications are restored after spending 1 Downtime Slot rebuilding the weapon.

### Loaded for the Hunt
{:#loaded-for-the-hunt}

Ballistic Corps hunters are trained to treat ammunition choice and firing position as part of the attack rather than bookkeeping between attacks.

Immediately after one of your Ballistic Attacks fully resolves, choose one:

- Cycle \- Exchange the currently loaded magazine for another compatible Load you are carrying. Preserve the remaining ammunition in both magazines.  
- Set \- Become Braced if you have not voluntarily moved since the beginning of the triggering Attack.  
- Reposition \- Move up to 2 yards without Speed or AP. Heavy or Siege weapons cannot choose Reposition. This movement cannot pass through creatures or ignore terrain or hazards.

### Corps Refit
{:#corps-refit-2}

At Trailhand or 2,500 total XP, whichever comes first, the Ballistics Annex rebuilds your Hunter Arm around the hunter you have become.

Gain 4 additional Modification Points. These points may be redesigned normally with the rest of your weapon.

## Ballistic Weapon Rules
{:#ballistic-weapon-rules}

### Report
{:#report}

Unless a weapon or Modification says otherwise, firing a Ballistic Weapon immediately ends ordinary Stealth and creates an obvious report. Spent cases, darts, sabots, scorch marks and seal residue may be tracked or studied normally.

### Magazine (X)
{:#magazine-x}

The weapon may make X normal attacks before it is empty. A normal Attack consumes 1 ammunition. Burst and specialist effects may consume more.

### Reload (X)
{:#reload-x}

Reloading is a Speed X Utility action with no additional Stamina cost. You must have a compatible Load available. A Load refills the weapon to its full Magazine value.

Removing a partially used magazine does not destroy the remaining ammunition. Track it separately until used again.

### Brace
{:#brace}

Brace is a Speed 5 Utility action. You remain Braced until you voluntarily move or are forcibly moved. Dodging also breaks your brace. Effects may alter how easily you lose Braced.

#### Burst
{:#burst}

When declaring a Ballistic Attack with a Burst weapon, you may consume 3 ammunition. The Attack gains \+3 Damage Dice, \+2 Stamina and \+1 Base Speed. Burst remains one Attack and may only modify an Attack once.

#### Full Auto
{:#full-auto}

When a Ballistic Weapon with the Burst property gains it again, the weapon instead gains access to this special Variable Speed Action — Full Auto: \[Variable Speed, Variable Stamina, Attack, Ballistic\].

When you declare it, choose a value of X between 2 and the number of complete 3-Round Bursts remaining in the Weapon’s Magazine, to a maximum of 5\. Consume 3 × X Ammunition.

The Action’s Speed becomes the Weapon’s Base Speed \+ X. The Action’s Stamina becomes the Weapon’s normal Stamina \+ (2 × X).

Draw a cone, with you as the point of Origin, up to your Weapon’s Range in any direction. Choose up to X creatures within that cone as possible targets. Make one Attack Roll against each chosen creature with a \-2 Accuracy Modifier. Each creature must defend against their Attack separately.

On a hit, deal the Weapon’s normal Damage \+ X + ⌈X / 2⌉ Damage Dice \+ BDB.

For the purposes of effects that care about repeated hits, such as Maintaining Handseals, Suppression, Concentration or similar effects, a creature hit by Full Auto counts as having been hit X times. These additional counted hits do not deal additional Damage, cannot independently Wound, and cannot trigger additional Damage effects.

**Simplification:**

- *a \= Weapon Base Speed || b \= Weapon Magazine Size || c \= Weapon Damage Dice || d \= Damage Bonus || e \= Weapon Stamina || X \= Chosen Value of X*


  Ammo \= 3X || Speed \= a \+ X || Stamina \= e \+ 2X || Targets \= X || Accuracy \= \-2 || Damage \= (c \+ X + ⌈X / 2⌉) Dice \+ d || Hit Count \= X

#### Heavy
{:#heavy}

Firing a Heavy weapon while not Braced gives the Attack \-1 Accuracy and \+2 Stamina.

#### Siege
{:#siege}

A Siege weapon must be Braced before it can declare an Attack. If its wielder is moved after declaring the Attack but before it resolves, the Attack is cancelled.

#### Scoped
{:#scoped}

A Scoped weapon gains the Aim action: \[Speed 5, Utility\]. Your next Ballistic Attack with that weapon before you move gains \+1 Accuracy, then consumes the Aim bonus.

#### Scatter Frame
{:#scatter-frame}

The weapon can safely chamber Scatter Loads.

#### Chakra-Driven
{:#chakra-driven}

When declaring an Attack, you may increase that attack’s cost by \+5 Chakra to use the higher of DEX / 24 or CHA / 24 as BDB. The Attack counts as chakra-enhanced wherever that distinction matters. Chakra-Driven weapons can safely chamber Elemental Loads.

## Ballistic Modifications
{:#ballistic-modifications}

### 1 Point Modifications
{:#1-point-modifications}

#### Extended Magazine
{:#extended-magazine}

Increase Magazine by 2\. May be taken twice.

#### Long Barrel
{:#long-barrel}

Increase Range by 5 yards, or by 10 yards on a Heavy Bowgun. Limit 1\.

#### Quick Feed
{:#quick-feed}

Reduce Reload by 1\. May be taken twice, to a minimum of half the Frame's original Reload.

#### Reinforced Receiver
{:#reinforced-receiver}

Increase Durability by 20%. Limit 1\.

#### Hunter Scope
{:#hunter-scope}

Gain the Scoped property. Limit 1\.

#### Balanced Stock
{:#balanced-stock}

Improve the weapon's Parry modifier by \+1. Limit 1\.

#### Specialist Feed
{:#specialist-feed}

Gain compatibility with one of Pierce, Slicing, Sticky, Tranq or Tracking Dye Loads. May be taken multiple times, choosing a different Load each time. This cannot grant Scatter, Elemental or Wyvern compatibility.

#### Quick Ejector
{:#quick-ejector}

When the weapon is empty, reduce its Reload by 1 after all other modifiers, to a minimum of 3\. Limit 1\.

#### Recoil Pad
{:#recoil-pad}

If the weapon has Heavy, its unbraced Stamina surcharge is \+1 instead of \+2. If it does not have Heavy, reduce forced movement caused by firing your own Ballistic Arts by 1 yard. Limit 1\.

#### Low-Profile Optics
{:#low-profile-optics}

Requires Scoped. Moving up to 1 yard after you Aim does not remove that Aim bonus. Moving farther still removes it normally. Limit 1\.

#### Sealed Magazine
{:#sealed-magazine}

Choose one compatible ammunition type when installed. Limit 1\.

#### Snap Sling
{:#snap-sling}

Drawing this weapon as part of a Ballistic Attack does not increase that Attack's Speed. Heavy and Siege weapons cannot benefit. Limit 1\.

#### Blast Shroud
{:#blast-shroud}

Firing still ends ordinary Stealth and creates a report, but creatures more than 20 yards away cannot determine your exact firing position from sound alone. Scatter Loads and Wyvern Shells cannot benefit. Limit 1\.

### 2 Point Modifications
{:#2-point-modifications}

#### Scatter Chamber
{:#scatter-chamber}

Gain the Scatter Frame property and compatibility with Scatter Loads. Limit 1\.

#### Chakra Coil
{:#chakra-coil}

Gain the Chakra-Driven property and compatibility with Elemental Loads. Limit 1\.

#### Burst Mechanism
{:#burst-mechanism}

Gain the Burst property. Limit 2\.

#### Recoil Brake
{:#recoil-brake}

If the weapon has Heavy, remove the \-1 Accuracy penalty for firing while unbraced. The additional \+2 Stamina remains. If the weapon does not have Heavy, reduce forced movement caused by firing your own Ballistic Arts by 2 yards. Limit 1\.

#### Tracking Assembly
{:#tracking-assembly}

Tracking Dye Loads fired from this weapon deal 2 Damage Dice instead of 1 and may add BDB normally. Limit 1\.

#### Precision Barrel
{:#precision-barrel}

Requires Scoped. A Ballistic Attack that consumes your Aim bonus gains \+1 Damage Die. Limit 1\.

#### Short Barrel
{:#short-barrel}

Reduce the weapon's listed Range by 25%, rounded normally, and reduce its Base Speed by 1\. Cannot be combined with Long Barrel. Limit 1\.

#### Overpressure Chamber
{:#overpressure-chamber}

Increase the weapon's Damage by \+1 Damage Die. All Ballistic Attacks with it gain \+2 Stamina and \+1 Delay. Limit 1\.

#### Choke Assembly
{:#choke-assembly}

Requires Scatter Frame. Scatter Loads gain their close-range bonus at two-thirds of the weapon's listed Range or closer instead of half Range. Limit 1\.

#### Gyro Stabilizer
{:#gyro-stabilizer}

While Braced, forced movement of 2 yards or less does not remove Braced from you. A Siege Attack is still cancelled if you are moved before it resolves. Limit 1\.

#### Capacitor Seal
{:#capacitor-seal}

Requires Chakra-Driven. When you pay Chakra to use Chakra-Driven or an Elemental Load, reduce the total Chakra paid for that Attack by 2, to a minimum of 1\. Limit 1\.

#### Quick Brace Assembly
{:#quick-brace-assembly}

Reduce the Speed of Brace by 1, to a minimum of 3\. Limit 1\.

#### Braced Loader
{:#braced-loader}

While you are Braced, reduce this weapon's Reload by 1, to a minimum of 3\. Limit 1\.

#### Lightened Chassis
{:#lightened-chassis}

Reduce Base Speed by 1\. Increase the Stamina of all Ballistic Attacks with this weapon by 2 and reduce its Durability by 10%. Cannot be combined with Counterweighted Chassis. Limit 1\.

#### Counterweighted Chassis
{:#counterweighted-chassis}

Reduce the Stamina of all Ballistic Attacks with this weapon by 2\. Increase Base Speed by 1 and increase its Durability by 10%. Cannot be combined with Lightened Chassis. Limit 1\.

### 3 Point Modifications
{:#3-point-modifications}

#### Dual Feed
{:#dual-feed}

The weapon can hold two compatible Loads at once. Each feed has its own remaining ammunition, up to the weapon's normal Magazine value. Choose which feed is used whenever you Attack. Reloading one feed does not reload the other. Limit 1\.

#### Guard Plate
{:#guard-plate}

Requires Heavy Bowgun Frame.

The weapon may use the normal Weapon Block action. While Braced, improve the weapon's Block reduction by 10%. Limit 1\.

#### Wyvern Chamber
{:#wyvern-chamber}

Requires Heavy Bowgun Frame.

Gain compatibility with Wyvern Shells. Limit 1\.

#### Rotary Action
{:#rotary-action}

Requires Burst.

Burst consumes 2 ammunition instead of 3\. Increase the weapon's Reload by 1\. Limit 1\.

#### Heavy Receiver
{:#heavy-receiver}

Requires a weapon without Heavy. Gain the Heavy property, \+1 Damage Die and \+10 Durability. Increase Base Speed by 1 and Stamina by 2\. Limit 1\.

#### Universal Chamber
{:#universal-chamber}

Gain compatibility with Pierce, Slicing, Sticky, Tranq and Tracking Dye Loads. This does not grant Scatter, Elemental or Wyvern compatibility. Limit 1\.

#### Buckler Housing
{:#buckler-housing}

The weapon may use the normal Weapon Block action. This grants no additional Block reduction by itself. Limit 1\.

#### Accelerator Coil
{:#accelerator-coil}

Requires Chakra-Driven. Whenever you activate Chakra-Driven, you may pay 2 additional Chakra. If you do, that Attack gains \+1 Damage Die and \+5 yards Range. Limit 1\.

#### Burst Regulator
{:#burst-regulator}

Requires Burst. When you use Burst, it adds \+4 Damage Dice instead of \+3, but its additional Stamina becomes \+4 and its additional Base Speed becomes \+2. Limit 1\.

#### High-Capacity Drum
{:#high-capacity-drum}

Increase Magazine by 50%, rounded up. Increase Reload by 2\. Limit 1\.

#### Dampened Action
{:#dampened-action}

Reduce the weapon's Delay by 1, to a minimum of 2\. All Ballistic Attacks with it gain \+2 Stamina. Limit 1\.

#### Tripod Mount
{:#tripod-mount}

Requires Heavy or Siege. Brace has Speed 3 with this weapon. While Braced, increase its listed Range by 5 yards. Limit 1\.

### 4 Modifications
{:#4-modifications}

These modifications require 2,500 total XP.

#### Siege Conversion
{:#siege-conversion}

Requires Heavy Bowgun Frame.

Gain the Siege property. Increase Damage by \+2 Damage Dice and Range by 10 yards. Increase Base Speed by 2\. Magazine becomes 1 and Reload increases by 3\. Limit 1\.

#### Monster Bore
{:#monster-bore}

When a Ballistic Attack from this weapon damages Armor Durability, a physical Barrier, or a destructible monster part, deal an additional 25% damage to that object's Durability. Limit 1\.

#### Belt Feed
{:#belt-feed}

Requires Burst. Double the weapon's Magazine and increase Reload by 3\. The weapon gains Heavy if it does not already have it. Limit 1\.

#### Full-Auto Compensator
{:#full-auto-compensator}

Requires Full Auto. Full Auto adds X Stamina instead of 2 × X Stamina. Its ammunition cost, Accuracy penalty, Speed and damage are unchanged. Limit 1\.

#### Servo Brace
{:#servo-brace}

Requires Heavy or Siege. While Braced, Ballistic Attacks with this weapon gain Final Speed \-1 and \-2 Stamina. Limit 1\.

#### Auto-Sealing Ejector
{:#auto-sealing-ejector}

When an Attack leaves this weapon empty, your next Reload with it before the end of your next Action gains Final Speed \-2. Limit 1\.

#### Anti-Materiel Rifling
{:#anti-materiel-rifling}

Pierce Loads fired from this weapon no longer reduce the Attack by 1 Damage Die. All of their other effects remain unchanged. Limit 1\.

#### Elemental Resonator
{:#elemental-resonator}

Requires Chakra-Driven. An Elemental Load fired from this weapon gains \+1 Damage Die without increasing its Chakra cost. Limit 1\.

#### Self-Leveling Mount
{:#self-leveling-mount}

Requires Heavy or Siege. Dodging does not remove Braced from you. Any movement caused by that Dodge still removes Braced normally if it moves you. Limit 1\.

### 5 Point Modifications
{:#5-point-modifications}

These modifications require 4,000 total XP.

#### Full-Auto Governor
{:#full-auto-governor}

Requires Full Auto and 4,000 total XP. Increase Full Auto's maximum X from 5 to 6\. You must still possess enough ammunition to pay 3 × X. Limit 1\.

#### Autoloader Core
{:#autoloader-core}

Requires 4,000 total XP. When an Attack empties this weapon, you may consume one compatible Load after the Attack resolves to refill the weapon to half its Magazine, rounded up. This counts as a Reload but has no Speed. It cannot trigger from a Wyvern Shell or Wyvern Execution. Limit 1\.

#### Anti-Materiel Conversion
{:#anti-materiel-conversion}

Requires 4,000 total XP and a non-Small Frame. Gain Heavy and \+2 Damage Dice. Increase Base Speed by 2, Stamina by 3 and Reload by 2\. Reduce Magazine by 50%, rounded up. Limit 1\.

#### Elemental Reactor
{:#elemental-reactor}

Requires 4,000 total XP and Chakra-Driven. When firing an Elemental Load, you may increase the CE cost by 3 additional Chakra to add \+2 Damage Dice to the Attack. Limit 1\.

#### Fortress Housing
{:#fortress-housing}

Requires 4,000 total XP and Buckler Housing or Guard Plate. While Braced, improve this weapon's Block reduction by 15%. This replaces, rather than stacks with, the bonus from Guard Plate. Limit 1\.

### 6 Point Modifications
{:#6-point-modifications}

These modifications require 5,000 total XP.

#### Rail Conversion
{:#rail-conversion}

Requires 5,000 total XP, Chakra-Driven and Scoped. When a Ballistic Attack consumes your Aim bonus, you may add \+8 Chakra Cost. That Attack gains \+2 Damage Dice and \+10 yards Range, but also \+1 Base Speed. Limit 1\.

#### Cyclone Feed
{:#cyclone-feed}

Requires 5,000 total XP and Full Auto. For Full Auto only, each point of X consumes 2 ammunition instead of 3, and the maximum available X is determined from complete 2-round segments. Increase this weapon's Reload by 2\. Limit 1\.

#### Siege Stabilizer
{:#siege-stabilizer}

Requires 5,000 total XP and Siege. If you are forcibly moved 2 yards or less after declaring a Siege Attack, the Attack is not cancelled. You lose Braced after it resolves. Voluntary movement still cancels the Attack. Limit 1\.

#### Titan Receiver
{:#titan-receiver}

Requires 5,000 total XP and Heavy. Increase Damage by \+2 Damage Dice and Durability by 20\. Increase Base Speed by 2, Stamina by 3 and Reload by 1\. Limit 1\.

---

## Ballistic Ammunition
{:#ballistic-ammunition}

A weapon may only load ammunition listed in its Compatible Ammo line or granted by one of its Modifications. A Load fills one magazine regardless of the weapon's exact Magazine value; this is an abstraction for play.

### Standard Load
{:#standard-load}

Cost: 800 Ryo per Load

Reliable jacketed shot, hardened darts, ceramic slugs or whatever the weapon was built to throw. No special modifier.

### Pierce Load
{:#pierce-load}

Cost: 1,200 Ryo per Load

Reduce the Attack by 1 Damage Die. Gain \+5% Wounding and deal \+50% damage to Armor Durability, physical Barriers and objects.

### Scatter Load
{:#scatter-load}

Cost: 1,000 Ryo per Load

Requires Scatter Frame.

At half the weapon's listed Range or closer, add \+3 Damage Dice. Beyond half Range, instead suffer \-1 Accuracy. Scatter does not change the number of creatures hit.

### Slicing Load
{:#slicing-load}

Cost: 1,500 Ryo per Load

Reduce the Attack by 1 Damage Die, change its damage type to Slashing, and gain \+5% Wounding.

### Sticky Load
{:#sticky-load}

Cost: 1,800 Ryo per Load

On a hit, deal no immediate weapon damage. Five IC later the lodged charge detonates for half the weapon's normal Damage Dice, rounded up, using the same die size.

Sticky damage gains no BDB and cannot Wound by itself, but deals double damage to Armor Durability, objects and physical Barriers. You may have only one Sticky charge lodged in the same target at a time.

### Elemental Load
{:#elemental-load}

Cost: 2,000 Ryo per Load

Requires Chakra-Driven.

Add \+5 CE to the cost of your attack. Choose the elemental nature the Load was prepared for. The Attack changes to that damage type and may use CHA / 24 instead of DEX / 24 for BDB. This does not automatically apply an elemental condition.

### Tranq Load
{:#tranq-load}

Cost: 1,000 Ryo per Load

Reduce the Attack by 2 Damage Dice and make it Non-Lethal. Against a Hunt target with an explicit capture or tranquilizer condition, a successful hit counts as one valid tranquilizer application. The monster profile determines the remaining requirements.

### Tracking Dye Load
{:#tracking-dye-load}

Cost: 600 Ryo per Load

The Attack deals only 1 Damage Die and gains no BDB. On hit, mark the target with bright alchemical dye and chakra-reactive scent for the rest of the scene. Track or Survival rolls made specifically to follow that creature gain \+2 while the mark remains.

### Wyvern Shell
{:#wyvern-shell}

Cost: 3,000 Ryo per Shell

Requires Heavy or Siege and explicit Wyvern compatibility.

The Attack gains \+6 Damage Dice, \+5 Base Speed, \+5 Stamina and \+10% Wounding. Firing a Wyvern Shell empties the current magazine regardless of remaining ammunition. The weapon cannot begin Reloading until 5 IC after the Attack resolves.

---

## Talent: Personal Pattern
{:#talent-personal-pattern}

The oldest Ballistic Corps hunters say that every great gun eventually stops being a model and becomes a name. Parts are replaced until nothing remains of the original weapon except the decisions that shaped it. The Personal Pattern talent path represents that relationship: not a mystical soul-bond, but years of field notes, custom tolerances, monster parts, favourite ammunition and tiny adjustments that make a weapon function properly for exactly one hunter.

### Named Pattern
{:#named-pattern}

Type: Talent

Requires: Ballistic Corps, 1,000 total XP, and at least 1 IC month using the same Hunter Arm  
Effects

Choose one of your Hunter Arms. It becomes your Named Arm.

Your Named Arm gains 2 additional Modification Points. These points follow all normal Modification rules.

Only you can benefit from Modification Points or Signature Mechanisms granted by this Talent Path. In another character's hands, the weapon functions as though those Talent features were absent.

You may move your Named Pattern to another Hunter Arm during a Week's Rest by spending 2 Downtime Slots rebuilding and tuning the new weapon. You may only have one Named Arm unless you possess Twin Pattern.

### Signature Pattern
{:#signature-pattern}

Type: Talent

Requires: Named Pattern, 2,000 total XP

Effects

Choose one Signature Mechanism for your Named Arm. You gain its Tier I effect. If a Signature has a property or Frame requirement, your Named Arm must meet it to select or benefit from that Signature.

Changing your Signature Mechanism requires 2 Downtime Slots during a Week's Rest.

#### Quickdraw Mechanism
{:#quickdraw-mechanism}

**Tier I — The first Ballistic Attack you make after drawing or Reloading your Named Arm gains Final Speed \-1.**

**Tier II** — The same Attack gains Final Speed \-2 instead.

#### Predator Glass
{:#predator-glass}

**Requires:** Scoped.

**Tier I** — An Attack that consumes your Aim bonus also gains \+5% Wounding.

**Tier II** — You may Aim twice before firing. The second Aim grants a second \+1 Accuracy. Moving removes both bonuses.

#### Three-Round Seal
{:#three-round-seal}

**Requires:** Burst.

**Tier I** — Burst adds \+4 Damage Dice instead of \+3.

**Tier II — If your previous Ballistic Attack was a Burst against the same target, the new Burst Attack also gains \+1 Accuracy. Moving, Reloading, or changing targets breaks the pattern.**

#### Fangburst Chamber
{:#fangburst-chamber}

**Requires:** Scatter Frame.

**Tier I** — At half Range or closer, Scatter ammunition adds \+4 Damage Dice instead of \+3, but the Attack gains \+1 Delay.

**Tier II — Ignore that \+1 Delay.**

#### Breach Teeth
{:#breach-teeth}

**Tier I** — Ballistic Attacks with the Named Arm deal \+50% damage to Armor Durability, physical Barriers and objects.

**Tier II** — Increase this bonus to \+100%.

#### Anchored Spine
{:#anchored-spine}

**Requires:** Heavy or Siege.

**Tier I** — If you have remained Braced and have not moved for at least 10 IC, Standard and Pierce Attacks gain \+1 Damage Die.

**Tier II** — They gain \+2 Damage Dice instead.

#### Stormrail Coil
{:#stormrail-coil}

**Requires:** Chakra-Driven.

**Tier I** — Elemental Loads fired from the Named Arm cost only 3 Chakra to activate.

**Tier II** — Choose one elemental nature you possess an Affinity for. Elemental Loads prepared for that nature may use CHA / 20 instead of CHA / 24 for BDB.

### Master Pattern
{:#master-pattern}

Type: Talent

Requires: Signature Pattern, 3,000 total XP

Effects

Your Named Arm gains 2 additional Modification Points.

Your chosen Signature Mechanism improves to Tier II.

Additionally, when your Named Arm is destroyed or permanently lost, you retain its Pattern. Rebuilding it costs the normal replacement chassis cost and one Downtime Slot, but all Talent-granted Modification Points and Signature features return with the rebuilt weapon.

### Twin Pattern
{:#twin-pattern}

Type: Talent

Requires: Master Pattern, 4,000 total XP

Effects

You have learned to maintain two personal weapons to the same impossible tolerances.

You may designate a second Hunter Arm as a Named Arm. It gains all benefits of Named Pattern and Master Pattern, including their 4 total bonus Modification Points.

Choose a Signature Mechanism for the second weapon separately. It begins at Tier II and must meet its own requirements.

Any once-per-scene effect granted by this Talent Path is shared between both Named Arms. Switching weapons cannot reset or duplicate that use.

Twin Pattern does not reduce the normal hands, drawing, Reload, ammunition, or Multi-Attack requirements for using two weapons.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-37}

### Gunner's Doctrine
{:#gunner-s-doctrine}

Type: Ability

Cost: 10

Limit: 1

**Effects**

Choose one Doctrine. You may change Doctrine with one Downtime Slot during a Week's Rest.

Skirmisher \- After you use Reposition, your next Ballistic Attack before the end of your next Action gains Final Speed \-1. You cannot benefit from this while Braced.

Marksman \- Aim has Speed 4 instead of 5\. An Attack that consumes your Aim bonus also gains \+5% Wounding.

Artillery \- Brace has Speed 3 instead of 5\. Forced movement of less than 3 yards does not remove Braced from you, although a Siege Attack is still cancelled if you are moved before it resolves.

### Ammunition Savant
{:#ammunition-savant}

Type: Ability

Cost: 10

Limit: 1

**Effects**

When you fire a non-Standard Load, choose one benefit:

● Ignore 1 Damage Die reduction imposed by that ammunition.

● Reduce additional Stamina imposed by that ammunition by 2\.

● Reduce additional Base Speed imposed by that ammunition by 1\.

Ammunition Savant cannot remove a restriction, compatibility requirement, ammunition cost, delayed effect or post-shot lockout.

### Field Loader
{:#field-loader}

Type: Ability

Cost: 12

Limit: 1

Requires: Ammunition Savant

**Effects**

Whenever an Attack leaves your Ballistic Weapon empty, your next Reload with that weapon before the end of your next Action gains Final Speed \-2.

Additionally, Cycle from Loaded for the Hunt may exchange to a partially used magazine or a fresh Load without requiring you to declare which one until Cycle resolves.

### Master Gunsmith
{:#master-gunsmith}

Type: Ability

Cost: 15

Limit: 1

Requires: 2,500 total XP, Gunner's Doctrine

**Effects**

Gain 3 additional Modification Points for your Hunter Arm.

Once per Week's Rest, you may redesign your Hunter Arm's Modifications without spending a Downtime Slot if you have access to the Ballistics Annex or an equivalent workshop. Changing Frame still follows its normal cost.

{:#jutsu-35}

{:#recoil-step}

Stamina: 10

Speed: \+2
