**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Environmental

**Effects**

Erupt the battlefield beneath the target area, dealing 12d12 damage. For 30 IC the area remains an extreme version of your Expression: Magma inflicts 3d10 Energy on anyone moving through 3+ yards of it; Quicklime applies Immobilization 2 each 5 IC spent within; Rubber grants you control over knockback, allowing you to redirect any forced movement inside the area by up to 90 degrees.

# Yuki
{:#yuki}

***CANON CLAN \- RARE***

*Yuki families in Haven Ember preserve Hyōton as a rare Water-and-Wind inheritance. Ember Corps users are taught to think in geometry: mirrors, frozen surfaces, projectiles, and temporary structures that alter sightlines and movement until the enemy is fighting the shape of the ice instead of the person who made it.*

**Starting Features**

* Yuki begin with Elemental Aptitude for Suiton and Fuuton, and Elemental Affinity: Hyōton, which includes Suiton and Fuuton.

## Passives
{:#passives-24}

### Hyouton - Ice Release
{:#hyouton-ice-release}

Yuki have the ability to use one of the coolest advanced chakra natures: ice release. This is obtained through a combination of water chakra and wind chakra. Ice Release combines the flexibility of water with the penetrating power of wind. You can convert any Suiton or Fuuton technique into a Hyouton technique, with the following effects:

* \+8 Chakra cost.

* Blocking reduces the damage from Hyouton techniques by 25%

* Unless an individual jutsu specifies otherwise, Hyouton inflicts Piercing wounds.

* Hyouton's damage for the purpose of wounding is considered 15% higher, which stacks additively with any existing increases on converted jutsu (50%+15% \= 65%).

* When you would inflict a wound with a Hyouton technique, you may instead decide not to, and inflict a “Permanent” Freeze to the limb the wound affected. Each leg affected this way can hold up to Immob 3, each Hand may hold up to Immob 3, for a total of 12 Immob applied to a target. Applied to the head or torso, this applies Suffocation without a limit. When the wound is applied, compared the location to the relevant Freeze effect (Immob for Legs and Arms, Suffocation for Torso and Head), and apply a severity equal to the wound category (Major=1,Severe=2,Critical=3). The Legs can only ever give a total of 6 Immob, as well as the arms. The Suffocation applied this way can fade naturally, but the Frozen Arms and Leg immob remains until the creature spends atleast an hour in a hot area thawing. 

* You can forgo rolling any or all damage dice when using a Hyouton Technique. Instead, treat those dice as though they rolled their average result, rounded down (IE; a d4 would be a 2, a D6 a 3…). You must make the decision of how many dice to convert before you roll damage.

* Fuuton converted into Hyouton may not be used with a fan, and gains a gallon-of-water requirement equal to a third of its chakra cost.

* Gallons of Ice may be used instead of Gallons of Water to pay any part of the Jutsu’s Cost.

* Anything that creates Gallons of Water instead creates Gallons of Ice.

* Hyouton that use Ice to pay for its cost, or that create Ice, that hit an opponent apply an Immobilize penalty based on its Rank (E \= 1, D \= 2, C \= 3, B \= 4, A \= 5). This penalty fades at a rate of 1 per 10 IC since the opponent was last hit with a Hyouton Jutsu. This only applies once per casting, even on persistent damage effects. 

* If "Water-Cutting Blade" or “Fan of Wind” is turned into a Hyouton Jutsu, then it gains the following Upgrade: (3) Your weapon attacks count as Hyouton Jutsu for the purpose of fading Immobilize penalties.

### Living Ice
{:#living-ice}

As a Speed 1 Action, freeze or liquefy up to 2 gallons of water you can see within CHA/2 yards. This cannot directly damage a creature or destroy an attended object. 

While within 2 yards of Ice you created, gain \+2 Dodge and \+20% movement. When you enter Ice you created, you can move an additional 3 yards without increasing your IC. Your own Ice never imposes difficult terrain or Immobilization on you unless a technique explicitly says it does.

Hyōton conversion costs are reduced by 2 Chakra, minimum \+1. Ice structures you create gain 25% more HP and Durability.

### Suitenhoufukyuu - Water Sky Convergence
{:#suitenhoufukyuu-water-sky-convergence}

You can use any water scattered about the battlefield to fuel your Suiton or Hyouton techniques, even if it’s normally inaccessible. This does allow you to seize control of someone else's water or techniques. Even if it's in a container, or is infused with someone's chakra, it's not off limits

You can take a Speed X action, with a Chakra cost of 5+2\*X, to freeze or liquify (CHA/30, minimum 1)\*X gallons of water on the battlefield. Ice may not be used in Suiton techniques; it can, however, be used to perform Hyouton (including Suiton jutsu which you have converted into Hyouton).

You can perform Suiton (but not Hyouton) jutsu without hand seals, by adding (the base Seal Speed \- CHA/10)/2, rounded up, to the action's base Speed. Normal alterations to Seal Speed (such as from Ninjutsu Specialist) do not apply to this ability.

There are even legends of Yuki once being able to drop rivers on people.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-23}

*No Heritage-specific purchasable abilities.*

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-22}

**D-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Sensatsu Suishou - Secret Technique: Thousand Flying Water Needles
{:#hijutsu-sensatsu-suishou-secret-technique-thousand-flying-water-needles}

**Type:** Suiton Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 16  
**Damage:** (CHA/8)d10  
**Range:** CHA/3  
**Speed:** 12  
**Seal Speed:** 12  
Gallon: 1 per 1d10

**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Piercing

**Effects**

This technique's damage is considered 25% higher for the purpose of inflicting wounds, and it always inflicts Piercing wounds. If it wounds an enemy, it also applies one wound of the next-lower rank.

**C-Rank**

### Hijutsu: Makyo Hyosho - Secret Technique: Demonic Mirror Ice Crystals
{:#hijutsu-makyo-hyosho-secret-technique-demonic-mirror-ice-crystals}

**Type:** Hyoton Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 15+X/2  
**Upkeep:** X/2  
**Range:** CHA/4  
**Speed:** 12  
**Seal Speed:** 10+X/2  
**Requires:** X\*2 Gallons of Water and/or Ice in any combination  
**Tags:** Utility, Environmental

**Effects**

You create X Demonic Ice Mirrors. These remain stationary on the battlefield, even floating mid air. They are one yard wide, two yards tall, and a few inches thick. They may be attacked, but you must deal CHA\*2 Damage in a single attack to destroy one (otherwise they just regenerate). While within 1 yard of a Mirror you can step into it and out any other mirror at will. Practically speaking, this means that you can go from one mirror to any other mirror using only 1 yard of movement, and you can draw range and line of sight from any of the other mirrors. This includes taking the Observe action (which is done from every Mirror) and things like Dodge, where you can calculate your movement versus an AoE as beginning at any Mirror.

If X is 20 or higher, this Jutsu forms a cage around an area. Stop tracking individual Ice Mirrors. Instead note a space on the battlefield as being the Cage, which has a Radius of up to your CHA/4 Yards. While within this area, the user steps into the Mirrors and begins bouncing between the Mirrors too quickly for the eye to see. This grants the following benefits:

* The user enters Level 1 Stealth, and may perform the Hide Action without any further justification.

* You can perform any form of attack while in Stealth without breaking that Stealth. The attack lowers the Stealth TN as though it were a Ranged Weapon attack.

* Until an enemy defeats the user's Stealth, that enemy suffers \-4 Accuracy on attacks against the user and cannot make Called Shots against them. An Area attack that covers the entire cage ignores this penalty.

* Once per 10 IC, when a creature attempts to leave the cage, you can convert their next non-Interrupt Action into a single-target Attack with a final Speed of 10 or less and resolve it as an Interrupt against that creature. Pay all normal costs. After it resolves, set the user's next IC no earlier than the current IC plus that Attack's final Speed. On a full hit that deals damage, the creature's movement ends at the edge of the cage. This is the user's next Action, not a free additional attack.

* You can consider themselves to be anywhere within the cage when they take an action.

* An enemy who has found you (usually by beating your Stealth TN) is tracking your movements. They may attack you normally, treating you as being anywhere inside of the Cage when they attack. If you Hide again, this penalty is lost until the opponent again finds you.

* Mirrors that form the cage reform instantly if they are destroyed.

This user cannot benefit from this Jutsu, either the individual mirrors or the cage, if they are Grabbed or incapable of movement. Using this Jutsu again removes all previously established Demonic Ice Mirrors, releasing their Upkeep appropriately.

### Hyōton: Hyōheki - Ice Dome
{:#hyoton-hyoheki-ice-dome}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 22  
**Speed:** 7  
**Seal Speed:** 12  
**Tags:** Interrupt, Utility

**Effects**

Raise an ice shell around yourself or a point within 10 yards. It has HP equal to CHA x 5 and DR 15\. It can intercept one incoming Attack when created. When destroyed, it leaves 6 gallons of usable Ice on the battlefield.

**B-Rank**

### Hyouton: Jisarenhyo - Ice Release: Earthen Consecutive Chains of Ice
{:#hyouton-jisarenhyo-ice-release-earthen-consecutive-chains-of-ice}

**Type:** Hyoton Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 28  
**Upkeep:** 6  
**Speed:** 14  
**Seal Speed:** 16  
**Tags:** Attack, Melee

**Effects**

On a hit, you gain Upkeep 6 and this jutsu applies Immobilization 5 that is lowered by 1 every 10 IC the opponent goes without being hit by a Hyouton. So long as the upkeep is maintained, the ice particles on their body freeze them whenever their chakra fluctuates. If the opponent attempts a Chakra roll, their immobilization penalty is maintained as though they were hit by a Hyouton Ninjutsu. Further, they lose (Immobilization Penalty from Hyouton \+ Chakra TN/5)% of their current Vitality (or HP, if Vitality is depleted) plus their current Immobilization Penalty from Hyouton as damage that cannot be reduced.

Creatures who can’t use Chakra that are affected by this jutsu freeze to death in 100 IC. The upkeep can be maintained for up to one day. If you can reduce the Seal Speed to 0, then you can apply this Jutsu with the slightest touch. Done covertly or out of combat, you can forgo the Immobilization penalty and apply the Upkeep portion of this Jutsu. If done covertly, the opponent may make an Awareness roll against your Espionage to realize what you have done.

### Hyōton: Sensatsu Kōri - Thousand Ice Needles
{:#hyoton-sensatsu-kori-thousand-ice-needles}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 32  
**Speed:** 15  
**Range:** 25  
**Area:** 8  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Projectile, Piercing

**Effects**

Create or consume up to 10 gallons of Ice. Deal 8d10 plus 1d10 per gallon used, maximum 18d10. Targets hit suffer Immobilization 2\. Against a target already suffering your Frostbite/Immobilization, this attack gains \+2 Accuracy.

### Makyō Reversal - Mirror Step
{:#makyo-reversal-mirror-step}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 20  
**Speed:** 5  
**Tags:** Interrupt, Movement  
**Requires:** At least two of your Demonic Ice Mirrors active

**Effects**

When targeted by an Attack, move instantly from your current position into or adjacent to another mirror within the technique's network. Treat this as a Dodge using Ninjutsu Accuracy. If successful, you can emerge from any mirror and gain \+2 Accuracy on your next Hyōton or Senbon attack before the end of your next Action.

<div id="heritage-up-3" class="clan-cost-banner"><span class="clan-cost-value">3 UP</span><span class="clan-cost-caption">HERITAGES <span class="clan-cost-count">04 ENTRIES</span></span></div>

# Karashi (Puppets)
{:#karashi-puppets}

***PUPPET HERITAGE***

*Karashi is Haven Ember’s name for a workshop lineage raised around puppet balance, chakra strings, hidden mechanisms, and the belief that a hunters’s reach should be limited by imagination rather than anatomy. Puppetry is not theirs alone, but Karashi characters begin inside the craft instead of approaching it from the outside.*

## Passives
{:#passives-15}

## Starting Features
{:#starting-features}

- Gain 3 ranks of Puppet Mastery at no XP cost. These ranks count as purchased for the cost of later ranks.

- Learn Kugutsu no Jutsu — Puppet Technique at no XP cost, ignoring its normal learning requirements.

- Construct Puppets up to your starting Collection Limit.

- You may begin with Elemental Affinity for Clan/General Taijutsu.

## Puppet Terms
{:#puppet-terms}

### User
{:#user}

The character controlling the Puppets.

### Collection
{:#collection}

The Puppets you currently have constructed. Puppets in your Collection are persistent named pieces of equipment with their own Frame, HP, Mounts, Attachments and installed equipment.

### Thread Limit (TL)
{:#thread-limit-tl}

The maximum number of Puppets you can have Active through Kugutsu no Jutsu at one time. Unused Thread Limit can instead become Heavy Threads under Concentrated Control.

### Heavy Thread
{:#heavy-thread}

An unused point of Thread Limit concentrated into a single Puppet under Concentrated Control.

### Collection Limit (CL)
{:#collection-limit-cl}

The maximum number of Puppets you can keep constructed at once. Your Collection Limit is twice your Thread Limit.

### Active Puppet
{:#active-puppet}

A Puppet currently connected to Kugutsu no Jutsu. Active Puppets have real positions on the battlefield and can be targeted normally.

### Primary Puppet
{:#primary-puppet}

One Active Puppet is your Primary. Ordinary Puppet Actions are performed through the Primary unless a rule explicitly uses a Support instead.

### Support Puppet
{:#support-puppet}

Every other Active Puppet is a Support. Supports have real positions, can be targeted and affected normally, and contribute through Support Routines and techniques that explicitly use them.

### Inactive Puppet
{:#inactive-puppet}

A Puppet in the field but not currently connected to Kugutsu no Jutsu. It cannot act or defend through your control and contributes no Routines. It remains a physical object at its location.

### Puppet Action
{:#puppet-action}

An Action you perform through a Puppet. It is still your Action, advances your IC normally and uses your resources unless a rule says otherwise.

### Frame Range
{:#frame-range}

The maximum distance over which a Frame can remain threaded. Puppet movement uses the normal Ember movement rules.

## Puppet Body
{:#puppet-body}

### Puppet HP
{:#puppet-hp}

Base Puppet HP:

50 \+ one-third of the User’s maximum Vitality, rounded down.

Its Frame then modifies that Base HP.

Damage remains on each individual Puppet when it changes between Primary and Support. At 0 HP, a Puppet becomes Disabled for the remainder of the scene, immediately becomes Inactive, and remains a physical object at its location. Recalculate Kugutsu no Jutsu’s Upkeep immediately.

Between scenes, ordinary repair returns Puppets to full HP and restores Disabled Puppets at no Ryo or Downtime cost. Replacing consumed or destroyed weapons, ammunition, poisons, explosives and other equipment follows the normal inventory rules.

### Construct Physiology
{:#construct-physiology}

Puppets are Constructs. They are immune to Genjutsu and effects that require a living body, functioning organs, blood, breathing, nerves or a biological mind. Damage to a Puppet never produces Wounds.

Physical effects that can affect objects—including Immobilization, Ignite, Knockback, Prone and Visibility—affect Puppets normally. Ignite deals half its normal damage to a Puppet.

Immobilization follows the normal Ember rules, with one exception: a Puppet at Immobilization 6 or greater cannot move from its current location. The normal numerical penalties continue to use the actual Immobilization severity.

### Malfunction
{:#malfunction}

When a single hostile Attack deals at least 50% of a Puppet’s maximum HP after mitigation, that Puppet Malfunctions after the Attack resolves.

A Malfunctioning Puppet suffers \-2 Accuracy, \-2 to Defensive Rolls and \-25% movement. A Puppet can have one Malfunction at a time. Field Refit can clear a Malfunction instead of repairing HP.

### Puppet Statistics
{:#puppet-statistics}

When a Puppet itself needs an Attribute:

- Use the User’s DEX as its DEX.

- Use the User’s AGI as its AGI.

- Use the User’s CHA in place of its STR and RES.

Use these substitutions only when the Puppet itself needs an Attribute. User-specific STR and RES effects continue to use the User’s own Attributes.

A Puppet uses the User’s Skill ranks when it is physically capable of the task and the User has enough information to direct it. Sensory checks from a Puppet’s position require a Sensor Mast or a similar effect.

Puppet Actions use the User’s AP, WP, Chakra, Fatigue and IC. Track each Puppet’s HP, position, statuses and spent equipment separately.

### Puppet Action Costs
{:#puppet-action-costs}

If a Puppet Action normally has a Stamina cost, treat that number as a Chakra cost instead and do not make the Stamina Exhaustion roll. Chakra costs remain Chakra costs normally.

A Puppet body may use Ninjutsu or Genjutsu only when a technique or Attachment explicitly permits it. Puppets cannot form handseals.

Puppets may use compatible Taijutsu and Bukijutsu the User knows when the Puppet has the required limbs, weapon, Mounts and equipment.

### Using Your Build
{:#using-your-build}

Puppet Actions are still the User’s Actions. Abilities, Uniques, Talents, Jutsu, weapon properties and crafted features that modify the declared Action, the mounted weapon, the relevant Taijutsu/Bukijutsu, or the roll being made apply normally when their requirements are met.

User-only bodily, sensory, Size, Wound, transformation, passive DR and personal movement effects remain on the User unless an effect explicitly transfers them.

Mounted weapons are real weapons. They keep their normal Speed, Stamina, Damage, Accuracy, Range or Reach, Delay, Tags, Durability, crafted properties and other rules. Mounted weapons are fixed to the Puppet for Disarm effects and remain valid targets for equipment damage.

## Puppet Action Economy
{:#puppet-action-economy}

### One Controller, One Turn
{:#one-controller-one-turn}

All Puppet Actions occur on the User’s IC.

On the User’s IC, the User declares one Action as normal. If it is a Puppet Action, the Action originates from the Primary Puppet and uses the Primary’s position, equipment and legal targets. It advances the User’s IC by its Final Speed.

Cooldowns, once-per-X-IC effects, Delay, Upkeep and similar timing are tracked from the User unless a rule explicitly says that an individual Puppet tracks something separately.

### Movement
{:#movement}

Frame Range is a control tether.

Active Puppets move using the normal Ember movement rules and must follow legal physical paths. A Primary can use ordinary movement Actions and the normal Move with an Attack options when legal.

Formation Shift — Variable Speed, Movement, Utility

Choose a Speed of at least 3\. Every Active Puppet may move the distance it could normally move during that many IC, following legal paths. This is one Action and advances the User’s IC by the chosen Speed. The User does not have to move.

When the User takes an ordinary Move Action with their own body, the Primary may also move up to the same distance along a legal path.

### Control Range
{:#control-range}

The Primary must remain within its Frame Range of the User.

A Support must remain within its own Frame Range of either the User or the Primary. A Support cannot anchor another Support.

An Active Puppet that ends outside legal Frame Range becomes Inactive immediately. Reconnect it using Kugutsu no Jutsu or another effect that explicitly restores the connection.

### Puppet Defenses
{:#puppet-defenses}

When an Active Puppet is targeted by an Attack, the User may declare any legal Defensive Interrupt through that Puppet. Resolve the Interrupt from the Puppet’s position using its substituted Attributes, equipment and applicable modifiers. Any Defensive Roll uses those values. Pay the Interrupt’s AP, Chakra and other costs from the User; its Final Speed advances the User’s IC normally.

When one Attack targets the User and/or multiple Active Puppets, each target may declare and resolve its own legal Defensive Interrupt. Pay every Interrupt’s costs. After all defenses against that Attack resolve, advance the User’s IC once using the highest Final Speed among those Defensive Interrupts.

## Primary, Supports and Formations
{:#primary-supports-and-formations}

### Choosing the Primary
{:#choosing-the-primary}

When Kugutsu no Jutsu is activated, choose one Active Puppet as Primary.

At the start of your IC, before declaring an Action, you may exchange Primary and Support when a rule such as Thread Weaving permits it. A Primary/Support exchange leaves both Puppets in their current positions unless the effect moves them.

### Support Puppets
{:#support-puppets}

Supports contribute through Routines, Formation Shift, defenses and explicit effects.

A Support:

- has a real position and can be targeted;

- can defend through the User as described above;

- does not take ordinary Actions;

- can move through Formation Shift and explicit effects;

- contributes one active Support Routine it knows;

- can be used by techniques such as Perfect Synchronization;

- stops contributing immediately if Disabled, Inactive or otherwise unable to meet that Routine’s requirements.

### Range Anchoring
{:#range-anchoring}

Whenever a Routine, Attack, Defensive Interrupt or technique originates from a Support, use that Support’s actual position for Range, Reach, Cover, Line of Sight, AoE, terrain and other positional rules.

### Formations
{:#formations}

While Kugutsu no Jutsu is active, use Assault Formation or Guard Formation unless Concentrated Control is active. Choose the Formation when Kugutsu no Jutsu is activated. Changing Formation or entering Concentrated Control is a Speed 3 Utility Action when legal.

### Assault Formation
{:#assault-formation}

Your strings are spread for pressure and overlapping attack angles.

Once per IC, when a damaging Puppet Attack through your Primary fully hits, add one of that Attack’s base Damage Dice. Assault Support Routines function.

### Guard Formation
{:#guard-formation}

Your formation collapses inward and the Primary stays close enough to physically protect you.

When entering Guard Formation, the Primary may immediately move up to the distance it could normally cover in 3 IC, ending within 5 yards of you. While Guard Formation is active, the Primary must remain within 5 yards of you, gains \+1 to its Defensive Rolls, and may use Take the Hit when you are targeted, following the normal Take the Hit rules. Guard Support Routines function.

\[Any\] Support Routines function in either Formation.

## Concentrated Control
{:#concentrated-control}

A master can draw every unused control string inward, driving the attention of an entire formation into a single masterpiece.

### Entering Concentrated Control
{:#entering-concentrated-control}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 3  
While Kugutsu no Jutsu is active and you control exactly one Active Puppet, you may enter Concentrated Control when Kugutsu no Jutsu is activated or as a Speed 3 Utility Action. Your only Active Puppet is your Primary.

Concentrated Control replaces Assault and Guard Formation while active. Support Routines apply only through effects that explicitly enable them, such as Lead Support. If another Puppet becomes Active, Concentrated Control ends immediately and you choose Assault or Guard Formation. If your Primary becomes Disabled or Inactive, Concentrated Control ends.

While Concentrated Control is active:

- Maintaining Kugutsu no Jutsu occupies one hand instead of both.

- Your Primary may use Take the Hit when you are targeted if it can legally reach you under the normal Take the Hit rules.

- Once per IC when your Primary Malfunctions, you may pay 10 Chakra. Ignore that Malfunction’s penalties until the start of your next IC; the Malfunction itself remains.

- When your Primary would become Inactive solely for ending outside Frame Range, it remains Active until the end of your next Action. If it is still outside legal Frame Range then, it becomes Inactive.

### Heavy Threads
{:#heavy-threads}

Your Heavy Threads equal your Thread Limit minus 1\. They represent every unused control string being tightened into the Primary instead of spread across additional bodies.

Concentrated Upkeep — Increase Kugutsu no Jutsu’s Upkeep by Heavy Threads/2, rounded up. This is added after determining its normal Upkeep from Active Puppets.

For each Heavy Thread:

- Increase the Primary’s maximum HP by 15% of its Frame-adjusted maximum HP, calculated additively and rounded down after all Heavy Threads are counted. Frame-adjusted maximum HP means after the Frame’s HP multiplier but before Attachments, Heavy Threads or other temporary modifiers.

- The Primary gains \+1 Damage Reduction.

For every 2 Heavy Threads, the Primary also gains \+1 Concentrated Slot. Concentrated Slots may hold Weapon Mounts or Attachments chosen when the Puppet is constructed. Equipment installed in Concentrated Slots functions while Concentrated Control is active. Straining Heavy Threads leaves Concentrated Slots active.

Recalculate Heavy Thread maximum HP whenever Concentrated Control begins or ends. Current HP remains unchanged when the maximum increases. If Concentrated Control ends while the Puppet is above its new maximum HP, reduce its current HP to that maximum.

### Heavy Thread Maneuvers
{:#heavy-thread-maneuvers}

A Heavy Thread can be Strained to force the Puppet beyond ordinary control. A Strained Heavy Thread still counts for maximum HP and Concentrated Slots, but its \+1 Damage Reduction is suppressed and it cannot be Strained again. At the start of your IC, all Strained Heavy Threads refocus.

Overdrive — When declaring a Puppet Attack, Strain 1 Heavy Thread. Reduce that Action’s Final Speed by 2, minimum its Base Speed. This changes its actual Final Speed, including IC advancement and Damage Bonus multiplication.

Brace — Speed 0 Interrupt. After damage to the Primary is determined but before it is applied, Strain 1 Heavy Thread to reduce that damage by 10% of the Primary’s maximum HP, rounded down. Brace can be used once per damage instance.

Break Restraint — Speed 0 Interrupt. When the Primary would suffer Knockback or gain Immobilization, Strain 1 Heavy Thread. Reduce that Knockback by 10 yards, or reduce the Immobilization severity being applied by 2, minimum 0\. For Immobilization, reduce only the severity applied by this trigger.

Auxiliary System — Once per 10 IC, when the Primary would activate a spent Workshop or Refined Attachment, Strain 2 Heavy Threads to use that Attachment as though it were unspent for this activation. It remains spent afterward. Any ammunition, poisons, Exploding Tags, stored consumables or other inventory resources required by the Attachment are consumed normally.

## Support Routines
{:#support-routines}

Each Puppet knows Support Routines according to Puppet Mastery. A Support contributes one Routine at a time. At the start of your IC, choose which known Routine each Support contributes.

A Routine with \[Assault\] functions only in Assault Formation. \[Guard\] functions only in Guard Formation. \[Any\] functions in either.

If a Routine requires a Mount or Attachment, the contributing Support must possess it and remain able to use it.

The same Routine can be contributed by several Supports, but its listed maximum is shared across the whole formation.

### Crossfire — \[Assault\]
{:#crossfire-assault}

**Requires:** a Weapon Mount or Puppet Mechanism with the Attack tag.  
When a damaging Puppet Attack through the Primary fully hits, increase that Attack’s Wounding by \+5% per active Crossfire, maximum \+15%.

### Angle of Attack — \[Assault\]
{:#angle-of-attack-assault}

**Requires:** a Weapon Mount or Puppet Mechanism with the Attack tag.  
If the target of the Primary’s Attack is within that Support’s legal weapon/mechanism Range and the Support has Line of Sight, gain \+1 Accuracy per active Angle of Attack, maximum \+2.

### Screen — \[Guard\]
{:#screen-guard}

**Requires:** Ablative Shell, Anchor Spikes, Deployable Cover, or a Reinforced, Juggernaut or Siege Frame.  
Once per IC when the Primary takes damage, reduce that damage by 5% of its maximum HP per active Screen, maximum 10%, rounded down. Apply this after ordinary percentage reductions.

### Interception — \[Guard\]
{:#interception-guard}

**Requires:** the Support be able to move and reach the protected target under the normal Take the Hit rules.  
Once per 10 IC across the entire formation, when the User or Primary is targeted by an Attack, one Support contributing Interception may attempt Take the Hit using the normal Action and timing rules. If successful, that Support becomes the target and resolves any further defense through itself.

### Mechanic — \[Any\]
{:#mechanic-any}

**Requires:** Tool Hands or Field Workshop.  
Field Refit restores an additional 10% of the target Puppet’s maximum HP per active Mechanic, maximum \+20%.

### Spotter — \[Any\]
{:#spotter-any}

**Requires:** Sensor Mast.  
If at least one Spotter Support can clearly perceive the Primary’s target, reduce penalties to the Primary’s Attack from Cover, smoke, darkness, weather or distance by 2 per active Spotter, maximum 4\.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-14}

### Puppet Mastery
{:#puppet-mastery}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 5  
**Scale:** 2  
**Limit:** 3 \+ total XP/500, maximum 10  
The first 3 ranks granted by Karashi count as purchased when determining the cost of later ranks.

Rank 1 — Workshop Craft

Thread Limit 1\. Unlock Small and Medium Frames, Workshop Weapon Mounts and Workshop Attachments. Each Puppet knows one Support Routine.

Rank 2 — Specialist Frames

Thread Limit 2\. Unlock Recon and Reinforced Frames.

Rank 3 — Three-Body Method

Thread Limit 3\. Unlock Assault Formation, Guard Formation, Concentrated Control and Heavy Thread Maneuvers.

Rank 4 — Fine Craft

Every Puppet gains \+1 Slot.

Rank 5 — Refined Craft

Thread Limit 4\. Unlock Refined Weapon Mounts, Refined Attachments and the Juggernaut Frame. Each Puppet knows two Support Routines. Gain Split Threading and Field Refit.

Rank 6 — Routine Library

Each Puppet knows three Support Routines it qualifies for.

Rank 7 — Thread Weaving

Gain Thread Weaving.

Rank 8 — Perfect Synchronization

Every Puppet gains another \+1 Slot. Gain Perfect Synchronization.

Rank 9 — Master of Strings

Thread Limit 5\. Gain Lead Support and Master of Strings.

Rank 10 — Masterwork Craft

Thread Limit 6\. Unlock Masterwork Weapon Mounts and Attachments, the Siege Frame and Hundred-Hand Performance.

### Collection Limit
{:#collection-limit}

Your Collection Limit is always twice your current Thread Limit.

## Puppet Mastery Techniques
{:#puppet-mastery-techniques}

### Split Threading
{:#split-threading}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 5  
While the number of Active Puppets you control is no more than half your Thread Limit, rounded up, maintaining Kugutsu no Jutsu occupies only one hand instead of both.

### Field Refit
{:#field-refit}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 5  
\[Speed 8 \+ X, Chakra 10 \+ 3X, Utility, Puppet\]

X is the number of previous times you have used Field Refit this scene.

Target one Active Puppet within Frame Range. Choose one:

- restore 25% of its maximum HP, rounded down;

- clear its Malfunction; or

- reset one spent Workshop or Refined Attachment.

Each individual Attachment can be reset by Field Refit only once per scene.

### Thread Weaving
{:#thread-weaving}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 7  
Hot Swap — After resolving a Puppet Action through the Primary, you may promote one Support to Primary in its current position. The previous Primary becomes a Support in its current position.

Thread Snap — When the Primary becomes Disabled, Inactive or unable to act because it is at Immobilization 6+, immediately promote one legal Support to Primary in its current position. This happens after the triggering effect resolves.

### Perfect Synchronization
{:#perfect-synchronization}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 8  
When declaring a Puppet Action that the Primary could legally perform, choose one Support that could also legally perform it from its current position. Increase the Action’s Final Speed by 2 and Chakra cost by 5\. That Support performs the Action instead of the Primary, using its own Frame, position, Mounts, Attachments and installed equipment. It remains a Support after the Action resolves.

### Lead Support
{:#lead-support}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 9  
At the start of your IC, designate one Support as Lead Support. It may contribute two different Support Routines it knows until the start of your next IC. It must meet both Routines’ requirements. Shared maximums still apply.

If Concentrated Control is active, at the start of your IC you may Strain 1 Heavy Thread to have the Primary contribute one Support Routine it knows until the start of your next IC. For this exception, treat that Routine’s \[Assault\] or \[Guard\] Formation requirement as met, but the Primary must still satisfy every Mount, Attachment and positional requirement. Shared maximums still apply.

### Master of Strings
{:#master-of-strings}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 9  
After determining the Final Speed of a Defensive Interrupt made through an Active Puppet, you may spend AP equal to half that Final Speed, rounded up. If you do, that Defensive Interrupt does not advance your IC.

Only one Defensive Interrupt against the same incoming Attack can benefit from Master of Strings.

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-14}

#### D-Rank
{:#d-rank}

### Kugutsu no Jutsu — Puppet Technique
{:#kugutsu-no-jutsu-puppet-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 8  
**Upkeep:** X  
**Speed:** 5  
**Tags:** Utility, Puppet, Self  
**Effects**

This technique requires no handseals. X is the number of Active Puppets you control, minimum 1\.

Choose up to your Thread Limit of deployed Puppets within 5 yards and connect them with Chakra Strings. They become Active. Choose one as Primary; the others become Supports. If you control exactly one Active Puppet and have Puppet Mastery 3+, you may enter Concentrated Control. Otherwise choose Assault or Guard Formation.

Maintaining Kugutsu no Jutsu occupies both hands. Concentrated Control or Split Threading reduces this to one hand when its requirements are met.

You may perform ordinary Actions with your own body or Puppet Actions through the Primary. All Puppet Body, Action Economy, Formation and Support rules in this document apply.

When an Active Puppet leaves Frame Range, it becomes Inactive and Kugutsu no Jutsu’s Upkeep recalculates immediately. While Kugutsu no Jutsu is maintained, using it again reconnects any number of deployed, non-Disabled Puppets within 5 yards up to your Thread Limit; retain existing legal connections and recalculate Upkeep from the new Active total.

You cannot maintain ordinary Clones while Kugutsu no Jutsu is active, and Clones cannot control Puppets. Kugutsu Henge is an exception.

A Puppet acting reveals that Puppet normally. The User remains subject to ordinary Awareness, sensing, line-of-sight and Chakra-thread tracing from their own position.

### Kugutsu Kuchiyose no Jutsu — Puppet Summoning Technique
{:#kugutsu-kuchiyose-no-jutsu-puppet-summoning-technique}

**Type:** Sealing Ninjutsu, Rank D  
**Chakra:** 5 \+ X  
**Speed:** 4 \+ X  
**Seal Speed:** 4  
**Tags:** Utility, Puppet, Sealing, Link  
**Requires:** Puppet Scrolls  
**Effects**

Store or release up to X Puppets from Puppet Scrolls you carry. Each Puppet requires one Scroll. Released Puppets appear in legal spaces within 3 yards of you.

When used as a Link to Kugutsu no Jutsu, add Chakra \+5 \+ X, Speed \+X and Seal Speed \+4 instead of using this technique’s standalone Speed. The released Puppets may immediately be selected as Active Puppets for Kugutsu no Jutsu.

Karashi may learn and use this technique without Seal Training or other general Sealing-Ninjutsu access. It still counts as Sealing Ninjutsu for effects that interact with that tag.

#### C-Rank
{:#c-rank}

### Kugutsu Henge no Jutsu — Puppet Transformation Technique
{:#kugutsu-henge-no-jutsu-puppet-transformation-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 14  
**Upkeep:** 1  
**Speed:** 8  
**Tags:** Utility, Puppet  
**Requires:** Henge no Jutsu, Kugutsu no Jutsu active  
**Effects**

Transform the Primary to appear as the User.

Immediately resolve the normal Hide Among Clones action using the transformed Primary as one Clone, without paying Hide Among Clones’ additional Speed. Make the normal Stealth contest, and thereafter use the normal Observe and random-target rules for being hidden among Clones.

The transformation ends if the Puppet becomes Disabled or Inactive, ceases to be Primary, Kugutsu no Jutsu ends, or it visibly uses equipment or mechanisms that make the disguise impossible.

#### B-Rank
{:#b-rank}

### Puppet Substitution
{:#puppet-substitution}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 20  
**Speed:** 6  
**Tags:** Utility, Puppet, Interrupt  
**Requires:** Kugutsu no Jutsu active  
**Effects**

When the User is targeted by a single-target Attack, swap positions with the Primary. Both positions must be legal and the Primary must be within Frame Range before the swap.

Like Take the Hit, Puppet Substitution is legal if its Final Speed is no greater than the triggering Attack’s Final Speed.

The Primary becomes the target of the triggering Attack and may declare one legal Defensive Interrupt. Pay both Interrupts’ costs. For that Attack, advance the User’s IC once using the higher Final Speed of Puppet Substitution or the Puppet’s Defensive Interrupt.

If the Primary cannot legally occupy the User’s former position, the technique fails and its costs are still paid.

#### A-Rank
{:#a-rank}

### Hundred-Hand Performance
{:#hundred-hand-performance}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank A  
**Chakra:** \+20  
**Speed:** \+2  
**Special:** Once per Scene  
**Tags:** Attack, Puppet  
**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 10, Kugutsu no Jutsu active  
**Effects**

Every string in the formation tightens at once.

Choose a single-target damaging Basic Weapon Attack, Bukijutsu or Puppet Mechanism Attack available through the Primary that normally makes one Attack against one target. Multi-Throw, Dual Wielding, Area Attacks and Actions that already make multiple Attacks cannot be chosen.

Choose one mode:

Swarm — Choose up to one different legal target per Active Puppet and assign one Active Puppet to each target. Each assigned Puppet must be able to perform the chosen Attack from its current position with its own equipment. Resolve one instance of the chosen Attack from each assigned Puppet against its target. Each target is attacked once and defends normally. Resolve all assigned Attacks as one Action with a single IC advancement.

Execution — Choose one target the Primary can legally attack. Make the chosen Attack once. Each Support that could legally attack that target with the chosen Attack from its current position contributes one Execution Thread. If Concentrated Control is active, each Heavy Thread instead contributes one Execution Thread. On a Full Hit, add one of the chosen Attack’s base Damage Dice per Execution Thread, maximum \+4 Damage Dice total, and increase Wounding by \+5% per Execution Thread, maximum \+20%. Execution bonuses apply only on a Full Hit.

Hundred-Hand Performance ignores Support Routines and cannot be Linked with Perfect Synchronization.

## Puppet Construction
{:#puppet-construction}

### Building and Rebuilding Puppets
{:#building-and-rebuilding-puppets}

Between scenes, you may construct, retire, rebuild and reconfigure Puppets up to your Collection Limit without spending a Downtime Slot or paying Ryo for the wooden/metal Frame itself.

Anything installed that is normally purchased, crafted or consumed still follows its normal rules. Weapons, Ballistic Weapons, ammunition, poisons, Exploding Tags, special materials and consumables all come from your inventory.

When you build a Puppet:

1\. Choose a Frame.

2\. Determine its maximum HP and Frame Range.

3\. Install Weapon Mounts and Attachments up to its normal Slots. If the Puppet is intended for Concentrated Control, you may also designate additional Mounts or Attachments for the maximum Concentrated Slots it can currently gain. Those systems remain installed but inactive outside Concentrated Control.

4\. Assign real weapons and consumables to the relevant Mounts.

5\. Choose the Support Routines it knows according to Puppet Mastery.

A Weapon Mount or Attachment may be installed once per Puppet unless it says Repeatable.

### Upgrade
{:#upgrade}

An Attachment marked Upgrade modifies one compatible Mount, Attachment or Frame feature named by its text. Unless stated otherwise, a Mount or Attachment can have only one Upgrade.

### Cosmetic Oddities
{:#cosmetic-oddities}

Each Puppet may have up to three harmless Cosmetic Oddities at 0 Slots: bells, faceplates, tea sets, masks, mascots, voice boxes, tiny shrines and similar flourishes. Cosmetic Oddities provide no numerical benefit.

### Slot Progression
{:#slot-progression}

The listed Slots are Base Slots. Every Puppet gains \+1 Slot at Puppet Mastery 4 and another \+1 Slot at Puppet Mastery 8\. Only these Puppet Mastery ranks increase Base Slots.

Concentrated Slots are temporary Slots granted by Heavy Threads. Equipment assigned to them is chosen when the Puppet is built or reconfigured between scenes and is active only during Concentrated Control. Real weapons installed in Concentrated Slots remain committed to that Puppet while those Slots are inactive.

## Puppet Frames
{:#puppet-frames}

### Base Frame Values
{:#base-frame-values}

Base Puppet HP \= 50 \+ maximum Vitality/3, rounded down.

Base Frame Range \= 10 \+ CHA/2 yards, rounded down.

### Small Frame
{:#small-frame}

**Size:** Small  
Base Slots: 5

**HP:** 75% Base Puppet HP, rounded down  
**Frame Range:** Base \+5 yards  
**Special:** \+2 Dodge, \+2 Stealth  
A compact assassin, courier or skirmisher frame.

### Medium Frame
{:#medium-frame}

**Size:** Medium  
Base Slots: 6

**HP:** 100% Base Puppet HP  
**Frame Range:** Base  
**Special:** \+1 Accuracy with Puppet Attacks  
A balanced general-purpose frame with enough room to specialize.

### Recon Frame
{:#recon-frame}

**Size:** Small  
Base Slots: 5

**HP:** 75% Base Puppet HP, rounded down  
**Frame Range:** Base \+20 yards  
**Special:** \+5 Stealth; Sensor Mast costs 0 Slots on this Frame  
A delicate long-range scout and observation platform.

### Reinforced Frame
{:#reinforced-frame}

**Size:** Medium  
Base Slots: 5

**HP:** 150% Base Puppet HP, rounded down  
**Frame Range:** Base \-5 yards, minimum 5  
**Special:** Damage Reduction equal to CHA/10 \+ Puppet Mastery, rounded down; \+2 to Take the Hit rolls  
A bodyguard frame built to occupy dangerous space and remain there.

### Juggernaut Frame
{:#juggernaut-frame}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 5  
**Size:** Large  
Base Slots: 6

**HP:** 175% Base Puppet HP, rounded down  
**Frame Range:** Base \-10 yards, minimum 5  
**Movement:** \-20%  
**Special:** Damage Reduction equal to CHA/10 \+ Puppet Mastery, rounded down  
A single-body war machine built for Puppeteers who would rather perfect one monster than command six smaller bodies.

Heavy Chassis — While this Puppet is your Primary under Concentrated Control, each Heavy Thread increases its maximum HP by an additional 5% of its Frame-adjusted maximum HP. This is additive with the normal Heavy Thread HP bonus.

Unstoppable — Treat this Puppet’s Immobilization as 2 lower, minimum 0, when determining whether Immobilization prevents movement. All other Immobilization effects use its actual severity.

Massive — This Frame may carry Extreme weapons through Heavy Weapon Mounts. It satisfies Reinforced Frame requirements; Siege Frame requirements still require a Siege Frame.

### Siege Frame
{:#siege-frame}

**Requires:** Puppet Mastery 10  
**Size:** Large  
Base Slots: 8

**HP:** 200% Base Puppet HP, rounded down  
**Remote Frame Range:** 5 yards  
**Special:** Damage Reduction equal to CHA/8 \+ Puppet Mastery, rounded down; \-2 Dodge; \-2 Parry  
A walking room, iron beast or piloted siege body.

Piloted Core — A Siege Puppet can carry one willing Small or Medium creature. Entering or leaving is a Speed 5 Action while adjacent. While the User pilots their own Siege Primary, they share its position and ignore its Remote Frame Range, but cannot use Supports, Formations or handseals.

Ordinary external Melee and Projectile Attacks target the Siege Puppet rather than an occupant. Area effects, Genjutsu, Chakra sensing, inhaled effects and other effects capable of reaching through the Puppet’s protection resolve according to their own rules. If the Siege Puppet becomes Disabled, its occupant is ejected Prone into an adjacent legal space and takes 25% of the damage from the Attack that Disabled it.

## Weapon Mounts
{:#weapon-mounts}

### General Mount Rules
{:#general-mount-rules}

A mounted weapon is an ordinary or crafted weapon the User owns. Installing it makes it unavailable to the User’s body until removed between scenes.

Mounted weapons retain all normal V6 weapon rules and may be used with compatible Taijutsu and Bukijutsu known by the User.

Weapon Mounts count as the limbs/mechanisms needed to wield their installed weapon. A Puppet begins with two functional arms and two functional legs for technique requirements, regardless of cosmetic shape. Additional limbs are cosmetic unless provided by a Mount or Attachment that says otherwise.

### Integrated Weapon Mount
{:#integrated-weapon-mount}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Install one Small or Medium Melee weapon or one Small Ranged weapon you own.

### Projectile Hive
{:#projectile-hive}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Choose one type of Small thrown weapon. Load actual weapons from your inventory. The Puppet may use Multi-Throw with that weapon if the User can normally use Multi-Throw with it.

### Heavy Weapon Mount
{:#heavy-weapon-mount}

**Slots:** 3  
**Grade:** Refined  
Install one weapon that does not qualify for Integrated Weapon Mount, including Large, Extreme, Two-Handed, Exotic or Ballistic weapons. The Frame must be at least Medium. Extreme weapons require Reinforced, Juggernaut or Siege Frame.

### Hundred-Arm Array
{:#hundred-arm-array}

**Slots:** 3  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
Upgrade

Choose an Integrated Weapon Mount carrying a Small or Medium Melee weapon. It may instead carry up to three qualifying one-handed weapons. The Puppet may use Dual Wielding with them if the User possesses Dual Wielding, following all normal Dual Wielding rules.

## Puppet Mechanisms
{:#puppet-mechanisms}

### Mechanism Rule
{:#mechanism-rule}

An Attachment with its own Attack profile is a Puppet Mechanism Attack.

Puppet Mechanism Attacks:

- count as Taijutsu for broad-discipline effects;

- use the User’s normal Taijutsu Accuracy unless stated otherwise;

- use CHA/18 as their Damage Bonus unless fixed damage says otherwise;

- originate from the Puppet using them;

- are not Weapon Attacks unless the Attachment explicitly says so;

- cannot use Bukijutsu unless explicitly stated.

A condition printed directly in a Puppet Mechanism’s Full Hit effect is a Clan effect and applies directly. Make an additional Status roll only when the entry instructs one.

### Adhesive Shot
{:#adhesive-shot}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
**Speed:** 7  
**Chakra:** 12  
**Damage:** 4d6  
**Range:** 15  
**Uses:** 1  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Blunt  
**Effects**

On a Full Hit, apply Immobilization 2, or Immobilization 3 at Puppet Mastery 8+. This Immobilization lasts 10 IC.

### Flame Jet
{:#flame-jet}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
**Speed:** 11  
**Chakra:** 15  
**Damage:** 10d6  
**Area:** Line 2, 15  
**Uses:** 1  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Energy  
**Effects**

Make one Attack against each creature crossed by the Line. On a Full Hit, inflict Ignite equal to 1 \+ Puppet Mastery/5, rounded down.

### Chakra Cannon
{:#chakra-cannon}

**Slots:** 3  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
**Requires:** Siege Frame  
**Speed:** 15  
**Delay:** 5  
**Chakra:** 22  
**Damage:** 8d12  
**Range:** 60  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Energy  
**Effects**

A long-range siege beam. It deals double Final Damage to mundane terrain, walls, structures and unattended objects.

## Workshop Attachments
{:#workshop-attachments}

### Ablative Shell
{:#ablative-shell}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
When this Puppet would take damage, reduce that damage by 15% of its maximum HP, rounded down. Ablative Shell is then spent.

### Anchor Spikes
{:#anchor-spikes}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
As a Speed 0 Interrupt when this Puppet would suffer Knockback, anchor it. Reduce that Knockback to 0\. While anchored, the Puppet cannot voluntarily move or be moved. Retracting the spikes is a Speed 5 Puppet Action.

### Aquatic Propulsion
{:#aquatic-propulsion}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
The Puppet moves and operates underwater without mundane penalties caused solely by swimming or being submerged.

### Disguised Shell
{:#disguised-shell}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Choose an ordinary object or animal of similar Size when the Puppet is built. When the Puppet is behaving consistently with the disguise, use the User’s Espionage against observers’ Awareness to identify it as a Puppet. Obvious combat Actions end the deception.

### Gyroscopic Core
{:#gyroscopic-core}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
The Puppet may stand from Prone as part of its next non-Interrupt Action without increasing that Action’s Speed. It also automatically rights itself after an ordinary uncontrolled fall if physically possible.

### Poison Reservoir
{:#poison-reservoir}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Repeatable

Store one prepared dose of a compatible Contact or Injury Poison you own. When declaring an Attack with a compatible mounted weapon, you may apply one stored dose as part of that Attack. The poison is consumed normally.

### Quick-Release Housing
{:#quick-release-housing}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Choose one carried item or weapon. The Puppet can draw, stow, drop or hand that item to an adjacent creature as part of another Action it takes.

### Recording Lens
{:#recording-lens}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Record and replay mundane images and audio perceived through the Puppet.

### Sample Locker
{:#sample-locker}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Safely store ordinary monster parts, biological samples and similarly hazardous material up to a reasonable amount for the Puppet’s Size.

### Sensor Mast
{:#sensor-mast}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
The User may see and hear from this Puppet’s position while it is Active and make relevant sensory Skill checks from there. Sensory penalties at the Puppet’s location apply normally.

### Tool Hands
{:#tool-hands}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
The Puppet can manipulate ordinary tools and perform Skill checks that require fine hands when the User can perceive and direct the task. It qualifies for the Mechanic Support Routine.

### Tool Launcher
{:#tool-launcher}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Choose a compatible consumable when loading the Puppet. The Puppet may deploy that consumable from its position using the consumable’s normal Action, costs and targeting rules.

### Reinforced Plating
{:#reinforced-plating}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Workshop  
Repeatable, maximum 2

Increase this Puppet’s maximum HP by 10% of its Frame-adjusted maximum per copy, calculated additively.

## Refined Attachments
{:#refined-attachments}

### Binding Cable Launcher
{:#binding-cable-launcher}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
The Puppet may use Grab or other compatible Grapple Taijutsu known by the User against a creature within 5 yards as though in Melee Range. The cable is part of the Grapple and can be targeted or broken when the Grapple rules would allow an escape or severing effect.

### Booster Reel
{:#booster-reel}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Refined  
Once per 10 IC, when this Puppet would end movement just outside Frame Range, pay 5 Chakra to extend its Frame Range by 15 yards until the end of that movement. If it is still outside normal Frame Range afterwards, it becomes Inactive.

### Burrowing Drill
{:#burrowing-drill}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
The Puppet may move through mundane earth, sand and similarly soft ground. It cannot pass through reinforced structures or Chakra-enhanced material and cannot end its movement completely enclosed.

### Chakra Conduit
{:#chakra-conduit}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Refined  
Upgrade

Choose one mounted Melee weapon. It may benefit from the User’s Chakra Flow, weapon-channeling and similar effects when all other requirements are met.

### Deployable Cover
{:#deployable-cover}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
As a Speed 4 Puppet Action, deploy or retract a shield assembly. While deployed, the Puppet counts as solid Cover appropriate to its Size for adjacent creatures and gains \+10% Block reduction, but suffers \-25% movement.

### Explosive Failsafe
{:#explosive-failsafe}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
Load one Exploding Tag you own. When the Puppet becomes Disabled, you may detonate the loaded Tag from the Puppet’s position as Speed 0 after the triggering Attack resolves. The Tag is consumed. The Puppet is affected by the explosion normally if relevant.

### Glider Wings
{:#glider-wings}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
**Requires:** Small or Recon Frame  
The Puppet may move through open air rather than requiring ground contact, but must still use ordinary movement and cannot exceed Frame Range. While at least 1 yard above the ground, it is not affected by Ground-Based effects that require physical contact with the ground. At Immobilization 6+, it descends to the nearest legal surface and cannot take off.

### Harpoon Reel
{:#harpoon-reel}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Refined  
Upgrade

Choose one compatible ranged or thrown Weapon Mount. On a Full Hit, instead of any ordinary Knockback from that Attack, you may pull the target up to 5 yards toward the Puppet. A target too large or anchored to be moved instead pulls the Puppet the same distance toward it. This is forced movement.

### Hidden Weapon Housing
{:#hidden-weapon-housing}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Refined  
Upgrade

Choose one mounted Small or Medium weapon. The weapon begins each scene concealed. On its first Attack against a creature that has not discovered it, make the User’s Espionage opposed by that creature’s Awareness. On success, apply \-2 to that creature’s first Defensive Roll against the Attack.

### Hollow Body
{:#hollow-body}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
Not Small or Recon

The Puppet can enclose one willing, helpless or Grappled creature of a Size it could reasonably contain. Entering or removing a willing creature is Speed 6\. An enclosed creature has solid Cover and cannot Attack, form handseals, control Puppets or interact outside without leaving.

### Rotary Drive
{:#rotary-drive}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
Upgrade

Choose one mounted Melee weapon. Its Attacks gain \+10% Wounding. A Weapon or Armor used to Block those Attacks takes double normal Durability damage from them.

### Weapon Carousel
{:#weapon-carousel}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Refined  
Upgrade

Choose one Weapon Mount that normally carries one weapon. It may contain up to three different weapons that qualify for that Mount. At the start of the User’s IC, choose which one is active. Only the active weapon can be used.

## Masterwork Attachments
{:#masterwork-attachments}

### Field Workshop
{:#field-workshop}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
The Puppet qualifies for the Mechanic Support Routine. Once per scene, when Field Refit targets this Puppet or another Active Puppet within 3 yards of it, reduce Field Refit’s Final Speed by 2, minimum 3\.

### Phased Thread Reel
{:#phased-thread-reel}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
Once per scene when this Puppet would become Inactive solely because it left Frame Range, keep it Active until the end of the User’s next Action. If Concentrated Control already grants this grace period, Phased Thread Reel instead keeps it Active until the end of the User’s following Action. If it has not returned to legal Frame Range by then, it becomes Inactive.

### Sealed Compartment
{:#sealed-compartment}

**Slots:** 1  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
Upgrade

Choose Hollow Body or Piloted Core. Mundane weather, smoke, dust, contaminated air and ordinary inhaled poisons cannot enter the compartment while sealed. Effects that explicitly penetrate solid Cover or do not need air can still affect the occupant.

### Wire Guillotine
{:#wire-guillotine}

**Slots:** 2  
**Grade:** Masterwork  
Upgrade

Choose one mounted thrown weapon used with Ninja Wire. Once per 10 IC after an Attack with that weapon misses or is fully Dodged, redirect it toward a second legal target within 10 yards of the first. Make a new Attack at \-3 Accuracy and 50% Final Damage. The redirected Attack cannot Wound and cannot trigger Wire Guillotine again.

# Bakuton
{:#bakuton}

***RARE KEKKEI GENKAI***

*Bakuton inheritors are drilled hard by Ember Corps breaching and demolition teams because their chakra turns directly into abrupt expansion and pressure. Survivors remember the flash and call it fire; trained operators know the difference. Bakuton exists in the instant between contact and consequence, and mastery is deciding where that consequence is allowed to go.*

**Starting Features**

* Bakuton users begin with Elemental Aptitude for Doton and Raiton and gain Elemental Affinity: Bakuton. Bakuton does not include Doton or Raiton in its Affinity and ordinary Doton or Raiton techniques cannot be converted into Bakuton unless a feature explicitly allows it. Damaging Bakuton techniques inflict Energy Wounds unless stated otherwise.

## Passives
{:#passives-18}

### Blast
{:#blast}

Many Bakuton techniques possess Blast X. When an Attack with Blast deals damage, after damage resolves choose one:

Force: The target suffers Knockback X directly away from the explosion's point of origin.

Recoil: If the explosion occurred within 1 yard of you, move yourself X yards directly away from its point of origin. If you choose Recoil, that target does not suffer the technique's Blast Knockback.

Recoil is forced movement rather than a Move Action. Partial Defense reduces Blast Knockback normally. A single Attack may only apply one instance of Blast to a creature.

### Blast Step
{:#blast-step}

After resolving one of your Bakuton techniques, you can voluntarily take Recoil 3 to move up to 6 yards in any direction without increasing your IC. This movement can carry you vertically and ignores ordinary difficult terrain.

### Detonation Marks
{:#detonation-marks}

When you hit a creature or object with a melee Bakuton Attack, you can place a Detonation Mark on it, maximum 4 per target and 8 total. Marks last for the scene. As a Speed 0 Action, detonate any number of your Marks within CHA/2 yards. Each Mark costs 5 Chakra and deals 4d10 Energy damage plus Knockback 3 to its host. Multiple Marks on the same target combine their damage into one hit for Wounding. A target may spend Speed 8 and win Chakra Control or Medicine against your Chakra Control to remove one visible Mark.

When a creature carrying at least one Detonation Mark begins a Move Action, movement technique, or forced movement, you can spend 2 AP to detonate one Mark before that movement finishes. The creature chooses one:

Brace — the Mark deals its normal damage but causes no Knockback.

Ride the Blast — the Mark deals 50% final damage; after damage, you choouse the direction of Knockback 6\. This replaces up to the first 6 yards of the triggering movement, and any unused movement continues from the new position.

This detonation cannot combine multiple Marks for Wounding and cannot trigger again from its own Knockback.

### Explosive Body
{:#explosive-body}

You gain 3 ranks of Blast Conditioning at no XP cost. You are only ever subject to the damage of a Bakuton technique if you are inside the area of the Jutsu itself. Casting a technique that you are not inside produces no negative effects for yourself. You are still subject to the Recoil of the action used.

Controlled Demolition is part of Explosive Body rather than a separate purchase. Whenever you use a Bakuton technique that can be modified this way, choose one mode: Standard Detonation resolves it normally; Concussive Detonation reduces Final Damage by 20% and doubles the technique's Blast without increasing other Knockback; Propulsive Detonation reduces Final Damage by 20% and, when the point of origin is within 3 yards, allows Recoil and doubles its Recoil distance; Shaped Charge removes the Attack's Area and Blast so it can affect only its original target, and increases Final Damage by 50% against objects, terrain, and created barriers. Shaped Charge does not gain this bonus against living creatures, Puppets, worn equipment, Armor, Summons, or Clones.

### Explosive Clay Branch
{:#explosive-clay-branch}

At 1,500 total XP, a Bakuton character may unlock the Explosive Clay branch by paying a Talent. Existing Clay techniques keep their normal XP costs. Clay creations are considered Bakuton for Detonation Marks and Blast Step.

## Abilities

### Blast Conditioning
{:#blast-conditioning}

**Type:** Ability  
**Base Cost:** 5  
**Scale:** 5  
**Limit:** RES/20

**Effects**

Per rank, reduce Knockback from your own Bakuton by 1 yards.

Per rank, reduce Damage from your own Bakuton by 5%.

Gain \+1 per rank to Resistance rolls caused by explosions, explosive environments, or Bakuton.

Even at 5 ranks you are not immune to your own Bakuton unless a technique explicitly says otherwise.

### Kinjutsu: Kibaku Nendo - Explosive Clay
{:#kinjutsu-kibaku-nendo-explosive-clay}

Most Bakuton inherit the ability to turn chakra into an explosive event. A vanishingly small number learn to give that event a body. Clay crawls, flies, waits, and turns the battlefield into a gallery where every sculpture might be the last thing somebody sees.

**Type:** Talent  
**Requires:** Bakuton Affinity, Doton Aptitude, Controlled Demolition, Chakra Control 10  
You gain access to techniques with the Clay tag.

Clay counts as both Bakuton and Doton when elemental strengths, weaknesses, negation, or suppression are relevant.

Clay constructs never gain independent Actions, AP, or Initiative Counts unless a technique explicitly says otherwise.

Movement, attacks, and detonations performed by Clay constructs use the Action described by their technique.

Clay techniques do not benefit from effects requiring Bakuton Melee techniques. Controlled Demolition may still modify their explosions unless stated otherwise.

A standard prepared clay pouch supplies a battle. Without clay you cannot perform Clay techniques. Destroyed clay does not chain-detonate.

# Kaguya
{:#kaguya}

***CANON CLAN***

*Kaguya descendants in Haven Ember inherit Shikotsumyaku without inheriting the old expectation that they exist only to be thrown into battle. Their skeletons become armour, ammunition, and weapons; Ember Corps training gives that frightening physiology discipline, squad doctrine, and somewhere to aim the instinct to keep escalating.*

**Starting Features**

* Kaguya may start with Elemental Affinity for Clan/General Ninjutsu.

## Passives
{:#passives-22}

### Battle-Born Skeleton
{:#battle-born-skeleton}

A Kaguya begins every combat with 3 Skeletal Modifications, plus 1 additional Modification per full 1,000 total XP, maximum 6 starting Modifications. Choose them when Initiative is rolled. The normal rule granting a Modification whenever you use a Kaguya Clan Jutsu remains in effect, and suffering a Wound grants another Modification as described under Skeletal Modifications.

Corpse Bone Chain. You are treated as having 3 ranks of Corpse Bone Chain at no XP cost. Additional ranks cost 3 XP each instead of 5, to the normal limit.

Marrowlust. The first time an Attack applies a Wound to a target, immediately acquire Skeletal Modifications equal to the Wound's Severity (Minor 1, Major 2, and so on). It can trigger once per target damaged by an Attack; a single multi-hit Attack cannot trigger it repeatedly on the same target. Wounds caused by Kaguya Clan Jutsu grant one additional Skeletal Modification beyond this normal amount.

### Bonefield Mastery
{:#bonefield-mastery}

While inside your Seedling Fern you can move to any point in the area as a Speed 2 Action. As Speed \+4 on a non-Interrupt melee Kaguya attack, emerge from another bone and make that attack a Surprise Attack. If a visible creature attempts to leave the area, you can spend 4 AP to move adjacent to it and make one Speed 8 Basic Bone Weapon or Unarmed Attack. On hit, cancel the remaining movement and knock it RES/10 yards toward the centre.

### Dance Mastery
{:#dance-mastery}

Whenever a Kaguya technique expends 3 or more Skeletal Modifications at once, after it resolves regain one Modification of a different type from those expended. Whenever it expends 6 or more, regain two instead. This cannot return the same Modifications paid into Corpse Bone Chain.

### Dead Bone Renewal
{:#dead-bone-renewal}

At 3,000 total XP, whenever Skeletal Realignment removes a Major or Severe Wound, recover Vitality equal to RES times the Wound's former severity. Once per Full Rest, you can spend 3 Modifications from one category to downgrade a Critical Blunt, Piercing, or Slashing Wound to Severe before using Realignment normally. This cannot restore death, a destroyed brain, or an effect explicitly stated to be beyond regeneration.

### Indestructible Skeleton
{:#indestructible-skeleton}

Gain DR equal to RES/5 against Blunt, Piercing, and Slashing damage. When one of those damage types would inflict a Wound, you can expend two Ossification Modifications to reduce the Wound by one severity, potentially negating a Minor Wound. This occurs after all other Wound modifiers.

### Skeletal Modifications
{:#skeletal-modifications}

As the Kaguya fight, their skeletal structure becomes increasingly better-adapted for combat. Every time you use one of your Clan Jutsu, you gain one of the following Skeletal Modifications. You can apply them repeatedly, their Effects stacking additively.

The primary purpose of Skeletal Modifications, besides the passive benefits having them provides is their ability to enhance the Kaguya’s Clan Jutsu by “Expending” those Skeletal Modifications, losing them permanently in return for a powerful boost to the Jutsu. In such cases when Skeletal Modifications are Expended, you still retain their passive benefits for that IC and for the Jutsu you Expended them on.

Whenever you suffer a Wound, your skeleton reacts violently to the trauma: gain one Skeletal Modification of your choice after the Wound resolves. Wounds never remove existing Skeletal Modifications unless a technique explicitly says they do.

After you take Blunt, Piercing, or Slashing damage from a hostile Attack, you may convert one existing Skeletal Modification into a different type without using Corpse Bone Chain. If that Attack inflicts a Wound, you may convert up to two instead. Converted Modifications are not treated as newly acquired and cannot trigger Corpse Bone Chain. The same attacker cannot trigger this adaptation twice in a row with the same damage type; it must change damage type or another creature must hit you first.

If you wish, you can suppress activation of these modifications. Suppressing any or all of your skeletal modifications is a Speed 0 action. Regardless, once combat has ended these effects all fade within an hour; bones retract into your body, and so forth.

Ossification

You extend your bones to cover your vital regions, forming broad, flexible plates. This gives you a damage reduction of (RES)/5, and can be applied up to 5 times.

Blades

Numerous spikes and blades of hardened bone protrude from your body in combat-useful ways, such as from your knees, extending past your hands, and so forth. This increases your damage bonus by 0.25 for Unarmed Taijutsu and weapons created by Dance of the Camellia, or 0.5 for Clan Jutsu (including Dance of the Clematis' weapon), each time it is applied. Blades has no combat limit, though a single attack can benefit from at most 10 ranks.

Spurs

Small shards and lengthier spikes of bones gradually cover your body, protruding from beneath your skin and injuring anyone foolish enough to strike you. Anyone who attacks you in melee range, including with any weapons of less than Large size, takes (your RES)/5 damage if they hit. This damage is also dealt any time they use a Grapple Jutsu against you. You can apply this up to 5 times.

Osteogenesis

The more a Kaguya fights, the easier it becomes for them to do so. Every application of this modification reduces the Chakra costs of your clan jutsu by 2\. You can apply this up to 5 times.

Skeletal Realignment

Your skeleton can rebuild itself around trauma. When you gain a Skeletal Modification from suffering a Blunt, Piercing, or Slashing Wound, you can immediately attempt Realignment. Spend existing Modifications from one single category equal to the Wound's severity: Minor 1, Major 2, Severe 3, Critical 4\. Remove that Wound after it resolves. The Modification gained from being Wounded may count toward this cost.

Critical Wounds may be removed this way only once per Full Rest. Realignment cannot repair a destroyed brain, restore death, remove a Gate Wound while its Gate remains open, or negate an effect explicitly stated to be beyond regeneration.

### Corpse Bone Chain
{:#corpse-bone-chain}

**Type:** Ability  
**Cost:** 5  
**Scale:** 0  
**Limit:** Max Willpower

**Effects**

Whenever you acquire a Skeletal Modification, from any source, you can increase your Stamina penalty by 2 and gain any other Skeletal Modification (which does not have to be one that technique could normally provide).

This may be used a number of times per battle equal to your ranks in this ability.

### Tessenka no Mai - Dance of the Clematis
{:#tessenka-no-mai-dance-of-the-clematis}

There are three weapons that can be created with this technique. Each requires a separate use of this Technique and a minimum of 5 Skeletal Modifications Expended on this Technique:

Ultimate Spear: This creates a Large, One-Handed/Two-Handed, Piercing, Polearm Weapon

Ultimate Whip: This creates a Medium, One-Handed, Slashing, Chain Weapon with a Range of 15 instead of 5 and an additional \+1 Accuracy.

Ultimate Shield: This creates a Medium, One-Handed, Blunt, Club Weapon. It can be used as a Speed 8 Interrupt to Block an attack (this speed is not modified by Skeletal Modifications). The weapon breaks if you block an attack that deals 35% of your Maximum Vitality (before reductions) while holding it. This Shield blocks 75% of Damage rather than the normal 50%, and does not get reduced when Blocking Weapons or Fuuton (Or Para-Elements based on Fuuton).

Attacking with any of these weapons is a Stamina 20, Speed 16, Delay 5 Action. It deals 1d12 damage per Skeletal Modification you have Expended on it, and you can take the Weapon Focus ability for this weapon applying its benefit to all three versions. For each rank in certain Skeletal Modifications that had been expended on it, its effects are modified as follows:

Ossification: \-1 Base Speed

Blades: Damage is considered 5% higher for determining wounds

Spurs: \+½ Accuracy Bonus (Rounded Up)

Osteogenesis: \-2 Stamina cost

Skeletal Modifications: Any two.

### Yanagi no Mai - Dance of the Willow
{:#yanagi-no-mai-dance-of-the-willow}

**Effects**

While maintaining Dance of the Willow's Upkeep, you can use a Speed 0 Action to spend 4 AP to attack someone within 5 yards of you with a “Spinal Thrust” Attack. By raising the cost to 6 AP you improve this Attack to Target everyone within 5 yards of you (Though it does not count as an AoE Attack). Spinal Thrust is always considered to be a surprise attack.

Spinal Thrust only deals (Speed \* PDB) Damage and is treated as a Speed 10 Attack, though it does not allow you to move as part of the attack; you do not delay your next action because of this, but that Speed is used to determine viable defenses and damage bonus. You can spend additional AP to lower the effective Speed further. Parrying Dance of the Willow reduces its damage by half and prevents it from inflicting wounds, rather than defending against it fully.

You acquire a Skeletal Modification every time you spend AP to activate Dance of the Willow’s Spinal Thrust Attack, but not when you first use the jutsu and pay its chakra cost.

Skeletal Modifications: Ossification, Blades, Spurs
