### Sawarabi no Mai - Dance of the Seedling Fern
{:#sawarabi-no-mai-dance-of-the-seedling-fern}

**Type:** Taijutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 30  
**Damage:** 3d12 per Skeletal Modification Expended  
**Area:** Physical damage bonus \*5 yards  
**Speed:** 20  
**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Piercing

**Effects**

This requires Expending a minimum of 5 Skeletal Modifications, yet has no maximum. Whilst inside the Seedling Fern Area, and by expending one Skeletal Modification of a given type as a Speed 4 Action, you can use one of these following Effects:

* Spurs: You can instantly move anywhere in the Area of Seedling Fern.

* Blades: You immediately perform the Spinal Thrust Attack given by Dance of the Willow against everybody within the Seedling Fern Area , this ignores the "Once per 10 ICs" rule but does not give you a Skeletal Modification.

* Ossification: By melding into and out of the bones ribs jutting out of the ground, you can remove any external Immobilization penalty (excluding those due to poison, genjutsu, etc.)

* Osteogenesis: You can Hide, regardless of it's normally be possible or not. When you hide, you begin at Stealth 2\.

When a creature attempts to leave the bone field, you can convert your next non-Interrupt Action into a Melee Attack with a final Speed of 10 or less and resolve it as an Interrupt against that creature. Pay all normal costs. You can move only as far as necessary to bring the target into that Attack's legal Melee Range. If the creature is Hidden, make one Search roll before declaring the Attack; on failure, the interception is not spent. After the Attack resolves, set your next IC no earlier than the current IC plus that Attack's final Speed. On a full hit, the creature's movement ends and it is moved half the distance back toward the centre of the bone field. This is your next Action, not a free additional attack.

This is an Environmental, AoE effect. When used, this effectively destroys any other Environmental effects that require contact with the ground within the area (Swamp of the Underworld would cease to apply within the area, but a Hidden Mist would not). Inside the field, the Kaguya has a \+5 bonus to Awareness. If this technique is used again when already inside its radius, the two fields overlap and combine; nobody already in the field will be damaged again; and the cost is halved, after applying Osteogenesis' reductions.

Even if someone dodges Sawarabi no Mai, they do not escape its area; they simply avoid being skewered by spikes of bone erupting from the ground.

After a few hours the bones will grow brittle as the chakra dissipates and begins crumbling. Within a day there will be nothing left but dust and bone chips.

Skeletal Modifications: Any three.

# Uchiha
{:#uchiha}

***CANON CLAN — 3 UP***

*In Haven Ember, the Uchiha are heirs to a dangerous legacy rather than residents of a sovereign clan compound. Their strength is breadth under pressure: the Sharingan turns observation into prediction, prediction into imitation, and imitation into a fighting style that becomes harder to solve the longer the duel continues.*

**Starting Features**

* Uchiha begin with Elemental Aptitude for Katon and Elemental Affinity for Katon or Genjutsu.

## Passives
{:#passives-8}

### The Curse of Hatred
{:#the-curse-of-hatred}

The Uchiha Clan have always had a strong connection to others. You are no different than the rest. Your emotions often overwhelm you, and you find yourself in places where you feel like the entire world is against you \- even when everything around you says otherwise. Whenever an ally within your line of sight suffers a Wound, you may designate the enemy responsible as the object of your hatred. Until that enemy is defeated, you gain \+2 Accuracy against them and your attacks against them deal \+10% Final Damage. However, you suffer \-2 Dodge against attacks from all other enemies. If the wounded ally is someone with whom you have an established in-character bond, your first attack against the responsible enemy also gains \-2 Final Speed, but counts as it’s base speed for the purposes of dealing damage.

You may only have one object of hatred at a time.

If you are the one that lands the final blow on your Object of Hatred, you begin an emotional surge. Until the end of the scene, your character begins to resent the ally that was wounded. Your mind betrays you, twisting your affection into resentment, your concern into blame, and your relief into an overwhelming sense of emptiness. 

When the scene ends, choose one:

* **Repression:** You bury your emotions and pretend nothing happened. Your next Emotional Surge lasts until the end of the following scene instead.  
* **Confrontation:** You openly blame the ally whose injury triggered your hatred. If you roleplay a meaningful confrontation with them, you gain \+20% XP from it..  
* **Acceptance:** You acknowledge that your hatred has become misplaced. You immediately end your Emotional Surge, but cannot benefit from the Curse of Hatred again until you spend 1 Downtime slot recharging it.

### Sharingan - Copy Wheel Eye
{:#sharingan-copy-wheel-eye}

The Sharingan manifests as red irises marked by tomoe. It is a power one must unlock through suffering and pain. 

Activating Sharingan is Speed 0, may be linked to another Action or Interrupt, and has Upkeep 2\. None of its abilities function while the Uchiha suffers a Visibility penalty of \-6 or worse or otherwise cannot clearly perceive the relevant creature.

The mature Sharingan has three stages and stops at three tomoe in this volume.

### Sharingan, Stage 1
{:#sharingan-stage-1}

 You gain Dousatsugan and Saimingan at no XP cost and ignore their normal rank or skill requirements.

### Sharingan, Stage 2
{:#sharingan-stage-2}

You gain Yogengan and Shouningan at no XP cost. While Sharingan is active, gain \+1 to all Skill rolls.

### Sharingan, Stage 3
{:#sharingan-stage-3}

You gain Magen: Kyouten Chiten and Senchigan at no XP cost. While Sharingan is active, the Stage 2 Skill bonus increases to \+2.

At Stage 3, Genjutsu used through Saimingan and attacks made with a technique currently retained through Dousatsugan gain \+1 Accuracy.

### Dousatsugan - Eye of Insight
{:#dousatsugan-eye-of-insight}

**Requires:** Stage 1 Sharingan  
You can see chakra flow and read the physical construction of techniques. Gain \+4 to defense rolls against Genjutsu.

Whenever you clearly witness a valid Taijutsu technique, or a Ninjutsu or Genjutsu that uses handseals, you can copy it. You must still satisfy elemental Aptitudes, special access, and physical requirements; Clan Jutsu, custom Jutsu, Master Techniques, bloodline-exclusive techniques, and effects that rely on physiology you do not possess cannot be copied.

You can copy techniques one rank above what you could normally learn. A witnessed valid technique may be used for the remainder of the in-character day without paying XP. In addition, you can retain two copied techniques per Sharingan Stage indefinitely without paying XP, maximum six at Stage 3\. Replacing a retained copy requires witnessing a valid replacement.

If you later purchase a retained copied technique permanently, reduce its XP cost by 25%, rounded up. Copying never removes the normal Action, Chakra, Stamina, Seal Speed, equipment, or positioning requirements of the technique.

### Reading the Fight
{:#reading-the-fight}

While Sharingan is active, whenever a hostile creature you can clearly see completes an Attack, defensive action, handseal sequence, or movement technique, gain 1 Read on that creature, maximum equal to your Sharingan Stage. A creature can grant only 1 Read per 10 IC.

Before rolling against that creature, spend Reads one-for-one for \+1 Accuracy or \+1 to a defensive roll, maximum \+3 on a single roll. After a creature with 2 or more Reads rolls a defense against you, you can spend 2 Reads to impose \-2 on that defense after seeing the roll but before the outcome is declared.

All Reads on a creature are lost if you cannot perceive it for 20 consecutive IC.

At Stage 2 or higher, once per creature per 10 IC, when a creature with at least 2 Reads declares the same named Action, defensive action, movement technique, or Jutsu that you have already watched it complete this battle, you can spend 2 Reads before costs are paid. It chooses one:

Break Pattern — cancel that declaration and declare a different legal Action whose final Speed is no lower than the original. It pays only the new Action's costs.

Be Read — keep the declaration. Before it resolves, you can either move up to 2 yards without increasing your IC or reduce the final Speed of one legal defensive Interrupt you use against that Action by 2\.

If it cannot legally change Actions, it must Be Read. This cannot trigger from basic movement that is not an Action.

### Saimingan - Eye of Hypnotism
{:#saimingan-eye-of-hypnotism}

**Requires:** Stage 1 Sharingan  
You can perform single-target Genjutsu without handseals through direct eye contact. The target may avoid eye contact, but doing so imposes the normal Visibility penalties for deliberately refusing to look at you.

At Stage 2, Genjutsu cast this way reduce their final Speed by 4\. At Stage 3, reduce it by 8 instead and gain \+1 Genjutsu Accuracy. Saimingan cannot convert an Area Genjutsu into an eye-contact attack.

### Shouningan - Eye of Recognition
{:#shouningan-eye-of-recognition}

**Requires:** Stage 2 Sharingan  
You can read lips when they are clearly visible, reproduce handwriting after watching a short sample, speed-read, and retain visual detail with near-photographic accuracy. Gain \+4 to Awareness rolls that materially rely on sight; this stacks with the Stage 2 or Stage 3 Sharingan Skill bonus.

Once per scene after watching a creature perform a mundane physical task, you can use your own relevant Skill in place of an untrained Skill roll to reproduce the procedure, though you do not gain tools, passwords, hidden information, or supernatural access you did not observe.

### Yogengan - Eye of Prediction
{:#yogengan-eye-of-prediction}

**Requires:** Stage 2 Sharingan  
Once per Fatigue category, when you make a defensive roll against a visible attack, you can treat the d20 as a 10 rather than rolling. At Stage 3, treat it as a 12\.

You can also take Observe as a Variable Speed Action similar to Guard/Total Defence/Block. Until Observe ends, when a creature you can see attacks you, you can use Yogengan without consuming its once-per-Fatigue-category use.

### Senchigan - Eye of Foresight
{:#senchigan-eye-of-foresight}

**Requires:** Stage 3 Sharingan  
When declaring an Action against a creature you can clearly see, name a technique category and a final Speed. The target declares and resolves any legal defense against that declaration. After seeing the defense result, choose any technique you know or currently retain of that category whose final Speed is no greater than the Speed you declared.

Resolve the chosen technique using its normal damage calculation, but the declared Speed determines interruption legality and when you next act. Increase the chosen technique's Chakra or Stamina cost by 4\. If your chosen technique invalidates the defense the target committed to, the defense still pays its normal costs but does not protect it.

### Three-Tomoe Mastery
{:#three-tomoe-mastery}

At 3,000 total XP your mature Sharingan becomes more efficient.

* Your maximum Reads on a creature increases by 2

* Senchigan increases the chosen technique's Chakra or Stamina cost by 2 instead of 4\.

* Once per 10 IC, when a creature reaches 4 Reads, mark it Mapped until you lose all Reads on it. Your first Attack and first defensive roll against a Mapped creature each 10 IC gain \+2.

* Retained copied techniques used against the creature you copied them from cost 3 less Chakra or Stamina, minimum 1\.

* When Magen: Kyouten Chiten successfully reflects Genjutsu from a Mapped creature, you can spend all 4 Reads to reduce the reflected technique's Speed increase by 4, minimum Speed \+0.

### Uchiha Fire Doctrine
{:#uchiha-fire-doctrine}

Katon Jutsu reduce their Seal Speed and Chakra cost by 2 per Sharingan Stage, minimum Chakra 1\. Damaging Katon Jutsu gain \+1 Accuracy at Stage 2 and \+2 Accuracy at Stage 3\.

At Stage 3, a Katon Jutsu that inflicts a Wound increases any Ignite or Burns it applies by 2\. If it does not normally apply Ignite or Burns, it instead applies Ignite 2 on a full hit.

### Uchiha Shuriken Doctrine
{:#uchiha-shuriken-doctrine}

While Sharingan is active, reduce penalties applied to a Multi-throw to a maximum reduction of 3\. After a visible enemy successfully Dodges one of your Projectile Weapon attacks, gain 1 Read on that enemy and retrieve one thrown weapon that could plausibly be recovered by wire or ricochet as Speed 0\.

## Abilities
{:#abilities-7}

*No Heritage-specific purchasable abilities.*

## Jutsu
{:#jutsu-7}

**C-Rank**

### Katon: Hōsenka Tsumabeni - Phoenix Flower Crimson Nail
{:#katon-hosenka-tsumabeni-phoenix-flower-crimson-nail}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank C  
**Chakra:** 22+X\*2  
**Damage:** 6d10\!o\>9  
**Range:** 25  
**Area:** Up to 4 targets within 8 yards of one another  
**Speed:** 14+X  
**Seal Speed:** 10  
**Special:** Ignite 3  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Katon, Weapon, Link  
**Requires:** Sharingan active  
**Note:** This attack does not use a static Accuracy, and instead makes attack rolls.

**Effects**

Launch a spread of fireballs concealing shuriken or senbon, this technique can be added as part of a Multithrow. First, make an attack roll (D20+Acc not including Base 10\) with this technique, where X is the number of weapons thrown within the Multithrow, on a partial success or full success, the target must then defend against the Multithrow, using it’s normal speed and Accuracy, on a partial success against this jutsu, it deals it’s normal damage following the Partial Damage rules. If this attack hits, it deals it’s full damage, and then includes the damage from the Multithrow. Any weapons used as part of this link are destroyed.

### Uchiha Ryū: Sōfūshasan no Tachi - Manipulated Windmill Triple Blade
{:#uchiha-ryu-sofushasan-no-tachi-manipulated-windmill-triple-blade}

**Type: Bukijutsu**, Rank C  
**Stamina:** 16  
**Damage:** As Weapon  
**Range:** Weapon Range \+ 10  
**Speed:** 12  
**Tags:** Attack, Projectile, Weapon, Control  
**Requires:** Sharingan active

**Effects**

Throw up to three wired or chained projectile weapons and resolve them as one Attack. Gain \+1 Accuracy per Read on the target spent when declaring the technique, maximum \+3. On a full hit, choose one: apply Immobilization 3 for 20 IC; move the target up to 4 yards toward a legal point along the wires; or disarm one held weapon if its wielder fails an opposed STR or DEX check against your DEX.

If the attack misses but is a partial, the weapons remain positioned around the target until your next Action. Your next Projectile Weapon attack against that target gains \+2 Accuracy.

**B-Rank**

### Katon: Gōryūka no Jutsu - Great Dragon Fire Technique
{:#katon-goryuka-no-jutsu-great-dragon-fire-technique}

**Type:** Ninjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 34  
**Damage:** 11d12  
**Range:** 35  
**Area:** Line 5, 25  
**Speed:** 18  
**Seal Speed:** 16  
**Special:** Ignite 5

**Tags:** Attack, AoE, Projectile, Energy, Katon

**Effects**

Launch a dragon-shaped mass of flame down the line. If you spend 2 Reads on one creature in the Area, bend the line once by up to 90 degrees at a point you can see; that creature also suffers \-2 Dodge against the attack. Terrain still blocks the flame normally.

If the attack creates a Wound, leave a burning path across its Area for 20 IC. A creature crossing 3 or more yards of the path takes NDB x 3 Energy damage once per Action.

### Magen: Kageutsushi - Demonic Illusion: False Opening
{:#magen-kageutsushi-demonic-illusion-false-opening}

**Type:** Genjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 28  
**Range:** Eye Contact  
**Speed:** 10  
**Tags:** Attack, Genjutsu, Control  
**Requires:** Stage 2 Sharingan

**Effects**

On a successful Genjutsu attack, the target sees a deliberate opening where none exists. Until the end of its next Action, it suffers \-2 to defenses against you and cannot gain a bonus from having observed, Prepared against, or Read your Action. If the target attacks you before the effect ends, gain 2 Reads on it after that attack resolves.

At Stage 3, you can spend 2 Reads on the target when declaring this technique to increase the defense penalty to \-4.

### Sharingan: Counter-Read
{:#sharingan-counter-read}

**Type:** Dōjutsu Technique, Rank B  
**Chakra:** 18  
**Speed:** 5  
**Tags:** Interrupt, Defense, Counter  
**Requires:** Stage 3 Sharingan

**Effects**

When a creature with at least 2 Reads targets you with a single-target Attack, spend 2 Reads and make a legal Dodge or Parry. Gain \+2 to that defense. On success, after the attack resolves you can immediately take one of the following without increasing your IC: move up to 4 yards; make a Basic Weapon or Unarmed Attack at \-2 Accuracy; or use a retained copied technique with final Speed 6 or less at 75% Final Damage. Pay all normal costs of the chosen option.

### Magen: Kyouten Chiten - Demonic Illusion: Mirror Heaven and Earth Change
{:#magen-kyouten-chiten-demonic-illusion-mirror-heaven-and-earth-change}

**Type:** Genjutsu, Rank B  
**Chakra:** As reflected technique  
**Speed:** 6 \+ X  
**Tags:** Interrupt, Genjutsu  
**Requires:** Stage 3 Sharingan

**Effects**

Immediately after you successfully defend against a Genjutsu targeting you, you can reflect it through Eye of Hypnotism at its original user. X is half the reflected technique's final Speed, rounded up. Use the result of your successful Genjutsu Defense as the reflected technique's Accuracy and pay its normal Chakra cost. You must know or currently retain the reflected technique through Dousatsugan.
